 136  law word_filter:
 137    for left: Array<U32>
 138    for right: Array<U32>
 139    for +left_size: U32
 140    for +right_size: U32
 141    for +threshold: U32
 142    Bool
 144  def word_filter(left, right, left_size, right_size, threshold):
 145    +required_matches = {U32.div((U32.add(U32.mul(threshold, C.minimum(left_size, right_size)), 9999)), 10000) : U32}
 146    interruptions = U32.sub(U32.add(left_size, right_size), U32.mul(required_matches, 2))
 147    +required_words = {C.subtract(required_matches, U32.mul(2, (U32.add(interruptions, 1))))
 148      : U32}
 149    C.branch(Bool, U32.is_eq(required_words, 0), \{
 150      True: (
 151        True{}
 152      )
 153      False: (
 154        U32.is_ge(C.words_common(256n, 0, left, right, 0), required_words)
 155      )
 156    })
 158  law filtered_alignment:
 159    for left: P.Sequence
 160    for right: P.Sequence
 161    for left_words: Array<U32>
 162    for right_words: Array<U32>
 163    for left_sketch: Array<U32>
 164    for right_sketch: Array<U32>
 165    for +left_size: U32
 166    for +right_size: U32
 167    for +threshold: U32
 168    for fast: Bool
 169    U32
 171  def filtered_alignment(left, right, left_words, right_words, left_sketch,
 172                         right_sketch, left_size, right_size, threshold, fast):
 173    C.branch(U32, word_filter(left_words, right_words,
 174      left_size, right_size, threshold), \{
 175      True: (
 176        C.branch(U32, fast, \{
 177          True: (
 178            C.branch(U32, U32.is_lt(C.minimum(left_size, right_size), 15), \{
 179              True: C.alignment_identity_threshold(left, right, left_size,
 180                right_size, True{}, threshold)
 181              False: C.fast_sketch_score(left_sketch, right_sketch, threshold)
 182            })
 183          )
 184          False: C.alignment_identity_threshold(left, right, left_size,
 185            right_size, True{}, threshold)
 186        })
 187      )
 188      False: (
 189        0
 190      )
 191    })
 193  law filtered_both:
 194    for left: P.Sequence
 195    for right: P.Sequence
 196    for left_words: Array<U32>
 197    for right_words: Array<U32>
 198    for left_sketch: Array<U32>
 199    for right_sketch: Array<U32>
 200    for +left_size: U32
 201    for +right_size: U32
 202    for +threshold: U32
 203    for +fast: Bool
 204    U32
 206  def filtered_both(left, right, left_words, right_words, left_sketch,
 207                    right_sketch, left_size, right_size, threshold, fast):
 208    C.apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 209      \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
 210        C.apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 211          \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
 212            C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, U32,
 213              \{Tuple: lw1 => lw2 => (
 214                C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, U32,
 215                  \{Tuple: lk1 => lk2 => (
 216                    reversed = C.sequence_reverse(r2, right_size)
 217                    C.apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 218                      \{Tuple: reverse_for_words => reverse_remaining => (
 219                        C.apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 220                          \{Tuple: reverse_for_sketch => reverse_for_alignment => (
 221                            reverse_words = P.signature(reverse_for_words,
 222                              right_size)
 223                            reverse_sketch = P.sketch(reverse_for_sketch,
 224                              right_size)
 225                            forward = filtered_alignment(l1, r1, lw1,
 226                              right_words, lk1, right_sketch, left_size,
 227                              right_size, threshold, fast)
 228                            backward = filtered_alignment(l2,
 229                              reverse_for_alignment, lw2, reverse_words, lk2,
 230                              reverse_sketch, left_size, right_size, threshold,
 231                              fast)
 232                            C.maximum(forward, backward)
 233                          )},
 234                          P.copy(reverse_remaining))
 235                      )},
 236                      P.copy(reversed))
 237                  )},
 238                  Array.clone(U32, left_sketch))
 239              )},
 240              Array.clone(U32, left_words))
 241          )},
 242          C.sequence_copy(right))
 243      )},
 244      C.sequence_copy(left))
 246  law score_pair:
 247    for left: C.Record
 248    for right: C.Record
 249    for reverse: Bool
 250    for threshold: U32
 251    for fast: Bool
 252    U32
 254  def score_pair(left, right, reverse, threshold, fast):
 255    match left right:
 256      case C.Record{li, ll, ls, lw, lwords, lsketch} C.Record{ri, rl, rs, rw, rwords, rsketch}:
 257        C.branch(U32, reverse, \{
 258          True: (
 259            filtered_both(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lsketch, rsketch, lw, rw,
 260              threshold, fast)
 261          )
 262          False: (
 263            filtered_alignment(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lsketch, rsketch, lw, rw,
 264              threshold, fast)
 265          )
 266        })
 268  law append_centroid:
 269    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 270    for item: C.Record
 271    List<C.Record>
 273  def append_centroid(centroids, item):
 274    match centroids:
 275      case Nil{}:
 276        Con{item, Nil{}}
 277      case Con{head, tail}:
 278        Con{head, append_centroid(tail, item)}
 280  law find_centroid:
 281    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 282    for query: C.Record
 283    for +threshold: U32
 284    for +reverse: Bool
 285    for +fast: Bool
 286    for +best: U32
 287    for +best_score: U32
 288    U32
 290  def find_centroid(centroids, query, threshold, reverse, fast, best, best_score):
 291    match centroids:
 292      case Nil{}:
 293        best
 294      case Con{head, tail}:
 295        C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, U32,
 296          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
 297            C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, U32,
 298              \{Tuple: q1 => q2 => (
 299                +index = {C.record_index(h1) : U32}
 300                +score = {score_pair(q1, h2, reverse, threshold, fast) : U32}
 301                C.branch(U32, U32.is_ge(score, threshold), \{
 302                  True: (
 303                    index
 304                  )
 305                  False: (
 306                    find_centroid(tail, q2, threshold, reverse, fast, best,
 307                      best_score)
 308                  )
 309                })
 310              )},
 311              C.record_copy(query))
 312          )},
 313          C.record_copy(head))
 315  law greedy:
 316    for items: List<C.Record>
 317    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 318    for labels: Array<U32>
 319    for +size: U32
 320    for +threshold: U32
 321    for +reverse: Bool
 322    for +fast: Bool
 323    Array<U32>
 325  def greedy(items, centroids, labels, size, threshold, reverse, fast):
 326    match items:
 327      case Nil{}:
 328        labels
 329      case Con{head, tail}:
 330        C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, Array<U32>,
 331          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
 332            C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, Array<U32>,
 333              \{Tuple: q1 => q2 => (
 334                C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, Array<U32>,
 335                  \{Tuple: c1 => c2 => (
 336                    +index = {C.record_index(q1) : U32}
 337                    +center = {find_centroid(c1, q2, threshold, reverse, fast,
 338                      size, 0) : U32}
 339                    C.branch(Array<U32>, U32.is_eq(center, size), \{
 340                      True: (
 341                        greedy(tail, append_centroid(c2, h2), Array.set(U32, labels, index, index),
 342                          size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 343                      )
 344                      False: (
 345                        greedy(tail, c2, Array.set(U32, labels, index, center), size, threshold,
 346                          reverse, fast)
 347                      )
 348                    })
 349                  )},
 350                  C.records_copy(centroids))
 351              )},
 352              C.record_copy(h1))
 353          )},
 354          C.record_copy(head))
 356  law cluster:
 357    for items: List<C.Record>
 358    for +size: U32
 359    for threshold: U32
 360    for +reverse: Bool
 361    for +fast: Bool
 362    for +lz: Bool
 363    for +kmer_minimum: U32
 364    for +kmer_identity: U32
 365    Array<U32>
 367  law indexed_choose:
 368    for items: List<C.Record>
 369    for +all_long: Bool
 370    for +size: U32
 371    for +threshold: U32
 372    Array<U32>
 374  def indexed_choose(items, all_long, size, threshold):
 375    C.branch(Array<U32>, all_long, \{
 376      True: indexed_cluster(items, size, threshold)
 377      False: greedy(C.records_sort(items), Nil{}, C.array_new(size, 0), size,
 378        threshold, False{}, True{})
 379    })
 381  def cluster(items, size, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 382              kmer_identity):
 383    C.branch(Array<U32>, lz, \{
 384      True: lz_cluster(items, size, threshold, kmer_minimum, kmer_identity)
 385      False: C.branch(Array<U32>, fast && Bool.not(reverse), \{
 386        True: C.apply(List<C.Record> & Bool, Array<U32>,
 387          \{Tuple: indexed_items => all_long => (
 388            indexed_choose(indexed_items, all_long, size, threshold)
 389          )}, V.records_all_long(items))
 390        False: greedy(C.records_sort(items), Nil{}, C.array_new(size, 0), size,
 391          threshold, reverse, fast)
 392      })
 393    })
 395  law run_dataset:
 396    for dataset: C.Dataset
 397    for +threshold: U32
 398    for reverse: Bool
 399    for fast: Bool
 400    for lz: Bool
 401    for kmer_minimum: U32
 402    for kmer_identity: U32
 403    for resolution: U32
 404    for seed: U32
 405    for passes: Nat
 406    IO(Unit)
 408  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 409                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
 410    match dataset:
 411      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
 412        +size = {raw_size : U32}
 413        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
 414          \{Tuple: input => output => (
 415            labels = cluster(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast, lz,
 416              kmer_minimum, kmer_identity)
 417            C.output(output, labels)
 418          )},
 419          C.records_copy(items))
 421  law run_config:
 422    for config: C.Config
 423    IO(Unit)
 425  def run_config(config):
 426    match config:
 427      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 428                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
 429        do IO<Unit>:
 430          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
 431          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 432            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
 434  law main:
 435    IO(Unit)
 437  def main():
 438    do IO<Unit>:
 439      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
 440      run_config(config)
complete_linkage.bend
4  import Base
   5  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   6  import ./lib/vclust_index.bend as V
   7  import ./lib/lz_ani_graph.bend as LZG
   9  type Candidate is Type:
  10    Candidate{score: U32, first: U32, other: U32}
  12  law candidate_choose:
  13    for best: Candidate
  14    for +raw_score: U32
  15    for +raw_left: U32
  16    for +raw_right: U32
  17    Candidate
  19  def candidate_choose(best, raw_score, raw_left, raw_right):
  20    match best:
  21      case Candidate{raw_previous, raw_first, raw_other}:
  22        +left = {C.minimum(raw_left, raw_right) : U32}
  23        +right = {C.maximum(raw_left, raw_right) : U32}
  24        +score = {raw_score : U32}
  25        +previous = {raw_previous : U32}
  26        +first = {raw_first : U32}
  27        +other = {raw_other : U32}
  28        better = U32.is_gt(score, previous) ||
  29          (U32.is_eq(score, previous) &&
  30            (U32.is_lt(left, first) ||
  31              (U32.is_eq(left, first) && U32.is_lt(right, other))))
  32        C.branch(Candidate, better, \{
  33          True: (
  34            Candidate{score, left, right}
  35          )
  36          False: (
  37            Candidate{previous, first, other}
  38          )
  39        })
  41  law row_best:
  42    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  43    for +row: U32
  44    for best: Candidate
  45    C.Neighbors & Candidate
  47  def row_best(neighbors, row, best):
  48    match neighbors:
  49      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  50        (C.NeighborsNil{}, best)
  51      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, raw_score, tail}:
  52        +target = {raw_target : U32}
  53        +score = {raw_score : U32}
  54        C.apply(C.Neighbors & Candidate, C.Neighbors & Candidate,
  55          \{Tuple: rest => final => (
  56            (C.NeighborsCon{target, score, rest}, final)
  57          )},
  58          row_best(tail, row, candidate_choose(best, score, row, target)))
  60  law best_rows:
  61    for fuel: Nat
  62    for +row: U32
  63    for +size: U32
  64    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
  65    for best: Candidate
  66    Array<C.Neighbors> & Candidate
  68  def best_rows(fuel, row, size, rows, best):
  69    match fuel:
  70      case Zero{}:
  71        (rows, best)
  72      case Succ{rest}:
  73        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors,
  74          Array<C.Neighbors> & Candidate,
  75          \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
  76            C.apply(C.Neighbors & Candidate,
  77              Array<C.Neighbors> & Candidate,
  78              \{Tuple: rebuilt => next_best => (
  79                updated = Array.set(C.Neighbors, next_rows, row, rebuilt)
  80                best_rows(rest, U32.add(row, 1), size, updated, next_best)
  81              )},
  82              row_best(neighbors, row, best))
  83          )},
  84          Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, row))
  86  law remove_pair:
  87    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  88    for +left: U32
  89    for +right: U32
  90    for left_score: U32
  91    for right_score: U32
  92    C.Neighbors & (U32 & U32)
  94  def remove_pair(neighbors, left, right, left_score, right_score):
  95    match neighbors:
  96      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  97        (C.NeighborsNil{}, (left_score, right_score))
  98      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, raw_score, tail}:
  99        +target = {raw_target : U32}
 100        +score = {raw_score : U32}
 101        next_left = C.select(U32.is_eq(target, left), score, left_score)
 102        next_right = C.select(U32.is_eq(target, right), score, right_score)
 103        keep = U32.is_ne(target, left) && U32.is_ne(target, right)
 104        C.apply(C.Neighbors & (U32 & U32), C.Neighbors & (U32 & U32),
 105          \{Tuple: rest => scores => (
 106            C.branch(C.Neighbors & (U32 & U32), keep, \{
 107              True: (
 108                (C.NeighborsCon{target, score, rest}, scores)
 109              )
 110              False: (
 111                (rest, scores)
 112              )
 113            })
 114          )},
 115          remove_pair(tail, left, right, next_left, next_right))
 117  law merge_one:
 118    for +index: U32
 119    for +left: U32
 120    for +right: U32
 121    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
 122    for new_left: C.Neighbors
 123    C.Neighbors & C.Neighbors
 125  def merge_one(index, left, right, neighbors, new_left):
 126    C.branch(C.Neighbors & C.Neighbors,
 127      U32.is_eq(index, left) || U32.is_eq(index, right), \{
 128      True: (
 129        (C.NeighborsNil{}, new_left)
 130      )
 131      False: (
 132        C.apply(C.Neighbors & (U32 & U32), C.Neighbors & C.Neighbors,
 133          \{Tuple: cleaned => \{Tuple: raw_a => raw_b => (
 134            +a = {raw_a : U32}
 135            +b = {raw_b : U32}
 136            C.branch(C.Neighbors & C.Neighbors,
 137              U32.is_gt(a, 0) && U32.is_gt(b, 0), \{
 138              True: (
 139                +score = {C.minimum(a, b) : U32}
 140                (C.NeighborsCon{left, score, cleaned},
 141                  C.NeighborsCon{index, score, new_left})
 142              )
 143              False: (
 144                (cleaned, new_left)
 145              )
 146            })
 147          )}},
 148          remove_pair(neighbors, left, right, 0, 0))
 149      )
 150    })
 152  law merge_rows:
 153    for fuel: Nat
 154    for +index: U32
 155    for +left: U32
 156    for +right: U32
 157    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 158    for new_left: C.Neighbors
 159    Array<C.Neighbors>
 161  def merge_rows(fuel, index, left, right, rows, new_left):
 162    match fuel:
 163      case Zero{}:
 164        Array.set(C.Neighbors, rows, left, new_left)
 165      case Succ{rest}:
 166        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Array<C.Neighbors>,
 167          \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
 168            C.apply(C.Neighbors & C.Neighbors, Array<C.Neighbors>,
 169              \{Tuple: rebuilt => next_left => (
 170                updated = Array.set(C.Neighbors, next_rows, index, rebuilt)
 171                merge_rows(rest, U32.add(index, 1), left, right, updated, next_left)
 172              )},
 173              merge_one(index, left, right, neighbors, new_left))
 174          )},
 175          Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, index))
 177  law merge_step:
 178    for best: Candidate
 179    for +size: U32
 180    for +threshold: U32
 181    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 182    for labels: Array<U32>
 183    for continue: Array<C.Neighbors> -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32>
 184    Array<U32>
 186  def merge_step(best, size, threshold, rows, labels, continue):
 187    match best:
 188      case Candidate{score, raw_left, raw_right}:
 189        +left = {raw_left : U32}
 190        +right = {raw_right : U32}
 191        C.branch(Array<U32>, U32.is_lt(score, threshold), \{
 192          True: (
 193            labels
 194          )
 195          False: (
 196            updated = merge_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, left, right, rows,
 197              C.NeighborsNil{})
 198            joined = C.labels_replace(C.to_nat(size), 0, labels, right, left)
 199            continue(updated, joined)
 200          )
 201        })
 203  law merge:
 204    for fuel: Nat
 205    for +size: U32
 206    for +threshold: U32
 207    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 208    for labels: Array<U32>
 209    Array<U32>
 211  def merge(fuel, size, threshold, rows, labels):
 212    match fuel:
 213      case Zero{}:
 214        labels
 215      case Succ{rest}:
 216        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & Candidate, Array<U32>,
 217          \{Tuple: next_rows => best => (
 218            merge_step(best, size, threshold, next_rows, labels,
 219              r => l => merge(rest, size, threshold, r, l))
 220          )},
 221          best_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, rows,
 222          Candidate{0, size, size}))
 224  law cluster:
 225    for graph: C.SparseGraph
 226    for +size: U32
 227    for threshold: U32
 228    for resolution: U32
 229    for seed: U32
 230    for passes: Nat
 231    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 233  def cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes):
 234    match graph:
 235      case C.SparseGraph{raw_size, rows}:
 236        Done{merge(C.to_nat(size), size, threshold, rows, C.labels_identity(size))}
 238  law run_dataset:
 239    for dataset: C.Dataset
 240    for +threshold: U32
 241    for reverse: Bool
 242    for fast: Bool
 243    for lz: Bool
 244    for kmer_minimum: U32
 245    for kmer_identity: U32
 246    for resolution: U32
 247    for seed: U32
 248    for passes: Nat
 249    IO(Unit)
 251  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 252                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
 253    match dataset:
 254      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
 255        +size = {raw_size : U32}
 256        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
 257          \{Tuple: input => output => (
 258            graph = C.branch(C.SparseGraph, lz, \{
 259              True: LZG.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, kmer_minimum,
 260                kmer_identity)
 261              False: V.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 262            })
 263            do IO<Unit>:
 264              labels: Array<U32> <- C.result_unwrap(Array<U32>,
 265                cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes))
 266              C.output(output, labels)
 267          )},
 268          C.records_copy(items))
 270  law run_config:
 271    for config: C.Config
 272    IO(Unit)
 274  def run_config(config):
 275    match config:
 276      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 277                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
 278        do IO<Unit>:
 279          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
 280          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 281            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
 283  law main:
 284    IO(Unit)
 286  def main():
 287    do IO<Unit>:
 288      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
 289      run_config(config)
leiden.bend
4  import Base
   5  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   6  import ./lib/vclust_index.bend as V
   7  import ./lib/lz_ani_graph.bend as LZG
  12  type Graph is Type:
  13    Graph{size: U32, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>, mass: Array<U32>}
  15  type Stats is Type:
  16    Stats{edges: Array<U32>, mass: Array<U32>, count: Array<U32>}
  21  law random_next:
  22    for seed: U32
  23    U32
  25  def random_next(seed):
  26    U32.add(U32.mul(seed, 1664525), 1013904223)
  28  law shuffle:
  29    for fuel: Nat
  30    for +index: U32
  31    for seed: U32
  32    for order: Array<U32>
  33    Array<U32> & U32
  35  def shuffle(fuel, index, seed, order):
  36    match fuel:
  37      case Zero{}:
  38        (order, seed)
  39      case Succ{rest}:
  40        +next_seed = {random_next(seed) : U32}
  41        +other = {U32.mod(next_seed, U32.add(index, 1)) : U32}
  42        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
  43          \{Tuple: next => first => (
  44            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
  45              \{Tuple: updated => second => (
  46                swapped = Array.set(U32, Array.set(U32, updated, other, first), index, second)
  47                shuffle(rest, U32.sub(index, 1), next_seed, swapped)
  48              )},
  49              Array.get(U32, next, other))
  50          )},
  51          Array.get(U32, order, index))
  53  law graph_copy:
  54    for graph: Graph
  55    Graph & Graph
  57  def graph_copy(graph):
  58    match graph:
  59      case Graph{raw, rows, mass}:
  60        +size = {raw : U32}
  61        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & Array<C.Neighbors>, Graph & Graph,
  62          \{Tuple: e1 => e2 => (
  63            C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Graph & Graph,
  64              \{Tuple: m1 => m2 => (
  65                (Graph{size, e1, m1}, Graph{size, e2, m2})
  66              )},
  67              Array.clone(U32, mass))
  68          )},
  69          Array.clone(C.Neighbors, rows))
  71  law array_add:
  72    for array: Array<U32>
  73    for +index: U32
  74    for value: U32
  75    Array<U32>
  77  def array_add(array, index, value):
  78    C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
  79      \{Tuple: next => old => (
  80        Array.set(U32, next, index, U32.add(old, value))
  81      )},
  82      Array.get(U32, array, index))
  84  law stats_add:
  85    for stats: Stats
  86    for +community: U32
  87    for edge: U32
  88    for mass: U32
  89    Stats
  91  def stats_add(stats, community, edge, mass):
  92    match stats:
  93      case Stats{edges, masses, counts}:

  95          array_add(masses, community, mass), array_add(counts, community, 1)}
  97  law stats_nodes:
  98    for fuel: Nat
  99    for +index: U32
 100    for +node: U32
 101    for mass: Array<U32>
 102    for labels: Array<U32>
 103    for stats: Stats

 106  def stats_nodes(fuel, index, node, mass, labels, stats):
 107    match fuel:
 108      case Zero{}:
 109        stats
 110      case Succ{rest}:
 111        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Stats,
 112          \{Tuple: next_mass => weight => (
 113            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Stats,
 114              \{Tuple: next_labels => community => (
 115                next_stats = C.branch(Stats, U32.is_eq(index, node), \{
 116                  True: (
 117                    stats
 118                  )
 119                  False: (
 120                    stats_add(stats, community, 0, weight)
 121                  )
 122                })
 123                stats_nodes(rest, U32.add(index, 1), node, next_mass,
 124                  next_labels, next_stats)
 125              )},
 126              Array.get(U32, labels, index))
 127          )},
 128          Array.get(U32, mass, index))
 130  law stats_edge_add:
 131    for stats: Stats

 133    for edge: U32

 136  def stats_edge_add(stats, community, edge):
 137    match stats:
 138      case Stats{edges, mass, counts}:
 139        Stats{array_add(edges, community, edge), mass, counts}
 141  law stats_neighbors:
 142    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
 143    for labels: Array<U32>
 144    for stats: Stats
 145    for +node: U32
 146    Stats
 148  def stats_neighbors(neighbors, labels, stats, node):
 149    match neighbors:
 150      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
 151        stats
 152      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, weight, tail}:
 153        +target = {raw_target : U32}
 154        C.branch(Stats, U32.is_eq(target, node), \{
 155          True: (
 156            stats_neighbors(tail, labels, stats, node)
 157          )
 158          False: (
 159            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Stats,
 160              \{Tuple: next_labels => community => (
 161                stats_neighbors(tail, next_labels,
 162                  stats_edge_add(stats, community, weight), node)
 163              )},
 164              Array.get(U32, labels, target))
 165          )
 166        })
 168  law node_stats:
 169    for graph: Graph
 170    for labels: Array<U32>
 171    for +node: U32

 174  def node_stats(graph, labels, node):
 175    match graph:
 176      case Graph{raw, rows, mass}:
 177        +size = {raw : U32}
 178        initial = {Stats{C.array_new(size, 0), C.array_new(size, 0),
 179          C.array_new(size, 0)} : Stats}
 180        C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Stats,
 181          \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
 182            base = stats_nodes(C.to_nat(size), 0, node, mass, l1, initial)
 183            C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Stats,
 184              \{Tuple: unused_rows => neighbors => (
 185                stats_neighbors(neighbors, l2, base, node)
 186              )},
 187              Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, node))
 188          )},
 189          Array.clone(U32, labels))
 191  law penalty:
 192    for resolution: U32
 193    for left: U32

 197  def penalty(resolution, left, right):
 198    Nat.mul(C.to_nat(resolution), Nat.mul(C.to_nat(left), C.to_nat(right)))
 200  law edge_score:
 201    for edge: U32
 202    Nat
 204  def edge_score(edge):
 205    Nat.mul(C.to_nat(edge), C.to_nat(10000))
 209  law score_better:
 210    for positive: Nat
 211    for negative: Nat
 212    for old_positive: Nat
 213    for old_negative: Nat
 214    Bool
 216  def score_better(positive, negative, old_positive, old_negative):
 217    Nat.is_lt(Nat.add(old_positive, negative),
 218      Nat.add(positive, old_negative))
 220  law best_community:
 221    for fuel: Nat
 222    for +index: U32
 223    for +node_mass: U32
 224    for +resolution: U32
 225    for edges: Array<U32>
 226    for mass: Array<U32>
 227    for +best: U32
 228    for +best_positive: Nat
 229    for +best_negative: Nat
 230    U32
 232  def best_community(fuel, index, node_mass, resolution, edges, mass, best, best_positive, best_negative):
 233    match fuel:
 234      case Zero{}:
 235        best
 236      case Succ{rest}:
 237        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 238          \{Tuple: next_edges => edge => (
 239            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 240              \{Tuple: next_mass => weight => (
 241                +positive = {edge_score(edge) : Nat}
 242                +negative = {penalty(resolution, node_mass, weight) : Nat}
 243                C.branch(U32, score_better(positive, negative, best_positive, best_negative), \{
 244                  True: (
 245                    best_community(rest, U32.add(index, 1), node_mass, resolution, next_edges,
 246                      next_mass, index, positive, negative)
 247                  )
 248                  False: (
 249                    best_community(rest, U32.add(index, 1), node_mass, resolution, next_edges,
 250                      next_mass, best, best_positive, best_negative)
 251                  )
 252                })
 253              )},
 254              Array.get(U32, mass, index))
 255          )},
 256          Array.get(U32, edges, index))
 258  law move_destination:
 259    for stats: Stats

 261    for +node_mass: U32
 262    for +size: U32
 263    for +resolution: U32
 264    U32
 266  def move_destination(stats, own, node_mass, size, resolution):
 267    match stats:
 268      case Stats{edges, mass, counts}:
 269        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 270          \{Tuple: next_edges => own_edge => (
 271            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 272              \{Tuple: next_mass => own_mass => (
 273                best_community(C.to_nat(size), 0, node_mass, resolution, next_edges,
 274                  next_mass, own, edge_score(own_edge),
 275                  penalty(resolution, node_mass, own_mass))
 276              )},
 277              Array.get(U32, mass, own))
 278          )},
 279          Array.get(U32, edges, own))

 291  law move_sweep:
 292    for fuel: Nat
 293    for +index: U32
 294    for +size: U32
 295    for +resolution: U32
 296    for graph: Graph
 297    for order: Array<U32>
 298    for labels: Array<U32>
 299    for changed: U32
 300    (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32
 302  def move_sweep(fuel, index, size, resolution, graph, order, labels, changed):
 303    match fuel:
 304      case Zero{}:
 305        ((graph, labels), changed)
 306      case Succ{rest}:
 307        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32,
 308          \{Tuple: next_order => raw_node => (
 309            +node = {raw_node : U32}
 310            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32,
 311              \{Tuple: next_labels => raw_own => (
 312                C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32,
 313                  \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
 314                    C.apply(Graph & Graph, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32,
 315                      \{Tuple: g1 => g2 => (
 316                        C.apply(Graph & Graph, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32,
 317                          \{Tuple: g3 => g4 => (
 318                            +own = {raw_own : U32}
 319                            node_mass = graph_node_mass(g1, node)
 320                            stats = node_stats(g3, l1, node)
 321                            +destination = {move_destination(stats, own, node_mass, size,
 322                              resolution) : U32}
 323                            updated = Array.set(U32, l2, node, destination)
 324                            moves = U32.add(changed, C.select(U32.is_eq(own, destination), 0, 1))
 325                            move_sweep(rest, U32.add(index, 1), size, resolution, g4, next_order,
 326                              updated, moves)
 327                          )},
 328                          graph_copy(g2))
 329                      )},
 330                      graph_copy(graph))
 331                  )},
 332                  Array.clone(U32, next_labels))
 333              )},
 334              Array.get(U32, labels, node))
 335          )},
 336          Array.get(U32, order, index))
 338  law move_nodes:
 339    for fuel: Nat
 340    for +size: U32
 341    for +resolution: U32
 342    for seed: U32
 343    for graph: Graph
 344    for labels: Array<U32>
 345    Result<String, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32>
 347  def move_nodes(fuel, size, resolution, seed, graph, labels):
 348    match fuel:
 349      case Zero{}:
 350        Fail{"Leiden local moves exceeded CLUSTER_PASSES"}
 351      case Succ{rest}:
 352        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Result<String, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32>,
 353          \{Tuple: order => next_seed => (
 354            C.apply((Graph & Array<U32>) & U32, Result<String, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32>,
 355              \{Tuple: \{Tuple: next_graph => next_labels => changed => (
 356                C.branch(Result<String, (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32>, U32.is_eq(changed, 0), \{
 357                  True: (
 358                    Done{((next_graph, next_labels), next_seed)}
 359                  )
 360                  False: (
 361                    move_nodes(rest, size, resolution, next_seed, next_graph, next_labels)
 362                  )
 363                })
 364              )}},
 365              move_sweep(C.to_nat(size), 0,
 366              size, resolution, graph, order, labels, 0))
 367          )},
 368          shuffle(C.to_nat(size), U32.sub(size, 1), seed,
 369          C.labels_identity(size)))
 371  law boundary_neighbors:
 372    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
 373    for +parent: U32
 374    for +community: U32
 375    for parents: Array<U32>
 376    for refined: Array<U32>
 377    for total: U32
 378    (Array<U32> & Array<U32>) & U32
 380  def boundary_neighbors(neighbors, parent, community, parents, refined, total):
 381    match neighbors:
 382      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
 383        ((parents, refined), total)
 384      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, edge, tail}:
 385        +target = {raw_target : U32}
 386        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, (Array<U32> & Array<U32>) & U32,
 387          \{Tuple: next_parents => other_parent => (
 388            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, (Array<U32> & Array<U32>) & U32,
 389              \{Tuple: next_refined => other_community => (
 390                included = U32.is_eq(parent, other_parent) &&
 391                  U32.is_ne(community, other_community)
 392                boundary_neighbors(tail, parent, community, next_parents,
 393                  next_refined, U32.add(total, C.select(included, edge, 0)))
 394              )},
 395              Array.get(U32, refined, target))
 396          )},
 397          Array.get(U32, parents, target))
 399  law boundary_rows:
 400    for fuel: Nat
 401    for +row: U32
 402    for +size: U32
 403    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 404    for parents: Array<U32>
 405    for refined: Array<U32>
 406    for boundary: Array<U32>
 407    Array<U32>
 409  def boundary_rows(fuel, row, size, rows, parents, refined, boundary):
 410    match fuel:
 411      case Zero{}:
 412        boundary
 413      case Succ{rest}:
 414        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
 415          \{Tuple: next_parents => parent => (
 416            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
 417              \{Tuple: next_refined => raw_community => (
 418                +community = {raw_community : U32}
 419                C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Array<U32>,
 420                  \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
 421                    C.apply((Array<U32> & Array<U32>) & U32, Array<U32>,
 422                      \{Tuple: \{Tuple: updated_parents => updated_refined => total => (
 423                        next_boundary = array_add(boundary, community, total)
 424                        boundary_rows(rest, U32.add(row, 1), size, next_rows,
 425                          updated_parents, updated_refined, next_boundary)
 426                      )}},
 427                      boundary_neighbors(neighbors, parent, community,
 428                      next_parents, next_refined, 0))
 429                  )},
 430                  Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, row))
 431              )},
 432              Array.get(U32, refined, row))
 433          )},
 434          Array.get(U32, parents, row))

 437    for graph: Graph
 438    for parents: Array<U32>
 439    for refined: Array<U32>
 440    Array<U32>
 442  def boundaries(graph, parents, refined):
 443    match graph:
 444      case Graph{raw, rows, mass}:
 445        +size = {raw : U32}
 446        boundary_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, rows, parents, refined,
 447          C.array_new(size, 0))
 449  law well_connected:
 450    for edge: U32
 451    for resolution: U32
 452    for left: U32
 453    for right: U32
 454    Bool
 456  def well_connected(edge, resolution, left, right):
 457    Bool.not(Nat.is_lt(edge_score(edge), penalty(resolution, left, right)))
 459  law eligible_node:
 460    for stats: Stats
 461    for +parent: U32
 462    for +node_mass: U32
 463    for resolution: U32
 464    Bool & U32
 466  def eligible_node(stats, parent, node_mass, resolution):
 467    match stats:
 468      case Stats{edges, mass, count}:
 469        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Bool & U32,
 470          \{Tuple: unused_edges => edge => (
 471            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Bool & U32,
 472              \{Tuple: unused_mass => raw_mass => (
 473                +other_mass = {raw_mass : U32}
 474                (well_connected(edge, resolution, node_mass, other_mass),
 475                  U32.add(node_mass, other_mass))
 476              )},
 477              Array.get(U32, mass, parent))
 478          )},
 479          Array.get(U32, edges, parent))
 481  law refine_candidates:
 482    for fuel: Nat
 483    for +index: U32
 484    for +node: U32
 485    for +node_mass: U32
 486    for +parent: U32
 487    for +parent_mass: U32
 488    for +resolution: U32
 489    for edges: Array<U32>
 490    for mass: Array<U32>
 491    for counts: Array<U32>
 492    for boundary: Array<U32>
 493    for parents: Array<U32>
 494    for +chosen: U32
 495    for +seen: U32
 496    for seed: U32
 497    U32 & U32
 499  def refine_candidates(fuel, index, node, node_mass, parent, parent_mass, resolution, edges, mass, counts, boundary, parents, chosen, seen, seed):
 500    match fuel:
 501      case Zero{}:
 502        (chosen, seed)
 503      case Succ{rest}:
 504        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 505          \{Tuple: next_edges => edge => (
 506            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 507              \{Tuple: next_mass => raw_weight => (
 508                C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 509                  \{Tuple: next_counts => count => (
 510                    C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 511                      \{Tuple: next_boundary => cut => (
 512                        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 513                          \{Tuple: next_parents => candidate_parent => (
 514                            +weight = {raw_weight : U32}
 515                            valid = U32.is_ne(index, node) && U32.is_gt(count, 0) &&
 516                              U32.is_eq(candidate_parent, parent) &&
 517                              well_connected(edge, resolution, node_mass, weight) &&
 518                              well_connected(cut, resolution, weight, C.subtract(parent_mass, weight))
 519                            C.branch(U32 & U32, valid, \{
 520                              True: (
 521                                +next_seed = {random_next(seed) : U32}
 522                                destination = C.select(U32.is_eq(U32.mod(next_seed, U32.add(seen, 1)), 0),
 523                                  index, chosen)
 524                                refine_candidates(rest, U32.add(index, 1), node, node_mass, parent,
 525                                  parent_mass, resolution, next_edges, next_mass, next_counts,
 526                                  next_boundary, next_parents, destination, U32.add(seen, 1), next_seed)
 527                              )
 528                              False: (
 529                                refine_candidates(rest, U32.add(index, 1), node, node_mass, parent,
 530                                  parent_mass, resolution, next_edges, next_mass, next_counts,
 531                                  next_boundary, next_parents, chosen, seen, seed)
 532                              )
 533                            })
 534                          )},
 535                          Array.get(U32, parents, index))
 536                      )},
 537                      Array.get(U32, boundary, index))
 538                  )},
 539                  Array.get(U32, counts, index))
 540              )},
 541              Array.get(U32, mass, index))
 542          )},
 543          Array.get(U32, edges, index))
 545  law refine_choice:
 546    for stats: Stats
 547    for +node: U32
 548    for node_mass: U32
 549    for parent: U32
 550    for parent_mass: U32
 551    for resolution: U32
 552    for +size: U32
 553    for boundary: Array<U32>
 554    for parents: Array<U32>
 555    for seed: U32
 556    U32 & U32
 558  def refine_choice(stats, node, node_mass, parent, parent_mass, resolution, size, boundary, parents, seed):
 559    match stats:
 560      case Stats{edges, mass, counts}:
 561        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32 & U32,
 562          \{Tuple: next_counts => own_count => (
 563            C.branch(U32 & U32, U32.is_eq(own_count, 0), \{
 564              True: (
 565                refine_candidates(C.to_nat(size), 0, node, node_mass, parent,
 566                  parent_mass, resolution, edges, mass, next_counts, boundary,
 567                  parents, node, 0, seed)
 568              )
 569              False: (
 570                (node, seed)
 571              )
 572            })
 573          )},
 574          Array.get(U32, counts, node))
 576  law refine_node:
 577    for graph: Graph
 578    for parents: Array<U32>
 579    for refined: Array<U32>
 580    for +node: U32
 581    for +size: U32
 582    for +resolution: U32
 583    for seed: U32
 584    Array<U32> & U32
 586  def refine_node(graph, parents, refined, node, size, resolution, seed):
 587    C.apply(Graph & Graph, Array<U32> & U32,
 588      \{Tuple: g1 => g2 => (
 589        C.apply(Graph & Graph, Array<U32> & U32,
 590          \{Tuple: g3 => g4 => (
 591            C.apply(Graph & Graph, Array<U32> & U32,
 592              \{Tuple: g5 => g6 => (
 593                C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 594                  \{Tuple: p1 => p2 => (
 595                    C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 596                      \{Tuple: p3 => p4 => (
 597                        C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 598                          \{Tuple: p5 => p6 => (
 599                            C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 600                              \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
 601                                C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 602                                  \{Tuple: r3 => r4 => (
 603                                    +node_mass = {graph_node_mass(g1, node) : U32}
 604                                    +parent = {C.labels_lookup(p1, node) : U32}
 605                                    parent_stats = node_stats(g3, p3, node)
 606                                    C.apply(Bool & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 607                                      \{Tuple: eligible => parent_mass => (
 608                                        stats = node_stats(g5, r1, node)
 609                                        boundary = boundaries(g6, p5, r3)
 610                                        result = C.branch(U32 & U32, eligible, \{
 611                                          True: (
 612                                            refine_choice(stats, node, node_mass, parent, parent_mass, resolution,
 613                                              size, boundary, p6, seed)
 614                                          )
 615                                          False: (
 616                                            (node, seed)
 617                                          )
 618                                        })
 619                                        C.apply(U32 & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 620                                          \{Tuple: chosen => next_seed => (
 621                                            (Array.set(U32, r4, node, chosen), next_seed)
 622                                          )},
 623                                          result)
 624                                      )},
 625                                      eligible_node(parent_stats, parent,
 626                                      node_mass, resolution))
 627                                  )},
 628                                  Array.clone(U32, r2))
 629                              )},
 630                              Array.clone(U32, refined))
 631                          )},
 632                          Array.clone(U32, p4))
 633                      )},
 634                      Array.clone(U32, p2))
 635                  )},
 636                  Array.clone(U32, parents))
 637              )},
 638              graph_copy(g4))
 639          )},
 640          graph_copy(g2))
 641      )},
 642      graph_copy(graph))
 644  law refine_sweep:
 645    for fuel: Nat
 646    for +index: U32
 647    for +size: U32
 648    for +resolution: U32
 649    for seed: U32
 650    for graph: Graph
 651    for parents: Array<U32>
 652    for order: Array<U32>
 653    for refined: Array<U32>
 654    Array<U32> & U32
 656  def refine_sweep(fuel, index, size, resolution, seed, graph, parents, order, refined):
 657    match fuel:
 658      case Zero{}:
 659        (refined, seed)
 660      case Succ{rest}:
 661        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 662          \{Tuple: next_order => raw_node => (
 663            C.apply(Graph & Graph, Array<U32> & U32,
 664              \{Tuple: g1 => g2 => (
 665                C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 666                  \{Tuple: p1 => p2 => (
 667                    +node = {raw_node : U32}
 668                    C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32> & U32,
 669                      \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
 670                        own = C.labels_lookup(r1, node)
 671                        result = C.branch(Array<U32> & U32, U32.is_eq(own, node), \{
 672                          True: (
 673                            refine_node(g1, p1, r2, node, size, resolution, seed)
 674                          )
 675                          False: (
 676                            (r2, seed)
 677                          )
 678                        })
 679                        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 680                          \{Tuple: next_refined => next_seed => (
 681                            refine_sweep(rest, U32.add(index, 1), size, resolution, next_seed, g2, p2,
 682                              next_order, next_refined)
 683                          )},
 684                          result)
 685                      )},
 686                      Array.clone(U32, refined))
 687                  )},
 688                  Array.clone(U32, parents))
 689              )},
 690              graph_copy(graph))
 691          )},
 692          Array.get(U32, order, index))

 695    for graph: Graph

 697    for +size: U32
 698    for resolution: U32

 702  def refine(graph, parents, size, resolution, seed):
 703    C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 704      \{Tuple: order => next_seed => (
 705        refine_sweep(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, resolution, next_seed, graph,
 706          parents, order, C.labels_identity(size))
 707      )},
 708      shuffle(C.to_nat(size), U32.sub(size, 1), seed,
 709      C.labels_identity(size)))

 712    for fuel: Nat
 713    for +index: U32
 714    for +sentinel: U32
 715    for labels: Array<U32>
 716    for mapping: Array<U32>
 717    for +count: U32

 720  def compact(fuel, index, sentinel, labels, mapping, count):
 721    match fuel:
 722      case Zero{}:
 723        (labels, count)
 724      case Succ{rest}:
 725        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 726          \{Tuple: next_labels => raw_old => (
 727            +old = {raw_old : U32}
 728            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
 729              \{Tuple: next_mapping => raw_known => (
 730                +known = {raw_known : U32}
 731                +assigned = {C.select(U32.is_eq(known, sentinel), count, known) : U32}
 732                new_count = U32.add(count, C.select(U32.is_eq(known, sentinel), 1, 0))
 733                updated_mapping = Array.set(U32, next_mapping, old, assigned)
 734                updated_labels = Array.set(U32, next_labels, index, assigned)
 735                compact(rest, U32.add(index, 1), sentinel, updated_labels, updated_mapping,
 736                  new_count)
 737              )},
 738              Array.get(U32, mapping, old))
 739          )},
 740          Array.get(U32, labels, index))

 751  law neighbor_weight_add:
 752    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
 753    for +target: U32
 754    for +value: U32
 755    C.Neighbors
 757  def neighbor_weight_add(neighbors, target, value):
 758    match neighbors:
 759      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
 760        C.NeighborsCon{target, value, C.NeighborsNil{}}
 761      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_other, raw_weight, tail}:
 762        +other = {raw_other : U32}
 763        +weight = {raw_weight : U32}
 764        C.branch(C.Neighbors, U32.is_eq(other, target), \{
 765          True: (
 766            C.NeighborsCon{other, U32.add(weight, value), tail}
 767          )
 768          False: (
 769            C.NeighborsCon{other, weight,
 770              neighbor_weight_add(tail, target, value)}
 771          )
 772        })
 774  law row_weight_add:
 775    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>

 777    for +target: U32
 778    for value: U32
 779    Array<C.Neighbors>
 781  def row_weight_add(rows, source, target, value):

 783      \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
 784        Array.set(C.Neighbors, next_rows, source,
 785          neighbor_weight_add(neighbors, target, value))
 786      )},
 787      Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, source))

 797    match neighbors:

 801        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Array<C.Neighbors>,
 802          \{Tuple: next_refined => target_community => (
 803            updated = row_weight_add(result, community, target_community, edge)
 804            aggregate_neighbors(tail, community, next_refined, updated)
 805          )},
 806          Array.get(U32, refined, target))

 809    for fuel: Nat

 822  def aggregate_rows(fuel, row, old_size, new_size, rows, mass, refined, parents, new_rows, new_mass, new_parents):
 823    match fuel:
 824      case Zero{}:
 825        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Graph & Array<U32>,
 826          \{Tuple: compact_parents => count => (
 827            (Graph{new_size, new_rows, new_mass}, compact_parents)
 828          )},
 829          compact_labels(new_parents, new_size,
 830          old_size))
 831      case Succ{rest}:
 832        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Graph & Array<U32>,
 833          \{Tuple: next_mass => weight => (
 834            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Graph & Array<U32>,
 835              \{Tuple: next_refined => raw_community => (
 836                C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Graph & Array<U32>,
 837                  \{Tuple: next_parents => parent => (
 838                    +community = {raw_community : U32}
 839                    C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Graph & Array<U32>,
 840                      \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
 841                        C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<C.Neighbors>, Graph & Array<U32>,
 842                          \{Tuple: updated_refined => updated_rows => (
 843                            updated_mass = array_add(new_mass, community, weight)
 844                            updated_parents = Array.set(U32, new_parents, community, parent)
 845                            aggregate_rows(rest, U32.add(row, 1), old_size, new_size,
 846                              next_rows, next_mass, updated_refined, next_parents,
 847                              updated_rows, updated_mass, updated_parents)
 848                          )},
 849                          aggregate_neighbors(neighbors, community, next_refined,
 850                          new_rows))
 851                      )},
 852                      Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, row))
 853                  )},
 854                  Array.get(U32, parents, row))
 855              )},
 856              Array.get(U32, refined, row))
 857          )},
 858          Array.get(U32, mass, row))

 861    for graph: Graph
 862    for refined: Array<U32>

 869      case Graph{raw_size, rows, mass}:
 870        +old_size = {raw_size : U32}
 871        aggregate_rows(C.to_nat(old_size), 0, old_size, new_size, rows, mass,
 872          refined, parents,
 873          Array.new(C.Neighbors, C.array_depth(31n, new_size, 1, 0n), C.NeighborsNil{}),
 874          C.array_new(new_size, 0), C.array_new(new_size, 0))
 876  law canonical_minima:
 877    for fuel: Nat
 878    for +index: U32
 879    for labels: Array<U32>
 880    for minima: Array<U32>

 883  def canonical_minima(fuel, index, labels, minima):
 884    match fuel:
 885      case Zero{}:
 886        (labels, minima)
 887      case Succ{rest}:
 888        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Array<U32>,
 889          \{Tuple: next_labels => raw_label => (
 890            +label = {raw_label : U32}
 891            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Array<U32>,
 892              \{Tuple: next_minima => old => (
 893                updated = Array.set(U32, next_minima, label, C.minimum(old, index))
 894                canonical_minima(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next_labels, updated)
 895              )},
 896              Array.get(U32, minima, label))
 897          )},
 898          Array.get(U32, labels, index))
 900  law canonicalize:
 901    for labels: Array<U32>
 902    for +size: U32
 903    Array<U32>
 905  def canonicalize(labels, size):
 906    C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Array<U32>,
 907      \{Tuple: next => minima => (
 908        C.labels_compose(C.to_nat(size), 0, next, minima)
 909      )},
 910      canonical_minima(C.to_nat(size), 0, labels,
 911      C.array_new(size, size)))
 913  law level_refine:
 914    for graph: Graph
 915    for parents: Array<U32>
 916    for +size: U32
 917    for +original_size: U32
 918    for resolution: U32
 919    for seed: U32
 920    for mapping: Array<U32>
 921    for continue: U32 -> U32 -> Graph -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32> -> Result<String, Array<U32>>
 922    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 924  def level_refine(graph, parents, size, original_size, resolution, seed, mapping, continue):
 925    C.apply(Graph & Graph, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 926      \{Tuple: g1 => g2 => (
 927        C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 928          \{Tuple: p1 => p2 => (
 929            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 930              \{Tuple: refined => next_seed => (
 931                C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 932                  \{Tuple: compacted => raw_size => (
 933                    C.apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 934                      \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
 935                        +new_size = {raw_size : U32}
 936                        C.apply(Bool, Result<String, Array<U32>>, \{
 937                            True: (
 938                              Fail{"Leiden refinement did not contract; try another seed"}
 939                            )
 940                            False: (
 941                              C.apply(Graph & Array<U32>, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 942                                \{Tuple: next_graph => next_parents => (
 943                                  next_mapping = C.labels_compose(C.to_nat(original_size), 0, mapping, r2)
 944                                  continue(new_size, next_seed, next_graph, next_parents, next_mapping)
 945                                )},
 946                                aggregate(g2, r1, p2, new_size))
 947                            )
 948                        }, U32.is_ge(new_size, size))
 949                      )},
 950                      Array.clone(U32, compacted))
 951                  )},
 952                  compact_labels(refined, size, size))
 953              )},
 954              refine(g1, p1, size, resolution, seed))
 955          )},
 956          Array.clone(U32, parents))
 957      )},
 958      graph_copy(graph))
 960  law level_moved:
 961    for result: (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32
 962    for +size: U32
 963    for +original_size: U32
 964    for resolution: U32
 965    for mapping: Array<U32>
 966    for continue: U32 -> U32 -> Graph -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32> -> Result<String, Array<U32>>
 967    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 969  def level_moved(result, size, original_size, resolution, mapping, continue):
 970    match result:
 971      case Tuple{Tuple{graph, parents}, seed}:
 972        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Result<String, Array<U32>>,
 973          \{Tuple: compacted => count => (
 974            C.branch(Result<String, Array<U32>>, U32.is_eq(count, size), \{
 975              True: (
 976                Done{C.labels_compose(C.to_nat(original_size), 0, mapping, compacted)}
 977              )
 978              False: (
 979                level_refine(graph, compacted, size, original_size, resolution, seed,
 980                  mapping, continue)
 981              )
 982            })
 983          )},
 984          compact_labels(parents, size, size))
 986  law levels:
 987    for fuel: Nat
 988    for +size: U32
 989    for +original_size: U32
 990    for +resolution: U32
 991    for seed: U32
 992    for +passes: Nat
 993    for graph: Graph
 994    for parents: Array<U32>
 995    for mapping: Array<U32>
 996    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 998  def levels(fuel, size, original_size, resolution, seed, passes, graph, parents, mapping):
 999    match fuel:
1000      case Zero{}:
1001        Fail{"Leiden aggregation exhausted its structural bound"}
1002      case Succ{rest}:
1003        do Result<&1, &1, String, Array<U32>>:
1004          result: (Graph & Array<U32>) & U32 <- move_nodes(passes, size,
1005            resolution, seed, graph, parents)
1006          level_moved(result, size, original_size, resolution, mapping,
1007            n => s => g => p => m => levels(rest, n, original_size,
1008              resolution, s, passes, g, p, m))
1010  law graph_unweight_row:
1011    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
1012    C.Neighbors
1014  def graph_unweight_row(neighbors):
1015    match neighbors:
1016      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
1017        C.NeighborsNil{}
1018      case C.NeighborsCon{target, score, tail}:
1019        C.NeighborsCon{target, 1, graph_unweight_row(tail)}
1021  law graph_unweight:
1022    for fuel: Nat
1023    for +index: U32
1024    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
1025    Array<C.Neighbors>
1027  def graph_unweight(fuel, index, rows):
1028    match fuel:
1029      case Zero{}:
1030        rows
1031      case Succ{rest}:
1032        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Array<C.Neighbors>,
1033          \{Tuple: next => neighbors => (
1034            updated = Array.set(C.Neighbors, next, index,
1035              graph_unweight_row(neighbors))
1036            graph_unweight(rest, U32.add(index, 1), updated)
1037          )},
1038          Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, index))
1040  law cluster:
1041    for sparse: C.SparseGraph
1042    for +size: U32
1043    for threshold: U32
1044    for resolution: U32
1045    for seed: U32
1046    for passes: Nat
1047    Result<String, Array<U32>>
1049  def cluster(sparse, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes):
1050    match sparse:
1051      case C.SparseGraph{raw_size, rows}:
1052        graph = {Graph{size, graph_unweight(C.to_nat(size), 0, rows),
1053          C.array_new(size, 1)} : Graph}
1054        do Result<&1, &1, String, Array<U32>>:
1055          labels: Array<U32> <- levels(Succ{C.to_nat(size)}, size, size, resolution,
1056            seed, passes, graph, C.labels_identity(size), C.labels_identity(size))
1057          return canonicalize(labels, size)
1059  law run_dataset:
1060    for dataset: C.Dataset
1061    for +threshold: U32
1062    for reverse: Bool
1063    for fast: Bool
1064    for lz: Bool
1065    for kmer_minimum: U32
1066    for kmer_identity: U32
1067    for resolution: U32
1068    for seed: U32
1069    for passes: Nat
1070    IO(Unit)
1072  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
1073                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
1074    match dataset:
1075      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
1076        +size = {raw_size : U32}
1077        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
1078          \{Tuple: input => output => (
1079            graph = C.branch(C.SparseGraph, lz, \{
1080              True: LZG.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, kmer_minimum,
1081                kmer_identity)
1082              False: V.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast)
1083            })
1084            do IO<Unit>:
1085              labels: Array<U32> <- C.result_unwrap(Array<U32>,
1086                cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes))
1087              C.output(output, labels)
1088          )},
1089          C.records_copy(items))
1091  law run_config:
1092    for config: C.Config
1093    IO(Unit)
1095  def run_config(config):
1096    match config:
1097      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
1098                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
1099        do IO<Unit>:
1100          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
1101          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
1102            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
1104  law main:
1105    IO(Unit)
1107  def main():
1108    do IO<Unit>:
1109      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
1110      run_config(config)

   6  def apply(-A: Type, -B: Type, body: A -> B, value: A) -> B:
   7    body(value)

  10    body(condition)

  49      )
  50    })

 105  law array_depth:
 106    for fuel: Nat
 107    for +size: U32
 108    for +capacity: U32
 109    for depth: Nat
 110    Nat
 112  def array_depth(fuel, size, capacity, depth):
 113    match fuel:
 114      case Zero{}:
 115        depth
 116      case Succ{rest}:
 117        branch(Nat, U32.is_le(size, capacity), \{
 118          True: (
 119            depth
 120          )
 121          False: (
 122            array_depth(rest, size, U32.mul(capacity, 2), Succ{depth})
 123          )
 124        })
 126  law array_new:
 127    for size: U32
 128    for value: U32
 129    Array<U32>
 131  def array_new(size, value):
 132    Array.new(U32, array_depth(31n, size, 1, 0n), value)
 134  law array_identity:
 135    for fuel: Nat
 136    for +index: U32
 137    for values: Array<U32>
 138    Array<U32>
 140  def array_identity(fuel, index, values):
 141    match fuel:
 142      case Zero{}:
 143        values
 144      case Succ{rest}:
 145        next = Array.set(U32, values, index, index)
 146        array_identity(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next)
 148  law labels_identity:

 153    array_identity(to_nat(size), 0, array_new(size, 0))
 155  law list_reverse:

 162    match values:
 163      case Nil{}:
 164        result
 165      case Con{head, tail}:
 166        list_reverse(A, tail, Con{head, result})

 192  law string_copy:

 197    (text, text)
 199  def record_copy(item: Record) -> Record & Record:
 200    match item:
 201      case Record{+index, +label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
 202        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, Record & Record,
 203          \{Tuple: left_bases => right_bases => (
 204            apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Record & Record,
 205              \{Tuple: left_words => right_words => (
 206                apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Record & Record,
 207                  \{Tuple: left_sketch => right_sketch => (
 208                    (Record{index, label, left_bases, width, left_words, left_sketch},
 209                     Record{index, label, right_bases, width, right_words, right_sketch})
 210                  )},
 211                  Array.clone(U32, sketch))
 212              )},
 213              Array.clone(U32, words))
 214          )},
 215          P.copy(bases))
 217  law records_copy_loop:
 218    for items: List<Record>
 219    for left: List<Record>
 220    for right: List<Record>
 221    List<Record> & List<Record>
 223  def records_copy_loop(items, left, right):
 224    match items:
 225      case Nil{}:
 226        (list_reverse(Record, left, Nil{}), list_reverse(Record, right, Nil{}))
 227      case Con{head, tail}:
 228        apply(Record & Record, List<Record> & List<Record>,
 229          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
 230            records_copy_loop(tail, Con{h1, left}, Con{h2, right})
 231          )},
 232          record_copy(head))
 234  law records_copy:
 235    for items: List<Record>
 236    List<Record> & List<Record>
 238  def records_copy(items):
 239    records_copy_loop(items, Nil{}, Nil{})
 241  law record_precedes:
 242    for left: Record
 243    for right: Record
 244    Bool
 246  def record_precedes(left, right):
 247    match left right:
 248      case Record{li, ll, lb, lw, lwords, lsketch} Record{ri, rl, rb, rw, rwords, rsketch}:
 249        +a = {lw : U32}
 250        +b = {rw : U32}
 251        U32.is_gt(a, b) || (U32.is_eq(a, b) && U32.is_lt(li, ri))
 253  type RecordRuns is Type:
 254    RecordRunsNil{}
 255    RecordRunsCon{run: List<Record>, rest: RecordRuns}
 257  law records_with_length:
 258    for items: List<Record>
 259    List<Record> & Nat
 261  def records_with_length(items):
 262    match items:
 263      case Nil{}:
 264        (Nil{}, 0n)
 265      case Con{head, tail}:
 266        apply(List<Record> & Nat, List<Record> & Nat,
 267          \{Tuple: rest => length => (
 268            (Con{head, rest}, Succ{length})
 269          )},
 270          records_with_length(tail))
 272  law records_merge_loop:
 273    for fuel: Nat
 274    for left: List<Record>
 275    for right: List<Record>
 276    List<Record>
 278  def records_merge_loop(fuel, left, right):
 279    match fuel:
 280      case Zero{}:
 281        match left:
 282          case Nil{}:
 283            right
 284          case Con{head, tail}:
 285            Con{head, tail}
 286      case Succ{rest}:
 287        match left right:
 288          case Nil{} remaining:
 289            remaining
 290          case Con{head, tail} Nil{}:
 291            Con{head, tail}
 292          case Con{left_head, left_tail} Con{right_head, right_tail}:
 293            apply(Record & Record, List<Record>,
 294              \{Tuple: left_test => left_value => (
 295                apply(Record & Record, List<Record>,
 296                  \{Tuple: right_test => right_value => (
 297                    branch(List<Record>, record_precedes(left_test, right_test), \{
 298                      True: (
 299                        Con{left_value,
 300                          records_merge_loop(rest, left_tail, Con{right_value, right_tail})}
 301                      )
 302                      False: (
 303                        Con{right_value,
 304                          records_merge_loop(rest, Con{left_value, left_tail}, right_tail)}
 305                      )
 306                    })
 307                  )},
 308                  record_copy(right_head))
 309              )},
 310              record_copy(left_head))
 312  law records_merge_ready:
 313    for left: List<Record> & Nat
 314    for right: List<Record> & Nat
 315    List<Record>
 317  def records_merge_ready(left, right):
 318    match left right:
 319      case Tuple{left_items, left_length} Tuple{right_items, right_length}:
 320        records_merge_loop(Nat.add(left_length, right_length), left_items, right_items)
 322  law records_merge:
 323    for left: List<Record>
 324    for right: List<Record>
 325    List<Record>
 327  def records_merge(left, right):
 328    records_merge_ready(records_with_length(left), records_with_length(right))
 330  law records_carry:
 331    for runs: RecordRuns
 332    for run: List<Record>
 333    RecordRuns
 335  def records_carry(runs, run):
 336    match runs:
 337      case RecordRunsNil{}:
 338        RecordRunsCon{run, RecordRunsNil{}}
 339      case RecordRunsCon{Nil{}, rest}:
 340        RecordRunsCon{run, rest}
 341      case RecordRunsCon{Con{head, tail}, rest}:
 342        RecordRunsCon{Nil{}, records_carry(rest, records_merge(Con{head, tail}, run))}
 344  law records_to_runs:
 345    for items: List<Record>
 346    RecordRuns
 348  def records_to_runs(items):
 349    match items:
 350      case Nil{}:
 351        RecordRunsNil{}
 352      case Con{head, tail}:
 353        records_carry(records_to_runs(tail), Con{head, Nil{}})
 355  law records_collapse:
 356    for runs: RecordRuns
 357    List<Record>
 359  def records_collapse(runs):
 360    match runs:
 361      case RecordRunsNil{}:
 362        Nil{}
 363      case RecordRunsCon{run, rest}:
 364        records_merge(run, records_collapse(rest))
 366  law records_sort:
 367    for items: List<Record>
 368    List<Record>
 370  def records_sort(items):
 371    records_collapse(records_to_runs(items))
