 464  law reverse_complement:
 465    for text: String
 466    for result: String
 467    String
 469  def reverse_complement(text, result):
 470    match text:
 471      case SNil{}:
 472        result
 473      case SCon{Chr{code}, tail}:
 474        head = {Chr{complement(code)} : Char}
 475        reverse_complement(tail, SCon{head, result})
 477  type AlignmentCell is Data:
 478    AlignmentCell{edits: U32, matches: U32}
 480  law alignment_minimum:
 481    for left: AlignmentCell
 482    for right: AlignmentCell
 483    AlignmentCell
 485  def alignment_minimum(left, right):
 486    match left right:
 487      case AlignmentCell{+left_edits, +left_matches} AlignmentCell{+right_edits, +right_matches}:
 488        branch(AlignmentCell,
 489          U32.is_lt(left_edits, right_edits) ||
 490          (U32.is_eq(left_edits, right_edits) && U32.is_ge(left_matches, right_matches)), \{
 491          True: (
 492            AlignmentCell{left_edits, left_matches}
 493          )
 494          False: (
 495            AlignmentCell{right_edits, right_matches}
 496          )
 497        })
 499  law alignment_indel:
 500    for cell: AlignmentCell
 501    AlignmentCell
 503  def alignment_indel(cell):
 504    match cell:
 505      case AlignmentCell{edits, matches}:
 506        AlignmentCell{U32.add(edits, 1), matches}
 508  law alignment_initial:
 509    for fuel: Nat
 510    for +index: U32
 511    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 512    Array<AlignmentCell>
 514  def alignment_initial(fuel, index, row):
 515    match fuel:
 516      case Zero{}:
 517        row
 518      case Succ{rest}:
 519        next = Array.set(AlignmentCell, row, index, AlignmentCell{index, 0})
 520        alignment_initial(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next)
 522  law alignment_diagonal:
 523    for base: U32
 524    for code: U32
 525    for diagonal: AlignmentCell
 526    AlignmentCell
 528  def alignment_diagonal(base, code, diagonal):
 529    match diagonal:
 530      case AlignmentCell{edits, matches}:
 531        branch(AlignmentCell, U32.is_eq(base, code), \{
 532          True: (
 533            AlignmentCell{edits, U32.add(matches, 1)}
 534          )
 535          False: (
 536            AlignmentCell{U32.add(edits, 1), matches}
 537          )
 538        })
 540  law alignment_columns:
 541    for text: String
 542    for +base: U32
 543    for +index: U32
 544    for diagonal: AlignmentCell
 545    for left: AlignmentCell
 546    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 547    Array<AlignmentCell>
 549  def alignment_columns(text, base, index, diagonal, left, row):
 550    match text:
 551      case SNil{}:
 552        row
 553      case SCon{Chr{code}, tail}:
 554        apply(Array<AlignmentCell> & AlignmentCell, Array<AlignmentCell>,
 555          \{Tuple: next => above_raw => (
 556            +above = {above_raw : AlignmentCell}
 557            diagonal_cost = alignment_diagonal(base, code, diagonal)
 558            +cell = {alignment_minimum(diagonal_cost,
 559              alignment_indel(alignment_minimum(left, above))) : AlignmentCell}
 560            updated = Array.set(AlignmentCell, next, index, cell)
 561            alignment_columns(tail, base, U32.add(index, 1), above, cell, updated)
 562          )},
 563          Array.get(AlignmentCell, row, index))
 565  law alignment_rows:
 566    for text: String
 567    for other: String
 568    for +index: U32
 569    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 570    Array<AlignmentCell>
 572  def alignment_rows(text, other, index, row):
 573    match text:
 574      case SNil{}:
 575        row
 576      case SCon{Chr{code}, tail}:
 577        apply(String & String, Array<AlignmentCell>,
 578          \{Tuple: o1 => o2 => (
 579            +row_index = {index : U32}
 580            +left = {AlignmentCell{row_index, 0} : AlignmentCell}
 581            initialized = Array.set(AlignmentCell, row, 0, left)
 582            next = alignment_columns(o1, code, 1,
 583              AlignmentCell{U32.sub(row_index, 1), 0}, left, initialized)
 584            alignment_rows(tail, o2, U32.add(index, 1), next)
 585          )},
 586          string_copy(other))
 588  law alignment_text:
 589    for left: String
 590    for right: String
 591    for +left_size: U32
 592    for +right_size: U32
 593    for shorter: Bool
 594    U32
 596  law alignment_score:
 597    for cell: AlignmentCell
 598    for +left_size: U32
 599    for +right_size: U32
 600    for shorter: Bool
 601    U32
 603  def alignment_score(cell, left_size, right_size, shorter):
 604    match cell:
 605      case AlignmentCell{edits, +matches}:
 606        branch(U32, shorter, \{
 607          True: (
 608            U32.div(U32.mul(matches, 10000), minimum(left_size, right_size))
 609          )
 610          False: (
 611            U32.div(U32.mul(matches, 10000), U32.add(matches, edits))
 612          )
 613        })
 615  def alignment_text(left, right, left_size, right_size, shorter):
 616    row = alignment_initial(to_nat(U32.add(right_size, 1)), 0,
 617      Array.new(AlignmentCell, array_depth(31n, U32.add(right_size, 1), 1, 0n),
 618        AlignmentCell{0, 0}))
 619    result = alignment_rows(left, right, 1, row)
 620    apply(Array<AlignmentCell> & AlignmentCell, U32,
 621      \{Tuple: unused => cell => (
 622        alignment_score(cell, left_size, right_size, shorter)
 623      )},
 624      Array.get(AlignmentCell, result, right_size))
 634  law alignment_edit_limit:
 635    for +left_size: U32
 636    for +right_size: U32
 637    for +threshold: U32
 638    for shorter: Bool
 639    U32
 641  def alignment_edit_limit(left_size, right_size, threshold, shorter):
 642    +small = {minimum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
 643    +large = {maximum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
 644    branch(U32, shorter, \{
 645      True: (
 646        +required = {U32.div(U32.add(U32.mul(threshold, small), 9999), 10000)
 647          : U32}
 648        U32.add(U32.sub(large, small), U32.mul(subtract(small, required), 2))
 649      )
 650      False: (
 651        minimum(large,
 652          U32.div(U32.mul(small, U32.sub(10000, threshold)), threshold))
 653      )
 654    })
 656  law alignment_bounded_indel:
 657    for cell: AlignmentCell
 658    for +sentinel: U32
 659    AlignmentCell
 661  def alignment_bounded_indel(cell, sentinel):
 662    match cell:
 663      case AlignmentCell{+edits, +matches}:
 664        branch(AlignmentCell, U32.is_ge(edits, sentinel), \{
 665          True: AlignmentCell{sentinel, 0}
 666          False: AlignmentCell{U32.add(edits, 1), matches}
 667        })
 669  law alignment_bounded_diagonal:
 670    for +base: U32
 671    for +code: U32
 672    for cell: AlignmentCell
 673    for +sentinel: U32
 674    AlignmentCell
 676  def alignment_bounded_diagonal(base, code, cell, sentinel):
 677    match cell:
 678      case AlignmentCell{+edits, +matches}:
 679        branch(AlignmentCell, U32.is_ge(edits, sentinel), \{
 680          True: AlignmentCell{sentinel, 0}
 681          False: (
 682            branch(AlignmentCell, U32.is_eq(base, code), \{
 683              True: AlignmentCell{edits, U32.add(matches, 1)}
 684              False: AlignmentCell{U32.add(edits, 1), matches}
 685            })
 686          )
 687        })
 689  law alignment_banded_initial:
 690    for fuel: Nat
 691    for +index: U32
 692    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 693    Array<AlignmentCell>
 695  def alignment_banded_initial(fuel, index, row):
 696    match fuel:
 697      case Zero{}:
 698        row
 699      case Succ{rest}:
 700        alignment_banded_initial(rest, U32.add(index, 1),
 701          Array.set(AlignmentCell, row, index, AlignmentCell{index, 0}))
 703  law alignment_banded_columns:
 704    for fuel: Nat
 705    for cursor: P.Cursor
 706    for +base: U32
 707    for +index: U32
 708    for diagonal: AlignmentCell
 709    for left: AlignmentCell
 710    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 711    for +sentinel: U32
 712    P.Cursor & Array<AlignmentCell>
 714  def alignment_banded_columns(fuel, cursor, base, index, diagonal, left, row,
 715                               sentinel):
 716    match fuel:
 717      case Zero{}:
 718        (cursor, row)
 719      case Succ{rest}:
 720        apply(P.Cursor & U32, P.Cursor & Array<AlignmentCell>,
 721          \{Tuple: following => +code => (
 722            apply(Array<AlignmentCell> & AlignmentCell,
 723              P.Cursor & Array<AlignmentCell>,
 724              \{Tuple: next => +above => (
 725                diagonal_cost = alignment_bounded_diagonal(base, code, diagonal,
 726                  sentinel)
 727                +cell = {alignment_minimum(diagonal_cost,
 728                  alignment_bounded_indel(alignment_minimum(left, above), sentinel))
 729                  : AlignmentCell}
 730                alignment_banded_columns(rest, following, base,
 731                  U32.add(index, 1), above, cell,
 732                  Array.set(AlignmentCell, next, index, cell), sentinel)
 733              )},
 734              Array.get(AlignmentCell, row, index))
 735          )},
 736          P.next(cursor))
 738  law alignment_banded_rows:
 739    for fuel: Nat
 740    for left: P.Cursor
 741    for right: Array<U32>
 742    for +right_bits: U32
 743    for +right_size: U32
 744    for +index: U32
 745    for row: Array<AlignmentCell>
 746    for +limit: U32
 747    for +sentinel: U32
 748    Array<AlignmentCell>
 750  def alignment_banded_rows(fuel, left, right, right_bits, right_size, index,
 751                            row, limit, sentinel):
 752    match fuel:
 753      case Zero{}:
 754        row
 755      case Succ{rest}:
 756        apply(P.Cursor & U32, Array<AlignmentCell>, \{Tuple: next_left => +base => (
 757          +low = {maximum(1, subtract(index, limit)) : U32}
 758          +high = {minimum(right_size, U32.add(index, limit)) : U32}
 759          +boundary = {U32.sub(low, 1) : U32}
 760          apply(Array<AlignmentCell> & AlignmentCell, Array<AlignmentCell>,
 761            \{Tuple: before => +diagonal => (
 762              +left_cell = {branch(AlignmentCell, U32.is_eq(low, 1), \{
 763                True: AlignmentCell{index, 0}
 764                False: AlignmentCell{sentinel, 0}
 765              }) : AlignmentCell}
 766              prepared = Array.set(AlignmentCell, before, boundary, left_cell)
 767              apply(P.Cursor & Array<AlignmentCell>, Array<AlignmentCell>,
 768                \{Tuple: after_cursor => after_columns => (
 769                  next_right = P.cursor_words(after_cursor)
 770                  +past = {U32.add(high, 1) : U32}
 771                  bounded = branch(Array<AlignmentCell>,
 772                    U32.is_le(past, right_size), \{
 773                      True: Array.set(AlignmentCell, after_columns, past,
 774                        AlignmentCell{sentinel, 0})
 775                      False: after_columns
 776                    })
 777                  alignment_banded_rows(rest, next_left, next_right, right_bits,
 778                    right_size, U32.add(index, 1), bounded, limit, sentinel)
 779                )},
 780                alignment_banded_columns(
 781                  to_nat(U32.add(U32.sub(high, low), 1)),
 782                  P.cursor_at(right, right_bits, boundary),
 783                  base, low, diagonal, left_cell, prepared, sentinel))
 784            )},
 785            Array.get(AlignmentCell, row, boundary))
 786        )}, P.next(left))

 789    for left: P.Sequence
 790    for right: P.Sequence

 798    for left: P.Sequence
 799    for right: P.Sequence
 800    for +left_size: U32
 801    for +right_size: U32
 802    for shorter: Bool
 803    for +limit: U32
 804    for +sentinel: U32

 807  law alignment_banded_score:
 808    for cell: AlignmentCell
 809    for +left_size: U32
 810    for +right_size: U32
 811    for shorter: Bool
 812    for +limit: U32
 813    U32
 815  def alignment_banded_score(cell, left_size, right_size, shorter, limit):
 816    match cell:
 817      case AlignmentCell{+edits, +matches}:
 818        branch(U32, U32.is_gt(edits, limit), \{
 819          True: 0
 820          False: alignment_score(AlignmentCell{edits, matches},
 821            left_size, right_size, shorter)
 822        })
 824  def alignment_banded_run(left, right, left_size, right_size, shorter, limit,
 825                           sentinel):
 826    match left right:
 827      case P.Sequence{left_words, left_bits, left_length}
 828           P.Sequence{right_words, right_bits, right_length}:
 829        +width = {U32.add(right_size, 1) : U32}
 830        row = alignment_banded_initial(
 831          to_nat(U32.add(minimum(right_size, limit), 1)), 0,
 832          Array.new(AlignmentCell, array_depth(31n, width, 1, 0n),
 833            AlignmentCell{sentinel, 0}))
 834        result = alignment_banded_rows(to_nat(left_size),
 835          P.Cursor{left_words, left_bits, 0, 0, 0}, right_words, right_bits,
 836          right_size, 1, row, limit, sentinel)
 837        apply(Array<AlignmentCell> & AlignmentCell, U32,
 838          \{Tuple: unused => cell => (
 839            alignment_banded_score(cell, left_size, right_size, shorter, limit)
 840          )},
 841          Array.get(AlignmentCell, result, right_size))
 843  def alignment_banded(left, right, left_size, right_size, shorter, threshold):
 844    +limit = {alignment_edit_limit(left_size, right_size, threshold, shorter) : U32}
 845    +sentinel = {U32.add(limit, 1) : U32}
 846    branch(U32, U32.is_gt(subtract(maximum(left_size, right_size),
 847      minimum(left_size, right_size)), limit), \{
 848      True: 0
 849      False: (
 850        alignment_banded_run(left, right, left_size, right_size, shorter, limit,
 851          sentinel)
 852      )
 853    })
 855  def sequence_copy(sequence: P.Sequence) -> P.Sequence & P.Sequence:
 856    P.copy(sequence)
 858  def sequence_reverse(sequence: P.Sequence, +length: U32) -> P.Sequence:
 859    P.pack(reverse_complement(P.unpack(sequence), ""), length)
 861  def sequence_text(sequence: P.Sequence) -> String:
 862    P.unpack(sequence)
 864  def alignment_identity(left: P.Sequence, right: P.Sequence, left_size: U32,
 865                         right_size: U32, shorter: Bool) -> U32:
 866    alignment_text(P.unpack(left), P.unpack(right), left_size, right_size, shorter)
 868  law alignment_identity_threshold:
 869    for left: P.Sequence
 870    for right: P.Sequence
 871    for +left_size: U32
 872    for +right_size: U32
 873    for shorter: Bool
 874    for +threshold: U32
 875    U32

 878                                   threshold):
 879    alignment_banded(left, right, left_size, right_size, shorter, threshold)
 881  law similarity_both:
 882    for left: P.Sequence
 883    for right: P.Sequence
 884    for +ln: U32
 885    for +rn: U32
 886    for +shorter: Bool
 887    U32
 889  def similarity_both(left, right, ln, rn, shorter):
 890    apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 891      \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
 892        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
 893          \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
 894            forward = alignment_identity(l1, r1, ln, rn, shorter)
 895            backward = alignment_identity(l2, sequence_reverse(r2, rn),
 896              ln, rn, shorter)
 897            maximum(forward, backward)
 898          )},
 899          sequence_copy(right))
 900      )},
 901      sequence_copy(left))

 904    for left: Record
 905    for right: Record
 906    for reverse: Bool
 907    for shorter: Bool
 908    U32
 910  def similarity(left, right, reverse, shorter):
 911    match left right:
 912      case Record{li, ll, ls, lw, lwords, lsketch} Record{ri, rl, rs, rw, rwords, rsketch}:
 913        branch(U32, reverse, \{
 914          True: (
 915            similarity_both(ls, rs, lw, rw, shorter)
 916          )
 917          False: (
 918            alignment_identity(ls, rs, lw, rw, shorter)
 919          )
 920        })

 935      case Zero{}:
 936        total
 937      case Succ{rest}:
 938        apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 939          \{Tuple: next_left => a => (
 940            apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 941              \{Tuple: next_right => b => (

 943                  U32.add(total, minimum(a, b)))
 944              )},

 946          )},

 949  law sketch_common:
 950    for fuel: Nat
 951    for +index: U32
 952    for left: Array<U32>
 953    for right: Array<U32>
 954    for +total: U32
 955    U32
 957  def sketch_common(fuel, index, left, right, total):
 958    match fuel:
 959      case Zero{}:
 960        total
 961      case Succ{rest}:
 962        apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 963          \{Tuple: next_left => +a => (
 964            apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32,
 965              \{Tuple: next_right => +b => (
 966                present = U32.is_ne(a, 4294967295) && U32.is_ne(b, 4294967295)
 967                same = present && U32.is_eq(a, b)
 968                sketch_common(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next_left, next_right,
 969                  U32.add(total, select(same, 1, 0)))
 970              )},
 971              Array.get(U32, right, index))
 972          )},
 973          Array.get(U32, left, index))

 978    for values: Array<U32>

 984      case Zero{}:
 985        total
 986      case Succ{rest}:
 987        apply(Array<U32> & U32, U32, \{Tuple: next => +value => (
 988          sketch_populated(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next,
 989            U32.add(total, select(U32.is_ne(value, 4294967295), 1, 0)))
 990        )}, Array.get(U32, values, index))
 995  law fast_sketch_candidate:
 996    for left: Array<U32>
 997    for right: Array<U32>
 998    for +left_size: U32
 999    for +right_size: U32
1000    Bool
1002  def fast_sketch_candidate(left, right, left_size, right_size):
1003    branch(Bool, U32.is_lt(minimum(left_size, right_size), 15), \{
1004      True: True{}
1005      False: (
1006        apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Bool, \{Tuple: left_count => left_common => (
1007          apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Bool,
1008            \{Tuple: right_count => right_common => (
1009              +populated = {minimum(
1010                sketch_populated(64n, 0, left_count, 0),
1011                sketch_populated(64n, 0, right_count, 0)) : U32}
1012              +required = {minimum(8, populated) : U32}
1013              U32.is_ge(sketch_common(64n, 0, left_common, right_common, 0),
1014                required)
1015            )},
1016            Array.clone(U32, right))
1017        )},
1018        Array.clone(U32, left))
1019      )
1020    })

1057  law words_candidate:
1058    for left: Array<U32>
1059    for right: Array<U32>
1060    for +left_size: U32
1061    for +right_size: U32
1062    for +required_matches: U32

1065  def words_candidate(left, right, left_size, right_size, required_matches):
1066    interruptions = subtract(U32.add(left_size, right_size),
1067      U32.mul(required_matches, 2))
1068    +required_words = {subtract(required_matches,
1069      U32.mul(2, U32.add(interruptions, 1))) : U32}
1070    branch(Bool, U32.is_eq(required_words, 0), \{
1071      True: (
1072        True{}
1073      )
1074      False: (
1075        U32.is_ge(words_common(256n, 0, left, right, 0), required_words)
1076      )
1077    })
1079  law global_word_filter:
1080    for left: Array<U32>

1084    for +threshold: U32

1087  def global_word_filter(left, right, left_size, right_size, threshold):
1088    +longer = {maximum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
1089    +shorter = {minimum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
1090    +required_matches = {U32.div(
1091      U32.add(U32.mul(threshold, longer), 9999), 10000) : U32}
1092    branch(Bool, U32.is_gt(required_matches, shorter), \{
1093      True: (
1094        False{}
1095      )
1096      False: (
1097        words_candidate(left, right, left_size, right_size, required_matches)
1098      )
1099    })

1109    for +right_size: U32

1113  def q15_candidate(left, right, left_size, right_size, threshold):
1114    +shorter = {minimum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
1115    +max_edits = {U32.div(U32.mul(shorter, U32.sub(10000, threshold)), threshold)
1116      : U32}
1117    +required_words = {subtract(subtract(shorter, 14), U32.mul(15, max_edits))
1118      : U32}
1119    branch(Bool, U32.is_eq(required_words, 0), \{
1120      True: (
1121        True{}
1122      )
1123      False: (
1124        a = P.signature15(left, left_size)
1125        b = P.signature15(right, right_size)
1126        U32.is_ge(words_common(65536n, 0, a, b, 0), required_words)
1127      )
1128    })

1189  def filtered_alignment_mode(left, right, left_words, right_words,
1190                              left_sketch, right_sketch, left_size, right_size,
1191                              threshold, fast):
1192    branch(U32, global_word_filter(left_words, right_words,
1193      left_size, right_size, threshold), \{
1194      True: (
1195        branch(U32, fast, \{
1196          True: (
1197            branch(U32, U32.is_lt(minimum(left_size, right_size), 15), \{
1198              True: alignment_identity_threshold(left, right, left_size,
1199                right_size, False{}, threshold)
1200              False: fast_sketch_score(left_sketch, right_sketch, threshold)
1201            })
1202          )
1203          False: alignment_identity_threshold(left, right, left_size, right_size,
1204            False{}, threshold)
1205        })
1206      )
1207      False: 0
1208    })
1210  law filtered_both:
1211    for left: P.Sequence
1212    for right: P.Sequence
1213    for left_words: Array<U32>
1214    for right_words: Array<U32>
1215    for +left_size: U32
1216    for +right_size: U32
1217    for +threshold: U32
1218    U32
1220  def filtered_both(left, right, left_words, right_words,
1221                    left_size, right_size, threshold):
1222    apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1223      \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
1224        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1225          \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
1226            apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, U32,
1227              \{Tuple: lw1 => lw2 => (
1228                reversed = sequence_reverse(r2, right_size)
1229                apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1230                  \{Tuple: reverse_for_words => reverse_for_alignment => (
1231                    reverse_words = P.signature(reverse_for_words, right_size)
1232                    forward = filtered_alignment(l1, r1, lw1, right_words,
1233                      left_size, right_size, threshold)
1234                    backward = filtered_alignment(l2, reverse_for_alignment,
1235                      lw2, reverse_words, left_size, right_size, threshold)
1236                    maximum(forward, backward)
1237                  )},
1238                  P.copy(reversed))
1239              )},
1240              Array.clone(U32, left_words))
1241          )},
1242          sequence_copy(right))
1243      )},
1244      sequence_copy(left))
1246  law filtered_both_mode:
1247    for left: P.Sequence
1248    for right: P.Sequence
1249    for left_words: Array<U32>
1250    for right_words: Array<U32>
1251    for left_sketch: Array<U32>
1252    for right_sketch: Array<U32>
1253    for +left_size: U32
1254    for +right_size: U32
1255    for +threshold: U32
1256    for +fast: Bool
1257    U32
1259  def filtered_both_mode(left, right, left_words, right_words,
1260                         left_sketch, right_sketch, left_size, right_size,
1261                         threshold, fast):
1262    apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1263      \{Tuple: l1 => l2 => (
1264        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1265          \{Tuple: r1 => r2 => (
1266            apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, U32,
1267              \{Tuple: lw1 => lw2 => (
1268                apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, U32,
1269                  \{Tuple: lk1 => lk2 => (
1270                    reversed = sequence_reverse(r2, right_size)
1271                    apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1272                      \{Tuple: reverse_for_words => reverse_remaining => (
1273                        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, U32,
1274                          \{Tuple: reverse_for_sketch => reverse_for_alignment => (
1275                            reverse_words = P.signature(reverse_for_words, right_size)
1276                            reverse_sketch = P.sketch(reverse_for_sketch, right_size)
1277                            forward = filtered_alignment_mode(l1, r1, lw1,
1278                              right_words, lk1, right_sketch, left_size,
1279                              right_size, threshold, fast)
1280                            backward = filtered_alignment_mode(l2,
1281                              reverse_for_alignment, lw2, reverse_words, lk2,
1282                              reverse_sketch, left_size, right_size, threshold,
1283                              fast)
1284                            maximum(forward, backward)
1285                          )},
1286                          P.copy(reverse_remaining))
1287                      )},
1288                      P.copy(reversed))
1289                  )},
1290                  Array.clone(U32, left_sketch))
1291              )},
1292              Array.clone(U32, left_words))
1293          )},
1294          sequence_copy(right))
1295      )},
1296      sequence_copy(left))
1298  law filtered_similarity:
1299    for left: Record
1300    for right: Record
1301    for reverse: Bool
1302    for threshold: U32
1303    U32
1305  def filtered_similarity(left, right, reverse, threshold):
1306    match left right:
1307      case Record{li, ll, ls, lw, lwords, lsketch} Record{ri, rl, rs, rw, rwords, rsketch}:
1308        branch(U32, reverse, \{
1309          True: (
1310            filtered_both(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lw, rw, threshold)
1311          )
1312          False: (
1313            filtered_alignment(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lw, rw, threshold)
1314          )
1315        })
1317  law filtered_similarity_mode:
1318    for left: Record
1319    for right: Record
1320    for reverse: Bool
1321    for +threshold: U32
1322    for +fast: Bool
1323    U32
1325  def filtered_similarity_mode(left, right, reverse, threshold, fast):
1326    match left right:
1327      case Record{li, ll, ls, lw, lwords, lsketch} Record{ri, rl, rs, rw, rwords, rsketch}:
1328        branch(U32, reverse, \{
1329          True: filtered_both_mode(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lsketch, rsketch,
1330            lw, rw, threshold, fast)
1331          False: filtered_alignment_mode(ls, rs, lwords, rwords, lsketch,
1332            rsketch, lw, rw, threshold, fast)
1333        })

1344  law lines_split:
1345    for text: String
1346    for line: String
1347    for lines: List<String>
1348    List<String>
1350  def lines_split(text, line, lines):
1351    match text:
1352      case SNil{}:
1353        list_reverse(String, Con{string_reverse(line, ""), lines}, Nil{})
1354      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1355        +code = {raw : U32}
1356        branch(List<String>, U32.is_eq(code, 10), \{
1357          True: (
1358            lines_split(tail, "", Con{string_reverse(line, ""), lines})
1359          )
1360          False: (
1361            branch(List<String>, U32.is_eq(code, 13), \{
1362              True: (
1363                lines_split(tail, line, lines)
1364              )
1365              False: (
1366                lines_split(tail, SCon{Chr{code}, line}, lines)
1367              )
1368            })
1369          )
1370        })

1377    match text:

1380      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1381        +code = {raw : U32}
1382        branch(String, U32.is_eq(code, 32) || U32.is_eq(code, 9), \{
1383          True: (
1384            trim_left(tail)
1385          )
1386          False: (
1387            SCon{Chr{code}, tail}
1388          )

1391  law header_clean_loop:
1392    for text: String
1393    for result: String
1394    Result<String, String>
1396  def header_clean_loop(text, result):
1397    match text:
1398      case SNil{}:
1399        Done{string_reverse(result, "")}
1400      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1401        +code = {raw : U32}
1402        branch(Result<String, String>, U32.is_eq(code, 9), \{
1403          True: (
1404            header_clean_loop(tail, SCon{' ', result})
1405          )
1406          False: (
1407            branch(Result<String, String>, U32.is_lt(code, 32) || U32.is_eq(code, 127), \{
1408              True: (
1409                Fail{"control character in FASTA header"}
1410              )
1411              False: (
1412                header_clean_loop(tail, SCon{Chr{code}, result})
1413              )
1414            })
1415          )
1416        })

1423    header_clean_loop(text, "")

1433      case SCon{head, tail}:
1434        header_clean(SCon{head, tail})
1436  law uppercase:
1437    for +code: U32
1438    U32
1440  def uppercase(code):
1441    branch(U32, U32.is_ge(code, 97) && U32.is_le(code, 122), \{
1442      True: (
1443        U32.sub(code, 32)
1444      )
1445      False: (
1446        code
1447      )
1448    })
1450  law dna_valid:
1451    for +code: U32
1452    Bool
1454  def dna_valid(code):
1455    U32.is_eq(code, 65) || U32.is_eq(code, 67) ||
1456    U32.is_eq(code, 71) || U32.is_eq(code, 84) ||
1457    U32.is_eq(code, 85) || U32.is_eq(code, 82) ||
1458    U32.is_eq(code, 89) || U32.is_eq(code, 83) ||
1459    U32.is_eq(code, 87) || U32.is_eq(code, 75) ||
1460    U32.is_eq(code, 77) || U32.is_eq(code, 66) ||
1461    U32.is_eq(code, 68) || U32.is_eq(code, 72) ||
1462    U32.is_eq(code, 86) || U32.is_eq(code, 78)
1464  law base_valid:
1465    for +code: U32
1466    for alphabet: U32
1467    Bool
1469  def base_valid(code, alphabet):
1470    branch(Bool, U32.is_eq(alphabet, 1), \{
1471      True: (
1472        dna_valid(code)
1473      )
1474      False: (
1475        (U32.is_ge(code, 65) && U32.is_le(code, 90)) || U32.is_eq(code, 42)
1476      )
1477    })
1479  law base_normalize:
1480    for +code: U32
1481    for alphabet: U32
1482    Char
1484  def base_normalize(code, alphabet):
1485    branch(Char, U32.is_eq(alphabet, 1) && U32.is_eq(code, 85), \{
1486      True: (
1487        'T'
1488      )
1489      False: (
1490        Chr{code}
1491      )
1492    })
1494  law sequence_line:
1495    for text: String
1496    for bases: String
1497    for +width: U32
1498    for +alphabet: U32
1499    Result<String, String & U32>
1501  def sequence_line(text, bases, width, alphabet):
1502    match text:
1503      case SNil{}:
1504        Done{(bases, width)}
1505      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1506        +code = {raw : U32}
1507        branch(Result<String, String & U32>, U32.is_eq(code, 32) || U32.is_eq(code, 9), \{
1508          True: (
1509            sequence_line(tail, bases, width, alphabet)
1510          )
1511          False: (
1512            branch(Result<String, String & U32>, U32.is_ge(width, 300000), \{
1513              True: (
1514                Fail{"sequence exceeds the 300000-residue limit"}
1515              )
1516              False: (
1517                +upper = {uppercase(code) : U32}
1518                branch(Result<String, String & U32>, base_valid(upper, alphabet), \{
1519                  True: (
1520                    base = base_normalize(upper, alphabet)
1521                    sequence_line(tail, SCon{base, bases}, U32.add(width, 1), alphabet)
1522                  )
1523                  False: (
1524                    Fail{"invalid sequence symbol (expected ungapped IUPAC DNA/RNA)"}
1525                  )
1526                })
1527              )
1528            })
1529          )
1530        })
1532  law parser_flush:
1533    for state: Parser
1534    Result<String, Parser>
1536  def parser_flush(state):
1537    match state:
1538      case Parser{items, label, bases, width, count, active}:
1539        +length = {width : U32}
1540        +size = {count : U32}
1541        branch(Result<String, Parser>, active, \{
1542          True: (
1543            branch(Result<String, Parser>, U32.is_eq(length, 0), \{
1544              True: (
1545                Fail{"FASTA record has no sequence"}
1546              )
1547              False: (
1548                branch(Result<String, Parser>, U32.is_ge(size, 16384), \{
1549                  True: (
1550                    Fail{"input exceeds the 16384-record limit"}
1551                  )
1552                  False: (
1553                    packed = P.pack(string_reverse(bases, ""), length)
1554                    apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, Result<String, Parser>,
1555                      \{Tuple: for_words => remaining => (
1556                        apply(P.Sequence & P.Sequence, Result<String, Parser>,
1557                          \{Tuple: for_sketch => for_record => (
1558                            words = P.signature(for_words, length)
1559                            sketch = P.sketch(for_sketch, length)
1560                            item = {Record{size, label, for_record, length, words, sketch}
1561                              : Record}
1562                            Done{Parser{Con{item, items}, "", "", 0,
1563                              U32.add(size, 1), False{}}}
1564                          )},
1565                          P.copy(remaining))
1566                      )},
1567                      P.copy(packed))
1568                  )
1569                })
1570              )
1571            })
1572          )
1573          False: (
1574            Done{Parser{items, label, bases, length, size, False{}}}
1575          )
1576        })

1584    match state:
1585      case Parser{items, old_label, bases, width, count, active}:
1586        Parser{items, label, "", 0, count, True{}}

1598        Parser{items, label, next, length, count, True{}}
1600  law parser_sequence:
1601    for state: Parser
1602    for text: String
1603    for alphabet: U32
1604    Result<String, Parser>
1606  def parser_sequence(state, text, alphabet):
1607    match state:
1608      case Parser{items, label, bases, width, count, active}:
1609        branch(Result<String, Parser>, active, \{
1610          True: (
1611            do Result<&1, &1, String, Parser>:
1612              parsed: String & U32 <- sequence_line(text, bases, width, alphabet)
1613              return parser_sequence_finish(parsed, items, label, count)
1614          )
1615          False: (
1616            Fail{"sequence encountered before a FASTA header"}
1617          )
1618        })
1620  law parser_line:
1621    for line: String
1622    for state: Parser
1623    for alphabet: U32
1624    Result<String, Parser>
1626  def parser_line(line, state, alphabet):
1627    match line:
1628      case SNil{}:
1629        Done{state}
1630      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1631        +code = {raw : U32}
1632        branch(Result<String, Parser>, U32.is_eq(code, 62), \{
1633          True: (
1634            do Result<&1, &1, String, Parser>:
1635              label: String <- header_validate(trim_left(tail))
1636              flushed: Parser <- parser_flush(state)
1637              return parser_header(flushed, label)
1638          )
1639          False: (
1640            branch(Result<String, Parser>, U32.is_eq(code, 59), \{
1641              True: (
1642                Done{state}
1643              )
1644              False: (
1645                parser_sequence(state, SCon{Chr{code}, tail}, alphabet)
1646              )
1647            })
1648          )
1649        })

1657    match value:
1658      case Fail{error}:
1659        Fail{"FASTA line " ++ U32.show(line) ++ ": " ++ error}
1660      case Done{state}:
1661        Done{state}
1663  law parser_lines:
1664    for lines: List<String>
1665    for state: Parser
1666    for +line: U32
1667    for +alphabet: U32
1668    Result<String, Parser>
1670  def parser_lines(lines, state, line, alphabet):
1671    match lines:
1672      case Nil{}:
1673        parser_error(parser_flush(state), line)
1674      case Con{head, tail}:
1675        apply(Result<String, Parser>, Result<String, Parser>, \{
1676          Fail: message => Fail{message}
1677          Done: next => parser_lines(tail, next, U32.add(line, 1), alphabet)
1678        }, parser_error(parser_line(trim_left(head), state, alphabet), line))

1702  law stream_chunk:

1710  def stream_chunk(text, line, state, number, alphabet):
1711    match text:
1712      case SNil{}:
1713        Done{Stream{state, line, number}}
1714      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1715        +code = {raw : U32}

1719              Fail: message => Fail{message}
1720              Done: next => stream_chunk(tail, "", next,
1721                U32.add(number, 1), alphabet)

1723                 state, alphabet), number))
1724          )
1725          False: (
1726            branch(Result<String, Stream>, U32.is_eq(code, 13), \{
1727              True: (
1728                stream_chunk(tail, line, state, number, alphabet)
1729              )
1730              False: (
1731                stream_chunk(tail, SCon{Chr{code}, line}, state, number, alphabet)
1732              )
1733            })
1734          )
1735        })

1747            parser_line(trim_left(string_reverse(line, "")), state, alphabet), number)
1748          flushed: Parser <- parser_error(parser_flush(parsed), number)
1749          return parser_dataset(flushed)

1774  law file_parse_read:
1775    for read: File & Result<(U32 & String), String>
1776    for stream: Stream
1777    for +alphabet: U32
1778    for continue: File -> Stream -> IO(Result<String, Dataset>)
1779    IO(Result<String, Dataset>)
1781  def file_parse_read(read, stream, alphabet, continue):
1782    match read:
1783      case Tuple{handle, result}:
1784        match result:
1785          case Fail{error}:
1786            apply(U32 & String, IO(Result<String, Dataset>),
1787              \{Tuple: code => message => stream_close_error(handle, message)}, error)
1788          case Done{text}:
1789            match text:
1790              case SNil{}:
1791                do IO<Result<String, Dataset>>:
1792                  closed: Unit <- File.close(handle)
1793                  return stream_finish(stream, alphabet)
1794              case SCon{head, tail}:
1795                match stream:
1796                  case Stream{state, line, number}:
1797                    stream_continue(
1798                      stream_chunk(SCon{head, tail}, line, state, number, alphabet),
1799                      handle, continue)
1801  law file_parse:
1802    for fuel: Nat
1803    for file: File
1804    for stream: Stream
1805    for +alphabet: U32
1806    IO(Result<String, Dataset>)
1808  def file_parse(fuel, file, stream, alphabet):
1809    match fuel:
1810      case Zero{}:
1811        stream_close_error(file, "input exceeds 256 MiB")
1812      case Succ{rest}:
1813        do IO<Result<String, Dataset>>:
1814          read: File & Result<(U32 & String), String> <- File.read(file, 65536)
1815          file_parse_read(read, stream, alphabet,
1816            handle => next => file_parse(rest, handle, next, alphabet))

1826        IO.die(A, 2, message)
1827      case Done{value}:
1828        IO.pure(A, value)

1879    for seen: Bool

1882  def decimal_parse(text, value, limit, seen):
1883    match text:
1884      case SNil{}:
1885        branch(Result<String, U32>, seen, \{
1886          True: (
1887            Done{value}
1888          )
1889          False: (
1890            Fail{"empty integer setting"}
1891          )
1892        })
1893      case SCon{Chr{raw}, tail}:
1894        +code = {raw : U32}
1895        branch(Result<String, U32>, U32.is_lt(code, 48) || U32.is_gt(code, 57), \{
1896          True: (
1897            Fail{"settings must contain decimal digits only"}
1898          )
1899          False: (
1900            +digit = {U32.sub(code, 48) : U32}
1901            branch(Result<String, U32>, U32.is_gt(value, U32.div(limit, 10)) || (U32.is_eq(value, U32.div(limit, 10)) && U32.is_gt(digit, U32.mod(limit, 10))), \{
1902              True: (
1903                Fail{"integer setting out of range"}
1904              )
1905              False: (
1906                decimal_parse(tail, U32.add(U32.mul(value, 10), digit), limit, True{})
1907              )
1908            })
1909          )
1910        })
1912  law env_number:
1913    for name: String
1914    for fallback: String
1915    for limit: U32
1916    IO(U32)
1918  def env_number(name, fallback, limit):
1919    do IO<U32>:
1920      text: String <- env(name, fallback)
1921      result_unwrap(U32, decimal_parse(text, 0, limit, False{}))
1923  law config_validate:
1924    for +identity: U32
1925    for +alphabet: U32
1926    for +reverse: U32
1927    for +fast: U32
1928    for +lz: U32
1929    for +kmer_minimum: U32
1930    for +kmer_identity: U32
1931    for +resolution: U32
1932    for seed: U32
1933    for +passes: U32
1934    IO(Config)
1936  def config_validate(identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
1937                      kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
1938    branch(IO(Config), U32.is_eq(identity, 0), \{
1939      True: (
1940        IO.die(Config, 2, "identity must be in 1..10000 basis points")
1941      )
1942      False: (
1943        branch(IO(Config), U32.is_eq(resolution, 0), \{
1944          True: (
1945            IO.die(Config, 2, "Leiden resolution must be positive")
1946          )
1947          False: (
1948            branch(IO(Config), U32.is_eq(passes, 0), \{
1949              True: (
1950                IO.die(Config, 2, "iteration limit must be positive")
1951              )
1952              False: (
1953                branch(IO(Config), U32.is_eq(fast, 1) && U32.is_eq(lz, 1), \{
1954                  True: (
1955                    IO.die(Config, 2,
1956                      "CLUSTER_FAST and CLUSTER_LZ_ANI are mutually exclusive")
1957                  )
1958                  False: (
1959                    branch(IO(Config), U32.is_eq(reverse, 1) && U32.is_eq(alphabet, 0), \{
1960                      True: (
1961                        IO.die(Config, 2,
1962                          "reverse complements require the DNA/RNA alphabet")
1963                      )
1964                      False: (
1965                        IO.pure(Config, Config{identity, alphabet,
1966                          U32.is_eq(reverse, 1), U32.is_eq(fast, 1),
1967                          U32.is_eq(lz, 1), kmer_minimum, kmer_identity,
1968                          resolution, seed, to_nat(passes)})
1969                      )
1970                    })
1971                  )
1972                })
1973              )
1974            })
1975          )
1976        })
1977      )
1978    })
1980  law config_read:
1981    IO(Config)
1983  def config_read():
1984    do IO<Config>:
1985      identity: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_IDENTITY", "9000", 10000)
1986      alphabet: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_DNA", "1", 1)
1987      reverse: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_REVERSE", "0", 1)
1988      fast: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_FAST", "0", 1)
1989      lz: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_LZ_ANI", "0", 1)
1990      kmer_minimum: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_KMER_MINIMUM", "20", 4294967295)
1991      kmer_identity: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_KMER_IDENTITY", "7000", 10000)
1992      resolution: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_RESOLUTION", "5000", 10000)
1993      seed: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_SEED", "1", 4294967295)
1994      passes: U32 <- env_number("CLUSTER_PASSES", "100", 100000)
1995      config_validate(identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
1996        kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)

2007  law sparse_add:
2008    for rows: Array<Neighbors>
2009    for +left: U32
2010    for +right: U32
2011    for +score: U32
2012    Array<Neighbors>
2014  def sparse_add(rows, left, right, score):
2015    apply(Array<Neighbors> & Neighbors, Array<Neighbors>,
2016      \{Tuple: next => left_row => (
2017        first = Array.set(Neighbors, next, left,
2018          NeighborsCon{right, score, left_row})
2019        apply(Array<Neighbors> & Neighbors, Array<Neighbors>,
2020          \{Tuple: updated => right_row => (
2021            Array.set(Neighbors, updated, right,
2022              NeighborsCon{left, score, right_row})
2023          )},
2024          Array.get(Neighbors, first, right))
2025      )},
2026      Array.get(Neighbors, rows, left))

2029    for fuel: Nat
2030    for items: List<Record>
2031    for query: Record
2032    for rows: Array<Neighbors>
2033    for +index: U32
2034    for +threshold: U32
2035    for +reverse: Bool
2036    for +fast: Bool

2039  law sparse_pair:
2040    for head_meta: Record
2041    for head_score: Record
2042    for tail: List<Record>
2043    for query_score: Record
2044    for query_next: Record
2045    for rows: Array<Neighbors>
2046    for +index: U32
2047    for +threshold: U32
2048    for +reverse: Bool
2049    for +fast: Bool
2050    for continue: List<Record> -> Record -> Array<Neighbors> -> Array<Neighbors>
2051    Array<Neighbors>
2053  def sparse_pair(head_meta, head_score, tail, query_score, query_next,
2054                  rows, index, threshold, reverse, fast, continue):
2055    match head_meta query_score:
2056      case Record{+target, head_label, head_bases, +head_size, head_words, head_sketch} Record{query_index, query_label, query_bases, +query_size, query_words, query_sketch}:
2057        +required_matches = {U32.div(
2058          U32.add(U32.mul(threshold, query_size), 9999), 10000) : U32}
2059        branch(Array<Neighbors>, U32.is_gt(required_matches, head_size), \{
2060          True: (
2061            rows
2062          )
2063          False: (
2064            +score = {filtered_similarity_mode(
2065              Record{query_index, query_label, query_bases, query_size,
2066                query_words, query_sketch},
2067              head_score, reverse, threshold, fast) : U32}
2068            updated = branch(Array<Neighbors>, U32.is_ge(score, threshold), \{
2069              True: (
2070                sparse_add(rows, index, target, score)
2071              )
2072              False: (
2073                rows
2074              )
2075            })
2076            continue(tail, query_next, updated)
2077          )
2078        })
2080  def sparse_row(fuel, items, query, rows, index, threshold, reverse, fast):
2081    match fuel items:
2082      case Zero{} remaining:
2083        rows
2084      case Succ{row_rest} Nil{}:
2085        rows
2086      case Succ{row_rest} Con{head, tail}:
2087        apply(Record & Record, Array<Neighbors>,
2088          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
2089            apply(Record & Record, Array<Neighbors>,
2090              \{Tuple: q1 => q2 => (
2091                sparse_pair(h1, h2, tail, q1, q2,
2092                  rows, index, threshold, reverse, fast,
2093                  remaining => next_query => next_rows =>
2094                    sparse_row(row_rest, remaining, next_query, next_rows,
2095                      index, threshold, reverse, fast))
2096              )},
2097              record_copy(query))
2098          )},
2099          record_copy(head))
2101  law sparse_rows:
2102    for fuel: Nat
2103    for items: List<Record>
2104    for rows: Array<Neighbors>
2105    for +threshold: U32
2106    for +reverse: Bool
2107    for +fast: Bool

2110  def sparse_rows(fuel, items, rows, threshold, reverse, fast):
2111    match fuel items:
2112      case Zero{} remaining:
2113        rows
2114      case Succ{+rest} Nil{}:
2115        rows
2116      case Succ{+rest} Con{head, tail}:
2117        apply(Record & Record, Array<Neighbors>,
2118          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
2119            apply(List<Record> & List<Record>, Array<Neighbors>,
2120              \{Tuple: t1 => t2 => (
2121                +index = {record_index(h1) : U32}
2122                next = sparse_row(rest, t1, h2, rows, index, threshold, reverse,
2123                  fast)
2124                sparse_rows(rest, t2, next, threshold, reverse, fast)
2125              )},
2126              records_copy(tail))
2127          )},
2128          record_copy(head))

2251          case Fail{error}:
2252            apply(U32 & String, IO(File),

2254                do IO<File>:
2255                  closed: Unit <- File.close(file)
2256                  IO.die(File, 2, message)

lib/fast_score.bend
3  import Base
   5  def choose(-A: Type, b: Bool, f: Bool -> A) -> A:
   6    f(b)
   8  def minimum(+a: U32, +b: U32) -> U32:
   9    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(a, b), \{True: a; False: b})
  11  law basis_power:
  12    for fuel: Nat
  13    for +value: U32
  14    for +accumulator: U32
  15    U32
  17  def basis_power(fuel, value, accumulator):
  18    match fuel:
  19      case Zero{}:
  20        accumulator
  21      case Succ{rest}:
  22        basis_power(rest, value,
  23          U32.div(U32.mul(accumulator, value), 10000))
  25  law score:
  26    for +common: U32
  27    for +populated: U32
  28    for +threshold: U32
  29    U32
  31  def score(common, populated, threshold):
  32    choose(U32, U32.is_eq(common, 0), \{
  33      True: 0
  34      False: (
  35        +word_identity = {basis_power(15n, threshold, 10000) : U32}
  36        +jaccard = {U32.div(U32.mul(word_identity, 10000),
  37          U32.sub(20000, word_identity)) : U32}
  38        +expected = {U32.div(U32.mul(64, jaccard), 10000) : U32}
  39        +rounded = {U32.div(U32.add(U32.mul(expected, 2), 4), 5) : U32}
  40        +raw_required = {choose(U32, U32.is_gt(1, rounded), \{
  41          True: 1
  42          False: rounded
  43        }) : U32}
  44        +required = {minimum(raw_required, populated) : U32}
  45        choose(U32,
  46          U32.is_eq(populated, 0) || U32.is_lt(common, required), \{
  47            True: 0
  48            False: U32.add(threshold,
  49              U32.div(U32.mul(U32.sub(10000, threshold), common), populated))
  50          })
  51      )
  52    })
  57  law indexed_score:
  58    for +common: U32
  59    for +populated: U32
  60    for +threshold: U32
  61    U32
  63  def indexed_score(common, populated, threshold):
  64    score(common, populated, threshold)
  66  law indexed_score_agrees:
  67    for +common: U32
  68    for +populated: U32
  69    for +threshold: U32
  70    {indexed_score(common, populated, threshold)
  71      == score(common, populated, threshold) : U32}
  73  def indexed_score_agrees(common, populated, threshold):
  74    {==}
  76  law zero_common_is_rejected:
  77    for +populated: U32

  81  def zero_common_is_rejected(populated, threshold):
  82    {==}
lib/kmer_db.bend
8  import Base
   9  import ./common.bend as C
  10  import ./packing.bend as P
  11  import ./kmer_score.bend as KS
  13  type Posting is Data:
  14    PostingNil{}
  15    PostingCon{record: U32, high: U32, low: U32, tail: Posting}
  17  type Candidates is Data:
  18    CandidatesNil{}
  19    CandidatesCon{record: U32, tail: Candidates}
  21  type InsertState is Type:
  22    InsertState{postings: Posting, counts: Array<U32>,
  23      candidates: Candidates, unique: U32}
  25  type ScanState is Type:
  26    ScanState{buckets: Array<Posting>, counts: Array<U32>,
  27      candidates: Candidates, unique: U32}
  29  type EvaluateState is Type:
  30    EvaluateState{kmer_counts: Array<U32>, counts: Array<U32>,
  31      rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  33  type BuildState is Type:
  34    BuildState{buckets: Array<Posting>, kmer_counts: Array<U32>,
  35      counts: Array<U32>, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  37  def same_key(+left_high: U32, +left_low: U32, +right_high: U32,
  38               +right_low: U32) -> Bool:
  39    U32.is_eq(left_high, right_high) && U32.is_eq(left_low, right_low)
  41  def key_less(+left_high: U32, +left_low: U32, +right_high: U32,
  42               +right_low: U32) -> Bool:
  43    U32.is_lt(left_high, right_high)
  44      || (U32.is_eq(left_high, right_high) && U32.is_lt(left_low, right_low))
  46  def key_bucket(+high: U32, +low: U32) -> U32:
  47    U32.and(P.hash30(U32.xor(low, P.hash30(high))), 65535)
  52  law count_shared:
  53    for counts: Array<U32>
  54    for candidates: Candidates
  55    for +record: U32
  56    Array<U32> & Candidates
  58  def count_shared(counts, candidates, record):
  59    C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Candidates,
  60      \{Tuple: next => +previous => (
  61        updated = Array.set(U32, next, record, U32.add(previous, 1))
  62        (updated, C.branch(Candidates, U32.is_eq(previous, 0), \{
  63          True: CandidatesCon{record, candidates}
  64          False: candidates
  65        }))
  66      )}, Array.get(U32, counts, record))
  68  law insert_prepend:
  69    for result: InsertState
  70    for +record: U32
  71    for +high: U32
  72    for +low: U32
  73    InsertState
  75  def insert_prepend(result, record, high, low):
  76    match result:
  77      case InsertState{postings, counts, candidates, unique}:
  78        InsertState{PostingCon{record, high, low, postings}, counts,
  79          candidates, unique}
  85  law insert_posting:
  86    for postings: Posting
  87    for +record: U32
  88    for +high: U32
  89    for +low: U32
  90    for counts: Array<U32>
  91    for candidates: Candidates
  92    for +unique: U32
  93    InsertState
  95  def insert_posting(postings, record, high, low, counts, candidates, unique):
  96    match postings:
  97      case PostingNil{}:
  98        InsertState{PostingCon{record, high, low, PostingNil{}}, counts,
  99          candidates, U32.add(unique, 1)}
 100      case PostingCon{+other, +entry_high, +entry_low, tail}:
 101        +same = {same_key(high, low, entry_high, entry_low) : Bool}
 102        C.branch(InsertState, same && U32.is_eq(record, other), \{
 103          True: InsertState{
 104            PostingCon{other, entry_high, entry_low, tail}, counts,
 105            candidates, unique}
 106          False: C.branch(InsertState, same, \{
 107            False: insert_prepend(
 108              insert_posting(tail, record, high, low, counts, candidates,
 109                unique), other, entry_high, entry_low)
 110            True: C.apply(Array<U32> & Candidates, InsertState,
 111              \{Tuple: next_counts => next_candidates => (
 112                insert_prepend(
 113                  insert_posting(tail, record, high, low, next_counts,
 114                    next_candidates, unique), other, entry_high, entry_low)
 115              )}, count_shared(counts, candidates, other))
 116          })
 117        })

 123    ScanState
 125  def scan_inserted(result, buckets, bucket):

 128        ScanState{Array.set(Posting, buckets, bucket, postings), counts,
 129          candidates, unique}
 131  law scan_insert:
 132    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 133    for counts: Array<U32>
 134    for candidates: Candidates
 135    for +unique: U32
 136    for +record: U32
 137    for +high: U32
 138    for +low: U32
 139    ScanState
 141  def scan_insert(buckets, counts, candidates, unique, record, high, low):
 142    +bucket = {key_bucket(high, low) : U32}
 143    C.apply(Array<Posting> & Posting, ScanState,
 144      \{Tuple: next => postings => (
 145        scan_inserted(insert_posting(postings, record, high, low, counts,
 146          candidates, unique), next, bucket)
 147      )}, Array.get(Posting, buckets, bucket))
 152  law scan_sequence:
 153    for fuel: Nat
 154    for cursor: P.Cursor
 155    for +record: U32
 156    for +forward_high: U32
 157    for +forward_low: U32
 158    for +reverse_high: U32
 159    for +reverse_low: U32
 160    for +valid: U32
 161    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 162    for counts: Array<U32>
 163    for candidates: Candidates
 164    for +unique: U32
 165    ScanState
 167  def scan_sequence(fuel, cursor, record, forward_high, forward_low,
 168                    reverse_high, reverse_low, valid, buckets, counts,
 169                    candidates, unique):
 170    match fuel:
 171      case Zero{}:
 172        ScanState{buckets, counts, candidates, unique}
 173      case Succ{rest}:
 174        C.apply(P.Cursor & U32, ScanState,
 175          \{Tuple: following => +base => (
 176            C.branch(ScanState, U32.is_gt(base, 3), \{
 177              True: scan_sequence(rest, following, record, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
 178                buckets, counts, candidates, unique)
 179              False: (
 180                +next_forward_high = {U32.and(U32.or(
 181                  U32.shln(forward_high, 2n), U32.shrn(forward_low, 30n)),
 182                  262143) : U32}
 183                +next_forward_low = {U32.or(U32.shln(forward_low, 2n), base)
 184                  : U32}
 185                +next_reverse_high = {U32.or(U32.shrn(reverse_high, 2n),
 186                  U32.shln(U32.sub(3, base), 16n)) : U32}
 187                +next_reverse_low = {U32.or(U32.shrn(reverse_low, 2n),
 188                  U32.shln(reverse_high, 30n)) : U32}
 189                +next_valid = {U32.add(valid, 1) : U32}
 190                +forward_is_canonical = {key_less(next_forward_high,
 191                  next_forward_low, next_reverse_high, next_reverse_low) : Bool}
 192                +key_high = {C.branch(U32, forward_is_canonical, \{
 193                  True: next_forward_high
 194                  False: next_reverse_high
 195                }) : U32}
 196                +key_low = {C.branch(U32, forward_is_canonical, \{
 197                  True: next_forward_low
 198                  False: next_reverse_low
 199                }) : U32}
 200                C.branch(ScanState, U32.is_ge(next_valid, 25), \{
 201                  False: scan_sequence(rest, following, record,
 202                    next_forward_high, next_forward_low, next_reverse_high,
 203                    next_reverse_low, next_valid, buckets, counts, candidates,
 204                    unique)
 205                  True: C.apply(ScanState, ScanState,
 206                    \{ScanState: next_buckets => next_counts =>
 207                      next_candidates => next_unique => (
 208                      scan_sequence(rest, following, record, next_forward_high,
 209                        next_forward_low, next_reverse_high, next_reverse_low,
 210                        next_valid, next_buckets, next_counts, next_candidates,
 211                        next_unique)
 212                    )}, scan_insert(buckets, counts, candidates, unique, record,
 213                      key_high, key_low))
 214                })
 215              )
 216            })
 217          )}, P.next(cursor))
 219  law candidate_evaluate:
 220    for candidates: Candidates
 221    for +record: U32
 222    for +record_kmers: U32
 223    for kmer_counts: Array<U32>
 224    for counts: Array<U32>
 225    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 226    for +minimum_common: U32
 227    for +minimum_identity: U32
 228    EvaluateState
 230  def candidate_evaluate(candidates, record, record_kmers, kmer_counts, counts,
 231                         rows, minimum_common, minimum_identity):
 232    match candidates:
 233      case CandidatesNil{}:
 234        EvaluateState{kmer_counts, counts, rows}
 235      case CandidatesCon{+target, tail}:
 236        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, EvaluateState,
 237          \{Tuple: next_counts => +common => (
 238            reset = Array.set(U32, next_counts, target, 0)
 239            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, EvaluateState,
 240              \{Tuple: next_kmer_counts => +target_kmers => (
 241                +accepted = {KS.candidate_accept(common, target_kmers,
 242                  record_kmers, minimum_common, minimum_identity) : Bool}
 243                updated = C.branch(Array<C.Neighbors>, accepted, \{
 244                  True: C.sparse_add(rows, target, record, common)
 245                  False: rows
 246                })
 247                candidate_evaluate(tail, record, record_kmers,
 248                  next_kmer_counts, reset, updated, minimum_common,
 249                  minimum_identity)
 250              )}, Array.get(U32, kmer_counts, target))
 251          )}, Array.get(U32, counts, target))
 253  law build_scanned:
 254    for scanned: ScanState
 255    for +record: U32
 256    for kmer_counts: Array<U32>
 257    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 258    for +minimum_common: U32
 259    for +minimum_identity: U32
 260    for continue: Array<Posting> -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32> ->
 261      Array<C.Neighbors> -> BuildState
 262    BuildState
 264  def build_scanned(scanned, record, kmer_counts, rows, minimum_common,
 265                    minimum_identity, continue):
 266    match scanned:
 267      case ScanState{buckets, counts, candidates, +unique}:
 268        updated_counts = Array.set(U32, kmer_counts, record, unique)
 269        C.apply(EvaluateState, BuildState,
 270          \{EvaluateState: final_kmers => final_counts => final_rows => (
 271            continue(buckets, final_kmers, final_counts, final_rows)
 272          )}, candidate_evaluate(candidates, record, unique, updated_counts,
 273            counts, rows, minimum_common, minimum_identity))
 275  law build_records:
 276    for items: List<C.Record>
 277    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 278    for kmer_counts: Array<U32>
 279    for counts: Array<U32>
 280    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 281    for +minimum_common: U32
 282    for +minimum_identity: U32
 283    BuildState
 285  def build_records(items, buckets, kmer_counts, counts, rows, minimum_common,
 286                    minimum_identity):
 287    match items:
 288      case Nil{}:
 289        BuildState{buckets, kmer_counts, counts, rows}
 290      case Con{head, tail}:
 291        match head:
 292          case C.Record{+index, label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
 293            build_scanned(scan_sequence(C.to_nat(width), P.cursor(bases), index,
 294              0, 0, 0, 0, 0, buckets, counts, CandidatesNil{}, 0), index,
 295              kmer_counts, rows, minimum_common, minimum_identity,
 296              next_buckets => next_kmers => next_counts => next_rows =>
 297                build_records(tail, next_buckets, next_kmers, next_counts,
 298                  next_rows, minimum_common, minimum_identity))
 300  law sparse_graph_built:
 301    for built: BuildState
 302    for +size: U32
 303    C.SparseGraph
 305  def sparse_graph_built(built, size):
 306    match built:
 307      case BuildState{buckets, kmer_counts, counts, rows}:
 308        C.SparseGraph{size, rows}
 310  law sparse_build:
 311    for items: List<C.Record>
 312    for +size: U32
 313    for +minimum_common: U32
 314    for +minimum_identity: U32
 315    C.SparseGraph
 317  def sparse_build(items, size, minimum_common, minimum_identity):
 318    sparse_graph_built(build_records(items,
 319      Array.new(Posting, 16n, PostingNil{}), C.array_new(size, 0),
 320      C.array_new(size, 0),
 321      Array.new(C.Neighbors, C.array_depth(31n, size, 1, 0n),
 322        C.NeighborsNil{}), minimum_common, minimum_identity), size)
lib/kmer_score.bend
2  import Base
   4  def minimum(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
   5    Bool.pick(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), left, right)
   7  def ani_shorter(+common: U32, +left: U32, +right: U32) -> F32:
   8    +denominator = {minimum(left, right) : U32}
   9    Bool.pick(F32, U32.is_eq(common, 0) || U32.is_eq(denominator, 0), 0.0,
  10      F32.sub(1.0, F32.mul(F32.neg(0.04),
  11        F32.log(F32.div(
  12          F32.mul(2.0, F32.div(U32.to_f32(common), U32.to_f32(denominator))),
  13          F32.add(F32.div(U32.to_f32(common), U32.to_f32(denominator)),
  14            1.0))))))
  16  def candidate_accept(+common: U32, +left: U32, +right: U32,
  17                       +minimum_common: U32, +minimum_identity: U32) -> Bool:
  18    U32.is_ge(common, minimum_common)
  19      && F32.is_ge(ani_shorter(common, left, right),
  20        F32.div(U32.to_f32(minimum_identity), 10000.0))
  25  law zero_common_is_rejected:
  26    for +left: U32
  27    for +right: U32
  28    {candidate_accept(0, left, right, 20, 7000) == False{} : Bool}
  30  def zero_common_is_rejected(left, right):
  31    {==}
lib/lz_ani_choice.bend
6  import Base
   8  def choose(-A: Type, condition: Bool, branches: Bool -> A) -> A:
   9    branches(condition)
  11  def probability_cost(+length: U32, +trials: U32) -> F32:
  12    choose(F32, U32.is_ge(length, 27), \{
  13      True: 0.0
  14      False: (
  15        +x = {F32.pow(4.0, F32.neg(U32.to_f32(length))) : F32}
  16        +x2 = {F32.mul(x, x) : F32}
  17        +x3 = {F32.mul(x2, x) : F32}
  18        +series = {F32.add(x,
  19          F32.add(F32.div(x2, 2.0), F32.div(x3, 3.0))) : F32}
  20        F32.mul(U32.to_f32(trials), series)
  21      )
  22    })
  24  law anchor_preferred:
  25    for +anchor_length: U32
  26    for +close_length: U32
  27    for +reference_length: U32
  28    for +literal_run: U32
  29    Bool
  31  def anchor_preferred(anchor_length, close_length, reference_length,
  32                       literal_run):
  33    +anchor_trials = {U32.mul(2,
  34      U32.sub(U32.add(reference_length, 1), anchor_length)) : U32}
  37    +close_trials = {U32.sub(U32.add(literal_run, 41), close_length) : U32}
  38    F32.is_lt(probability_cost(anchor_length, anchor_trials),
  39      probability_cost(close_length, close_trials))

  14  type PackedWords is Data:
  15    PackedWordsNil{}
  16    PackedWordsCon{head: U32, tail: PackedWords}
  18  type PlainRecord is Data:
  19    PlainRecord{index: U32, words: PackedWords, word_count: U32,
  20      bits: U32, width: U32}
  22  type RecordSlot is Data:
  23    RecordSlotEmpty{}
  24    RecordSlotFull{record: PlainRecord}
  26  type PairResult is Type:
  27    PairResult{left: PlainRecord, right: PlainRecord,
  28      left_reference: LZ.ReferenceIndex, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  30  type NeighborResult is Type:
  31    NeighborResult{records: Array<RecordSlot>, left: PlainRecord,
  32      left_reference: LZ.ReferenceIndex, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  34  type RowResult is Type:
  35    RowResult{records: Array<RecordSlot>, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  37  def ratio(+numerator: U32, +denominator: U32) -> U32:
  38    C.branch(U32, U32.is_eq(denominator, 0), \{
  39      True: 0
  40      False: U32.div(U32.mul(numerator, 10000), denominator)
  41    })
  46  law words_from_array:
  47    for fuel: Nat
  48    for +index: U32
  49    for words: Array<U32>
  50    PackedWords
  52  def words_from_array(fuel, index, words):
  53    match fuel:
  54      case Zero{}:
  55        PackedWordsNil{}
  56      case Succ{rest}:
  57        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, PackedWords,
  58          \{Tuple: next => +word => (
  59            PackedWordsCon{word, words_from_array(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next)}
  60          )}, Array.get(U32, words, index))
  62  law words_to_array:
  63    for words: PackedWords
  64    for +index: U32
  65    for values: Array<U32>
  66    Array<U32>
  68  def words_to_array(words, index, values):
  69    match words:
  70      case PackedWordsNil{}:
  71        values
  72      case PackedWordsCon{+head, tail}:
  73        words_to_array(tail, U32.add(index, 1),
  74          Array.set(U32, values, index, head))

  81    match record:
  82      case PlainRecord{+index, +words, +word_count, +bits, +width}:
  83        (PlainRecord{index, words, word_count, bits, width},
  84         P.Sequence{words_to_array(words, 0, Array.new(U32,

  87  law records_fill:
  88    for items: List<C.Record>
  89    for records: Array<RecordSlot>
  90    Array<RecordSlot>
  92  def records_fill(items, records):
  93    match items:
  94      case Nil{}:
  95        records
  96      case Con{head, tail}:
  97        match head:
  98          case C.Record{+index, label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
  99            match bases:
 100              case P.Sequence{packed, +bits, length}:
 101                +word_count = {U32.div(U32.add(U32.mul(width, bits), 31), 32)
 102                  : U32}
 103                records_fill(tail, Array.set(RecordSlot, records, index,
 104                  RecordSlotFull{PlainRecord{index,
 105                    words_from_array(C.to_nat(word_count), 0, packed),
 106                    word_count, bits, width}}))

 120  law pair_second:
 121    for left: PlainRecord
 122    for right: PlainRecord
 123    for first: LZ.ScoreResult
 124    for second: LZ.ScoreResult
 125    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 126    for +threshold: U32
 127    PairResult
 129  def pair_second(left, right, first, second, rows, threshold):
 130    match left right:
 131      case PlainRecord{+left_index, left_words, +left_word_count, +left_bits,
 132        +left_width}
 133        PlainRecord{+right_index, right_words, +right_word_count, +right_bits,
 134          +right_width}:
 135        match first second:
 136          case LZ.ScoreResult{left_reference,
 137            LZ.Metrics{+left_matches, left_mismatches, left_components, left_ani,
 138              left_gani, left_coverage}}
 139            LZ.ScoreResult{right_reference,
 140            LZ.Metrics{+right_matches, right_mismatches, right_components,
 141              right_ani, right_gani, right_coverage}}:
 142            +score = {ratio(U32.add(left_matches, right_matches),
 143              U32.add(left_width, right_width)) : U32}
 144            updated = C.branch(Array<C.Neighbors>,
 145              U32.is_ge(score, threshold), \{
 146              True: C.sparse_add(rows, left_index, right_index, score)
 147              False: rows
 148            })
 149            PairResult{PlainRecord{left_index, left_words, left_word_count,
 150              left_bits, left_width}, PlainRecord{right_index, right_words,
 151              right_word_count, right_bits, right_width}, left_reference,
 152              updated}
 154  law pair_materialized:
 155    for left: PlainRecord
 156    for left_reference: LZ.ReferenceIndex
 157    for right_query: P.Sequence
 158    for left_query: P.Sequence
 159    for right: PlainRecord
 160    for right_for_index: P.Sequence
 161    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 162    for +threshold: U32
 163    PairResult
 165  def pair_materialized(left, left_reference, right_query, left_query, right,
 166                        right_for_index, rows, threshold):
 167    match left right:
 168      case PlainRecord{+left_index, left_words, +left_word_count, +left_bits,
 169        +left_width}
 170        PlainRecord{+right_index, right_words, +right_word_count, +right_bits,
 171          +right_width}:
 172        pair_second(PlainRecord{left_index, left_words, left_word_count,
 173          left_bits, left_width}, PlainRecord{right_index, right_words,
 174          right_word_count, right_bits, right_width},
 175          LZ.score(left_reference, right_query, right_width),
 176          LZ.score(LZ.build_index(right_for_index, right_width), left_query,
 177            left_width), rows, threshold)

 183    for right_query: P.Sequence
 184    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>

 188  def pair_right_copy(left, left_reference, saved_right, right_query,
 189                      rows, threshold):
 190    C.apply(PlainRecord & P.Sequence, PairResult,
 191      \{Tuple: final_left => left_query => (
 192        C.apply(PlainRecord & P.Sequence, PairResult,

 195              left_query, final_right, right_for_index, rows, threshold)
 196          )}, record_sequence(saved_right))
 197      )}, record_sequence(left))

 258  def score_neighbors(neighbors, left_index, records, left, left_reference,
 259                      rows, threshold):
 260    match neighbors:

 262        NeighborResult{records, left, left_reference, rows}
 263      case C.NeighborsCon{+target, score, tail}:
 264        C.branch(NeighborResult, U32.is_gt(target, left_index), \{
 265          False: score_neighbors(tail, left_index, records, left,
 266            left_reference, rows, threshold)
 267          True: C.apply(Array<RecordSlot> & RecordSlot, NeighborResult,
 268            \{Tuple: next => slot => (

 270                \{NeighborResult: final_records => final_left =>
 271                  final_reference => final_rows => (
 272                  score_neighbors(tail, left_index, final_records, final_left,
 273                    final_reference, final_rows, threshold)
 274                )}, score_neighbor(slot, next, left, left_reference, rows,
 275                  target, threshold))
 276            )}, Array.get(RecordSlot, records, target))
 277        })

 374    for +threshold: U32
 375    C.SparseGraph
 377  def score_candidates(candidates, items, size, threshold):
 378    match candidates:
 379      case C.SparseGraph{candidate_size, candidate_rows}:
 380        C.SparseGraph{size, score_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, candidate_rows,
 381          records_table(items, size),
 382          Array.new(C.Neighbors, C.array_depth(31n, size, 1, 0n),
 383            C.NeighborsNil{}), threshold)}
 385  law sparse_build:
 386    for items: List<C.Record>
 387    for +size: U32
 388    for +threshold: U32
 389    for +minimum_common: U32
 390    for +minimum_identity: U32
 391    C.SparseGraph
 393  def sparse_build(items, size, threshold, minimum_common, minimum_identity):
 394    C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, C.SparseGraph,
 395      \{Tuple: for_prefilter => for_scoring => (
 396        score_candidates(K.sparse_build(for_prefilter, size, minimum_common,
 397          minimum_identity), for_scoring, size, threshold)
 398      )}, C.records_copy(items))

  59  def choose(-A: Type, condition: Bool, branches: Bool -> A) -> A:
  60    branches(condition)
  62  def apply(-A: Type, -B: Type, body: A -> B, value: A) -> B:
  63    body(value)
  65  def minimum(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
  66    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{True: left; False: right})
  68  def subtract(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
  69    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{True: 0; False: U32.sub(left, right)})
  71  law array_depth:
  72    for fuel: Nat
  73    for +size: U32
  74    for +capacity: U32
  75    for depth: Nat
  76    Nat
  78  def array_depth(fuel, size, capacity, depth):
  79    match fuel:
  80      case Zero{}:
  81        depth
  82      case Succ{rest}:
  83        choose(Nat, U32.is_le(size, capacity), \{
  84          True: depth
  85          False: array_depth(rest, size, U32.mul(capacity, 2), Succ{depth})
  86        })
  88  def hash30(+word: U32) -> U32:
  89    +a = {U32.xor(word, U32.shrn(word, 16n)) : U32}
  90    +b = {U32.mul(a, 2146121005) : U32}
  91    +c = {U32.xor(b, U32.shrn(b, 15n)) : U32}
  92    +d = {U32.mul(c, 2221713035) : U32}
  93    U32.xor(d, U32.shrn(d, 16n))
  95  def complement(base: U32) -> U32:
  96    match base:
  97      case 0: 3
  98      case 1: 2
  99      case 2: 1
 100      case 3: 0
 101      case _: 4
 106  law sequence_fill:
 107    for fuel: Nat
 108    for cursor: P.Cursor

 110    for values: Array<U32>

 113  def sequence_fill(fuel, cursor, index, values):
 114    match fuel:
 115      case Zero{}:
 116        values
 117      case Succ{rest}:
 118        apply(P.Cursor & U32, Array<U32>, \{Tuple: following => base => (
 119          sequence_fill(rest, following, U32.add(index, 1),
 120            Array.set(U32, values, index, base))
 121        )}, P.next(cursor))

 147        )}, Array.get(U32, source, index))

 163  law copy_reverse:
 164    for fuel: Nat
 165    for +index: U32
 166    for +length: U32
 167    for source: Array<U32>
 168    for target: Array<U32>
 169    Arrays
 171  def copy_reverse(fuel, index, length, source, target):
 172    match fuel:
 173      case Zero{}:
 174        Arrays{source, target}
 175      case Succ{rest}:
 176        +source_index = {U32.sub(U32.sub(length, 1), index) : U32}
 177        apply(Array<U32> & U32, Arrays, \{Tuple: next => base => (
 178          copy_reverse(rest, U32.add(index, 1), length, next,
 179            Array.set(U32, target, U32.add(U32.add(length, 80), index),
 180              complement(base)))
 181        )}, Array.get(U32, source, source_index))

 200    match arrays:

 203          copy_reverse(U32.to_nat(length), 0, length, source,

 206  law reference_array:
 207    for sequence: P.Sequence
 208    for +length: U32

 211  def reference_array(sequence, length):
 212    +total = {U32.add(U32.mul(length, 2), 120) : U32}
 213    source = sequence_array(sequence, length)
 214    reference_array_reverse(copy_forward(U32.to_nat(length), 0, source,
 215      Array.new(U32, array_depth(31n, total, 1, 0n), 4)), length, total)
 220  law array_kmer:
 221    for fuel: Nat
 222    for bases: Array<U32>
 223    for +position: U32
 224    for +value: U32

 226    for +mask: U32

 229  def array_kmer(fuel, bases, position, value, valid, mask):
 230    match fuel:
 231      case Zero{}:
 232        (bases, Kmer{value, valid})
 233      case Succ{rest}:
 234        apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Kmer,
 235          \{Tuple: next => +base => (
 236            array_kmer(rest, next, U32.add(position, 1),
 237              U32.and(U32.or(U32.shln(value, 2n), U32.and(base, 3)), mask),
 238              valid && U32.is_lt(base, 4), mask)
 239          )}, Array.get(U32, bases, position))
 241  law insert_posting:

 243    for +bucket: U32
 244    for +key: U32
 245    for +position: U32

 249    apply(Array<Posting> & Posting, Array<Posting>,
 250      \{Tuple: next => postings => (
 251        Array.set(Posting, next, bucket, PostingCon{key, position, postings})
 252      )}, Array.get(Posting, buckets, bucket))

 389  law pair_equal_len:
 390    for fuel: Nat
 391    for state: PairState
 392    for +reference_position: U32
 393    for +query_position: U32
 394    for +total: U32
 395    PairState & U32
 397  def pair_equal_len(fuel, state, reference_position, query_position, total):
 398    match fuel:
 399      case Zero{}:
 400        (state, total)
 401      case Succ{rest}:
 402        apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 403          \{Tuple: after_ref => +ref_base => (
 404            apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 405              \{Tuple: after_query => +query_base => (
 406                choose(PairState & U32,
 407                  U32.is_eq(ref_base, query_base) && U32.is_lt(ref_base, 4), \{
 408                    True: pair_equal_len(rest, after_query,
 409                      U32.add(reference_position, 1),
 410                      U32.add(query_position, 1), U32.add(total, 1))
 411                    False: (after_query, total)
 412                  })
 413              )}, pair_get_query(after_ref, query_position))
 414          )}, pair_get_reference(state, reference_position))
 416  def longer(candidate: Anchor, best: Anchor) -> Anchor:
 417    match candidate best:
 418      case Anchor{candidate_position, +candidate_length, candidate_close}
 419           Anchor{best_position, +best_length, best_close}:
 420        choose(Anchor, U32.is_gt(candidate_length, best_length), \{
 421          True: Anchor{candidate_position, candidate_length, candidate_close}
 422          False: Anchor{best_position, best_length, best_close}
 423        })
