 434  def search_long_postings(postings, key, query_position, available, state, best):
 435    match postings:
 436      case PostingNil{}:
 437        (state, best)
 438      case PostingCon{+entry_key, +position, tail}:
 439        choose(PairState & Anchor, U32.is_eq(entry_key, key), \{
 440          False: search_long_postings(tail, key, query_position, available,
 441            state, best)
 442          True: apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Anchor,
 443            \{Tuple: next => length => (
 444              search_long_postings(tail, key, query_position, available, next,
 445                longer(Anchor{position, length, False{}}, best))
 446            )}, pair_equal_len(U32.to_nat(available), state, position,
 447              query_position, 0))
 448        })

 509  def better_close(candidate: Anchor, best: Anchor, +predicted: U32) -> Anchor:
 510    match candidate best:
 511      case Anchor{+candidate_position, +candidate_length, candidate_close}
 512           Anchor{+best_position, +best_length, best_close}:
 513        +candidate_distance = {choose(U32,
 514          U32.is_lt(candidate_position, predicted),
 515          \{True: U32.sub(predicted, candidate_position)
 516            False: U32.sub(candidate_position, predicted)}) : U32}
 517        +best_distance = {choose(U32, U32.is_lt(best_position, predicted),
 518          \{True: U32.sub(predicted, best_position)
 519            False: U32.sub(best_position, predicted)}) : U32}
 520        choose(Anchor,
 521          U32.is_gt(candidate_length, best_length)
 522            || (U32.is_eq(candidate_length, best_length)
 523              && U32.is_lt(candidate_distance, best_distance)), \{
 524            True: Anchor{candidate_position, candidate_length, True{}}
 525            False: Anchor{best_position, best_length, best_close}
 526          })
 528  law search_short_postings:
 529    for postings: Posting
 530    for +query_position: U32
 531    for +available: U32
 532    for +lower: U32
 533    for +upper: U32
 534    for +predicted: U32
 535    for state: PairState
 536    for best: Anchor
 537    PairState & Anchor
 539  def search_short_postings(postings, query_position, available, lower, upper,
 540                            predicted, state, best):
 541    match postings:
 542      case PostingNil{}:
 543        (state, best)
 544      case PostingCon{key, +position, tail}:
 545        choose(PairState & Anchor,
 546          U32.is_ge(position, lower) && U32.is_lt(position, upper), \{
 547            False: search_short_postings(tail, query_position, available, lower,
 548              upper, predicted, state, best)
 549            True: apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Anchor,
 550              \{Tuple: next => +length => (
 551                next_best = choose(Anchor, U32.is_ge(length, 7), \{
 552                  True: better_close(Anchor{position, length, True{}}, best,
 553                    predicted)
 554                  False: best
 555                })
 556                search_short_postings(tail, query_position, available, lower,
 557                  upper, predicted, next, next_best)
 558              )}, pair_equal_len(U32.to_nat(available), state, position,
 559                query_position, 0))
 560          })

 563    for kmer: Kmer

 579              search_short_postings(postings, query_position, available,
 580                subtract(predicted, literal_run), U32.add(predicted, 40),
 581                predicted, next, Anchor{0, 0, True{}})
 582            )}, pair_short_bucket(state, value))
 583        })

 602  def select_predicted(long: Anchor, close: Anchor, +reference_length: U32,
 603                       +literal_run: U32) -> Anchor:
 604    match long close:
 605      case Anchor{+long_position, +long_length, long_close}
 606           Anchor{close_position, +close_length, close_flag}:
 607        +has_long = {U32.is_ne(long_position, 0)
 608          && U32.is_ge(long_length, 11) : Bool}
 609        +has_close = {U32.is_ge(close_length, 7) : Bool}
 610        choose(Anchor, has_long, \{
 611          False: choose(Anchor, has_close, \{
 612            True: Anchor{close_position, close_length, True{}}
 613            False: Anchor{0, 0, False{}}
 614          })
 615          True: choose(Anchor, has_close, \{
 616            False: Anchor{long_position, long_length, False{}}
 617            True: choose(Anchor, Choice.anchor_preferred(long_length,
 618              close_length, reference_length, literal_run), \{
 619                True: Anchor{long_position, long_length, False{}}
 620                False: Anchor{close_position, close_length, True{}}
 621              })
 622          })
 623        })
 625  def distance(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
 626    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{
 627      True: U32.sub(right, left)
 628      False: U32.sub(left, right)
 629    })
 631  def classify_selected(anchor: Anchor, +predicted: U32) -> Anchor:
 632    match anchor:
 633      case Anchor{+position, length, close}:
 634        Anchor{position, length,
 635          U32.is_le(distance(position, predicted), 40)}

 638    for first: PairState & Anchor
 639    for +query_position: U32
 640    for +available: U32
 641    for +predicted: U32
 642    for +literal_run: U32
 643    for +reference_length: U32
 644    PairState & Anchor
 646  def find_anchor_predicted(first, query_position, available, predicted,
 647                            literal_run, reference_length):
 648    match first:
 649      case Tuple{state, long}:
 650        apply(PairState & Anchor, PairState & Anchor,
 651          \{Tuple: next => close => (next, classify_selected(
 652            select_predicted(long, close, reference_length, literal_run),
 653            predicted))},
 654          find_short(state, query_position, available, predicted, literal_run))
 656  law find_anchor:
 657    for state: PairState
 658    for +query_position: U32
 659    for +available: U32
 660    for +has_prediction: Bool
 661    for +predicted: U32
 662    for +literal_run: U32
 663    for +reference_length: U32
 664    PairState & Anchor
 666  def find_anchor(state, query_position, available, has_prediction, predicted,
 667                  literal_run, reference_length):
 668    choose(PairState & Anchor, has_prediction, \{
 669      True: find_anchor_predicted(find_long(state, query_position, available),
 670        query_position, available, predicted, literal_run, reference_length)
 671      False: apply(PairState & Anchor, PairState & Anchor,
 672        \{Tuple: next => \{Anchor: position => +length => close => (
 673          (next, choose(Anchor, U32.is_ge(length, 11), \{
 674            True: Anchor{position, length, False{}}
 675            False: Anchor{0, 0, False{}}
 676          }))
 677        )}}, find_long(state, query_position, available))
 678    })
 680  law pair_count_equal:
 681    for fuel: Nat
 682    for state: PairState
 683    for +reference_position: U32
 684    for +query_position: U32
 685    for +total: U32
 686    PairState & U32
 688  def pair_count_equal(fuel, state, reference_position, query_position, total):
 689    match fuel:
 690      case Zero{}:
 691        (state, total)
 692      case Succ{rest}:
 693        apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 694          \{Tuple: after_ref => +ref_base => (
 695            apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 696              \{Tuple: after_query => +query_base => (
 697                pair_count_equal(rest, after_query,
 698                  U32.add(reference_position, 1), U32.add(query_position, 1),
 699                  U32.add(total, choose(U32,
 700                    U32.is_eq(ref_base, query_base) && U32.is_lt(ref_base, 4),
 701                    \{True: 1; False: 0})))
 702              )}, pair_get_query(after_ref, query_position))
 703          )}, pair_get_reference(state, reference_position))
 705  law gap_split_right:
 706    for first: PairState & U32
 707    for +query_start: U32
 708    for +anchor_end: U32
 709    for +gap_length: U32
 710    for +scan_length: U32
 711    for +split: U32
 712    PairState & U32
 714  def gap_split_right(first, query_start, anchor_end, gap_length, scan_length,
 715                      split):
 716    match first:
 717      case Tuple{state, +left_matches}:
 718        +right_length = {U32.sub(scan_length, split) : U32}
 719        apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 720          \{Tuple: next => right_matches => (
 721            (next, U32.add(left_matches, right_matches))
 722          )}, pair_count_equal(U32.to_nat(right_length), state,
 723            subtract(anchor_end, right_length),
 724            U32.add(query_start, U32.sub(gap_length, right_length)), 0))
 726  law gap_split:
 727    for state: PairState
 728    for +query_start: U32
 729    for +predicted: U32
 730    for +anchor_end: U32
 731    for +gap_length: U32
 732    for +scan_length: U32
 733    for +split: U32
 734    PairState & U32
 736  def gap_split(state, query_start, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 737                scan_length, split):
 738    gap_split_right(pair_count_equal(U32.to_nat(split), state,
 739      subtract(predicted, gap_length), query_start, 0), query_start, anchor_end,
 740      gap_length, scan_length, split)
 742  law best_gap:
 743    for fuel: Nat
 744    for state: PairState
 745    for +query_start: U32
 746    for +predicted: U32
 747    for +anchor_end: U32
 748    for +gap_length: U32
 749    for +scan_length: U32
 750    for +split: U32
 751    for +best: U32
 752    PairState & U32
 754  def best_gap(fuel, state, query_start, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 755               scan_length, split, best):
 756    match fuel:
 757      case Zero{}:
 758        (state, best)
 759      case Succ{rest}:
 760        apply(PairState & U32, PairState & U32,
 761          \{Tuple: next => +candidate => (
 762            best_gap(rest, next, query_start, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 763              scan_length, U32.add(split, 1),
 764              choose(U32, U32.is_ge(candidate, best),
 765                \{True: candidate; False: best}))
 766          )}, gap_split(state, query_start, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 767            scan_length, split))
 769  def flags_take(fuel: Nat, flags: Flags) -> Flags:
 770    match fuel flags:
 771      case Zero{} remaining:
 772        FlagsNil{}
 773      case Succ{rest} FlagsNil{}:
 774        FlagsNil{}
 775      case Succ{rest} FlagsCon{flag, tail}:
 776        FlagsCon{flag, flags_take(rest, tail)}
 778  def flags_sum(flags: Flags, +total: U32) -> U32:
 779    match flags:
 780      case FlagsNil{}:
 781        total
 782      case FlagsCon{flag, tail}:
 783        flags_sum(tail, U32.add(total, flag))
 785  law extension_loop:
 786    for fuel: Nat
 787    for state: PairState
 788    for +reference_position: U32
 789    for +query_position: U32
 790    for window: Flags
 791    for +length: U32
 792    for +run: U32
 793    for +matches: U32
 794    for +mismatches: U32
 795    for +good_length: U32
 796    for +good_matches: U32
 797    for +good_mismatches: U32
 798    PairState & Extension
 800  def extension_loop(fuel, state, reference_position, query_position, window,
 801                     length, run, matches, mismatches, good_length,
 802                     good_matches, good_mismatches):
 803    match fuel:
 804      case Zero{}:
 805        (state, Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches})
 806      case Succ{rest}:
 807        apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Extension,
 808          \{Tuple: after_ref => +ref_base => (
 809            apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Extension,
 810              \{Tuple: after_query => +query_base => (
 811                +is_match = {U32.is_eq(ref_base, query_base)
 812                  && U32.is_lt(ref_base, 4) : Bool}
 813                +flag = {choose(U32, is_match, \{True: 0; False: 1}) : U32}
 814                +next_window = {flags_take(15n, FlagsCon{flag, window}) : Flags}
 815                +next_length = {U32.add(length, 1) : U32}
 816                +next_run = {choose(U32, is_match,
 817                  \{True: U32.add(run, 1); False: 0}) : U32}
 818                +next_matches = {U32.add(matches,
 819                  choose(U32, is_match, \{True: 1; False: 0})) : U32}
 820                +next_mismatches = {U32.add(mismatches, flag) : U32}
 821                choose(PairState & Extension,
 822                  U32.is_gt(flags_sum(next_window, 0), 7), \{
 823                    True: (after_query,
 824                      Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches})
 825                    False: choose(PairState & Extension,
 826                      U32.is_ge(next_run, 3), \{
 827                        True: extension_loop(rest, after_query,
 828                          U32.add(reference_position, 1),
 829                          U32.add(query_position, 1), next_window, next_length,
 830                          next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches, next_length,
 831                          next_matches, next_mismatches)
 832                        False: extension_loop(rest, after_query,
 833                          U32.add(reference_position, 1),
 834                          U32.add(query_position, 1), next_window, next_length,
 835                          next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches, good_length,
 836                          good_matches, good_mismatches)
 837                      })
 838                  })
 839              )}, pair_get_query(after_ref, query_position))
 840          )}, pair_get_reference(state, reference_position))
 842  law extension_backward_loop:
 843    for fuel: Nat
 844    for state: PairState
 845    for +reference_position: U32
 846    for +query_position: U32
 847    for window: Flags
 848    for +length: U32
 849    for +run: U32
 850    for +matches: U32
 851    for +mismatches: U32
 852    for +good_length: U32
 853    for +good_matches: U32
 854    for +good_mismatches: U32
 855    PairState & Extension
 857  def extension_backward_loop(fuel, state, reference_position, query_position,
 858                              window, length, run, matches, mismatches,
 859                              good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches):
 860    match fuel:
 861      case Zero{}:
 862        (state, Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches})
 863      case Succ{rest}:
 864        choose(PairState & Extension,
 865          U32.is_eq(reference_position, 0) || U32.is_eq(query_position, 0), \{
 866            True: (state, Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches})
 867            False: (
 868              +ref_at = {U32.sub(reference_position, 1) : U32}
 869              +query_at = {U32.sub(query_position, 1) : U32}
 870              apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Extension,
 871                \{Tuple: after_ref => +ref_base => (
 872                  apply(PairState & U32, PairState & Extension,
 873                    \{Tuple: after_query => +query_base => (
 874                      +is_match = {U32.is_eq(ref_base, query_base)
 875                        && U32.is_lt(ref_base, 4) : Bool}
 876                      +flag = {choose(U32, is_match,
 877                        \{True: 0; False: 1}) : U32}
 878                      +next_window = {flags_take(15n,
 879                        FlagsCon{flag, window}) : Flags}
 880                      +next_length = {U32.add(length, 1) : U32}
 881                      +next_run = {choose(U32, is_match,
 882                        \{True: U32.add(run, 1); False: 0}) : U32}
 883                      +next_matches = {U32.add(matches,
 884                        choose(U32, is_match, \{True: 1; False: 0})) : U32}
 885                      +next_mismatches = {U32.add(mismatches, flag) : U32}
 886                      choose(PairState & Extension,
 887                        U32.is_gt(flags_sum(next_window, 0), 7), \{
 888                          True: (after_query, Extension{good_length,
 889                            good_matches, good_mismatches})
 890                          False: choose(PairState & Extension,
 891                            U32.is_ge(next_run, 3), \{
 892                              True: extension_backward_loop(rest, after_query,
 893                                ref_at, query_at, next_window, next_length,
 894                                next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches,
 895                                next_length, next_matches, next_mismatches)
 896                              False: extension_backward_loop(rest, after_query,
 897                                ref_at, query_at, next_window, next_length,
 898                                next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches,
 899                                good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches)
 900                            })
 901                        })
 902                    )}, pair_get_query(after_ref, query_at))
 903                )}, pair_get_reference(state, ref_at))
 904            )
 905          })
 907  def finish_region(+region_matches: U32, +region_mismatches: U32,
 908                    counts: Counts) -> Counts:
 909    match counts:
 910      case Counts{matches, mismatches, components}:
 911        choose(Counts,
 912          U32.is_ge(U32.add(region_matches, region_mismatches), 35), \{
 913            True: Counts{U32.add(matches, region_matches),
 914              U32.add(mismatches, region_mismatches),
 915              U32.add(components, 1)}
 916            False: Counts{matches, mismatches, components}
 917          })
 919  law parse_loop:
 920    for fuel: Nat
 921    for state: PairState
 922    for +query_position: U32
 923    for +has_prediction: Bool
 924    for +predicted: U32
 925    for +literal_run: U32
 926    for +region_matches: U32
 927    for +region_mismatches: U32
 928    for counts: Counts
 929    ParseResult
 931  def parse_loop(fuel, state, query_position, has_prediction, predicted,
 932                 literal_run, region_matches, region_mismatches, counts):
 933    match fuel:
 934      case Zero{}:
 935        ParseResult{state,
 936          finish_region(region_matches, region_mismatches, counts)}
 937      case Succ{rest}:
 938        match state:
 939          case PairState{reference, query, long, short, +reference_length,
 940                         +query_length}:
 941            +available = {subtract(query_length, query_position) : U32}
 942            choose(ParseResult, U32.is_eq(available, 0), \{
 943              True: ParseResult{
 944                PairState{reference, query, long, short, reference_length,
 945                  query_length},
 946                finish_region(region_matches, region_mismatches, counts)}
 947              False: apply(PairState & Anchor, ParseResult,
 948                \{Tuple: found_state =>
 949                  \{Anchor: +position => +length => +close => (
 950                    choose(ParseResult, U32.is_eq(length, 0), \{
 951                      True: parse_loop(rest, found_state,
 952                        U32.add(query_position, 1),
 953                        has_prediction && U32.is_lt(literal_run, 40),
 954                        U32.add(predicted, 1), U32.add(literal_run, 1),
 955                        region_matches, region_mismatches, counts)
 956                      False: apply(PairState & Extension, ParseResult,
 957                        \{Tuple: extended_state =>
 958                          \{Extension: +extension_length => +extension_matches =>
 959                            +extension_mismatches => (
 960                              +consumed = {U32.add(length, extension_length) : U32}
 961                              +next_position = {U32.add(
 962                                U32.add(position, length), extension_length) : U32}
 963                              choose(ParseResult, close, \{
 964                                True: (
 965                                  +left_start = {subtract(predicted,
 966                                    literal_run) : U32}
 967                                  +anchor_end = {U32.add(position, length) : U32}
 968                                  +scan_length = {choose(U32,
 969                                    U32.is_lt(anchor_end, left_start), \{
 970                                      True: literal_run
 971                                      False: minimum(U32.sub(anchor_end,
 972                                        left_start), literal_run)
 973                                    }) : U32}
 974                                  apply(PairState & U32, ParseResult,
 975                                    \{Tuple: next => +gap_matches => (
 976                                      parse_loop(rest, next,
 977                                        U32.add(query_position, consumed), True{},
 978                                        next_position, 0,
 979                                        U32.add(U32.add(U32.add(region_matches,
 980                                          length), gap_matches), extension_matches),
 981                                        U32.add(U32.add(region_mismatches,
 982                                          U32.sub(literal_run, gap_matches)),
 983                                          extension_mismatches), counts)
 984                                    )}, best_gap(Succ{U32.to_nat(scan_length)},
 985                                      extended_state,
 986                                      subtract(query_position, literal_run),
 987                                      predicted, anchor_end, literal_run,
 988                                      scan_length, 0, 0))
 989                                )
 990                                False: (
 991                                  +region_span = {U32.add(region_matches,
 992                                    region_mismatches) : U32}
 993                                  +pending = {choose(U32,
 994                                    U32.is_lt(region_span, 35), \{
 995                                      True: U32.add(region_span, literal_run)
 996                                      False: literal_run
 997                                    }) : U32}
 998                                  apply(PairState & Extension, ParseResult,
 999                                    \{Tuple: backward_state =>
1000                                      \{Extension: backward_length =>
1001                                        +backward_matches =>
1002                                        +backward_mismatches => (
1003                                          parse_loop(rest, backward_state,
1004                                            U32.add(query_position, consumed),
1005                                            True{}, next_position, 0,
1006                                            U32.add(U32.add(backward_matches,
1007                                              length), extension_matches),
1008                                            U32.add(backward_mismatches,
1009                                              extension_mismatches),
1010                                            finish_region(region_matches,
1011                                              region_mismatches, counts))
1012                                        )}},
1013                                    extension_backward_loop(U32.to_nat(pending),
1014                                      extended_state, position, query_position,
1015                                      FlagsNil{}, 0, 3, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0))
1016                                )
1017                              })
1018                            )}},
1019                        extension_loop(
1020                          U32.to_nat(U32.sub(available, length)), found_state,
1021                          U32.add(position, length),
1022                          U32.add(query_position, length), FlagsNil{}, 0, 3,
1023                          0, 0, 0, 0, 0))
1024                    })
1025                  )}},
1026                find_anchor(PairState{reference, query, long, short,
1027                  reference_length, query_length}, query_position, available,
1028                  has_prediction, predicted, literal_run, reference_length))
1029            })
1031  def ratio(+numerator: U32, +denominator: U32) -> U32:
1032    choose(U32, U32.is_eq(denominator, 0), \{
1033      True: 0
1034      False: U32.div(U32.mul(numerator, 10000), denominator)
1035    })
1037  def metrics(counts: Counts, +query_length: U32) -> Metrics:
1038    match counts:
1039      case Counts{+matches, +mismatches, +components}:
1040        Metrics{matches, mismatches, components,
1041          ratio(matches, U32.add(matches, mismatches)),
1042          ratio(matches, query_length),
1043          ratio(U32.add(matches, mismatches), query_length)}
1045  law score_finish:
1046    for result: ParseResult
1047    for +query_length: U32
1048    ScoreResult
1050  def score_finish(result, query_length):
1051    match result:
1052      case ParseResult{PairState{reference, query, long, short, +reference_length,
1053                                 raw_query_length}, counts}:
1054        ScoreResult{ReferenceIndex{reference, long, short, reference_length},
1055          metrics(counts, query_length)}
1057  law score:
1058    for index: ReferenceIndex
1059    for query: P.Sequence
1060    for +query_length: U32
1061    ScoreResult
1063  def score(index, query, query_length):
1064    score_finish(parse_loop(U32.to_nat(query_length),
1065      pair_from_query(index, query, query_length), 0, False{}, 0, 0, 0, 0,
1066      Counts{0, 0, 0}), query_length)
lib/lz_ani_numeric.bend
10  import Base
  11  import ./lz_ani_choice.bend as Choice
  13  type Bases is Data:
  14    BasesNil{}
  15    BasesCon{head: U32, tail: Bases}
  17  type Anchor is Data:
  18    Anchor{position: U32, length: U32, close: Bool}
  20  type Counts is Data:
  21    Counts{matches: U32, mismatches: U32, components: U32}
  23  type Metrics is Data:
  24    Metrics{matches: U32, mismatches: U32, components: U32,
  25      ani: U32, gani: U32, coverage: U32}
  27  type Extension is Data:
  28    Extension{length: U32, matches: U32, mismatches: U32}
  30  def choose(-A: Type, condition: Bool, branches: Bool -> A) -> A:
  31    branches(condition)
  33  def apply(-A: Type, -B: Type, body: A -> B, value: A) -> B:
  34    body(value)
  36  def minimum(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
  37    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{True: left; False: right})
  39  def distance(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
  40    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{
  41      True: U32.sub(right, left)
  42      False: U32.sub(left, right)
  43    })
  45  def subtract(+left: U32, +right: U32) -> U32:
  46    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(left, right), \{
  47      True: 0
  48      False: U32.sub(left, right)
  49    })
  51  def base_code(code: U32) -> U32:
  52    match code:
  53      case 65: 0
  54      case 97: 0
  55      case 67: 1
  56      case 99: 1
  57      case 71: 2
  58      case 103: 2
  59      case 84: 3
  60      case 116: 3
  61      case 85: 3
  62      case 117: 3
  63      case _: 4
  65  law from_string:
  66    for text: String
  67    Bases
  69  def from_string(text):
  70    match text:
  71      case SNil{}:
  72        BasesNil{}
  73      case SCon{Chr{code}, tail}:
  74        BasesCon{base_code(code), from_string(tail)}
  76  law bases_length:
  77    for bases: Bases
  78    for +total: U32
  79    U32
  81  def bases_length(bases, total):
  82    match bases:
  83      case BasesNil{}:
  84        total
  85      case BasesCon{head, tail}:
  86        bases_length(tail, U32.add(total, 1))
  88  law bases_drop:
  89    for fuel: Nat
  90    for bases: Bases

  94    match fuel bases:
  95      case Zero{} remaining:
  96        remaining
  97      case Succ{rest} BasesNil{}:
  98        BasesNil{}
  99      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{head, tail}:
 100        bases_drop(rest, tail)
 102  law bases_take:
 103    for fuel: Nat
 104    for bases: Bases
 105    Bases
 107  def bases_take(fuel, bases):
 108    match fuel bases:
 109      case Zero{} remaining:
 110        BasesNil{}
 111      case Succ{rest} BasesNil{}:
 112        BasesNil{}
 113      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{head, tail}:
 114        BasesCon{head, bases_take(rest, tail)}
 116  law bases_sum:

 122    match bases:
 123      case BasesNil{}:
 124        total
 125      case BasesCon{head, tail}:
 126        bases_sum(tail, U32.add(total, head))
 128  law bases_append:
 129    for left: Bases
 130    for right: Bases

 133  def bases_append(left, right):
 134    match left:
 135      case BasesNil{}:
 136        right
 137      case BasesCon{head, tail}:
 138        BasesCon{head, bases_append(tail, right)}
 140  law bases_repeat:
 141    for fuel: Nat
 142    for +base: U32
 143    Bases

 146    match fuel:
 147      case Zero{}:
 148        BasesNil{}
 149      case Succ{rest}:
 150        BasesCon{base, bases_repeat(rest, base)}
 152  def complement(base: U32) -> U32:
 153    match base:
 154      case 0: 3
 155      case 1: 2
 156      case 2: 1
 157      case 3: 0
 158      case _: 4
 160  law reverse_complement_loop:
 161    for bases: Bases
 162    for result: Bases
 163    Bases
 165  def reverse_complement_loop(bases, result):
 166    match bases:
 167      case BasesNil{}:
 168        result
 169      case BasesCon{head, tail}:
 170        reverse_complement_loop(tail, BasesCon{complement(head), result})
 172  law reverse_complement:
 173    for bases: Bases
 174    Bases

 177    reverse_complement_loop(bases, BasesNil{})
 179  law bases_reverse_loop:
 180    for bases: Bases
 181    for result: Bases

 185    match bases:
 186      case BasesNil{}:
 187        result
 188      case BasesCon{head, tail}:
 189        bases_reverse_loop(tail, BasesCon{head, result})

 192    for bases: Bases
 193    Bases

 196    bases_reverse_loop(bases, BasesNil{})

 201    for +reference: Bases
 202    Bases
 204  def reference_space(reference):
 205    bases_append(reference,
 206      bases_append(bases_repeat(80n, 4),
 207        bases_append(reverse_complement(reference), bases_repeat(40n, 4))))
 209  law equal_prefix:
 210    for left: Bases
 211    for right: Bases
 212    for +total: U32
 213    U32
 215  def equal_prefix(left, right, total):
 216    match left right:
 217      case BasesNil{} remaining:
 218        total
 219      case BasesCon{head, tail} BasesNil{}:
 220        total
 221      case BasesCon{+left_head, left_tail} BasesCon{+right_head, right_tail}:
 222        choose(U32, U32.is_eq(left_head, right_head) && U32.is_lt(left_head, 4), \{
 223          True: equal_prefix(left_tail, right_tail, U32.add(total, 1))
 224          False: total
 225        })
 227  law count_equal:
 228    for fuel: Nat
 229    for left: Bases
 230    for right: Bases
 231    for +total: U32
 232    U32
 234  def count_equal(fuel, left, right, total):
 235    match fuel left right:
 236      case Zero{} left_remaining right_remaining:
 237        total
 238      case Succ{rest} BasesNil{} right_remaining:
 239        total
 240      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{head, tail} BasesNil{}:
 241        total
 242      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{+left_head, left_tail} BasesCon{+right_head, right_tail}:
 243        count_equal(rest, left_tail, right_tail,
 244          U32.add(total, choose(U32,
 245            U32.is_eq(left_head, right_head) && U32.is_lt(left_head, 4),
 246            \{True: 1; False: 0})))
 252  law gap_split_matches:
 253    for +reference: Bases
 254    for +gap: Bases
 255    for +predicted: U32
 256    for +anchor_end: U32
 257    for +gap_length: U32
 258    for +split: U32
 259    U32
 261  def gap_split_matches(reference, gap, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length, split):
 262    +right_length = {U32.sub(gap_length, split) : U32}
 263    +left_matches = {count_equal(U32.to_nat(split),
 264      bases_drop(U32.to_nat(subtract(predicted, gap_length)), reference),
 265      gap, 0) : U32}
 266    +right_matches = {count_equal(U32.to_nat(right_length),
 267      bases_drop(U32.to_nat(subtract(anchor_end, right_length)), reference),
 268      bases_drop(U32.to_nat(split), gap), 0) : U32}
 269    U32.add(left_matches, right_matches)
 271  law best_gap_loop:
 272    for fuel: Nat
 273    for +reference: Bases
 274    for +gap: Bases
 275    for +predicted: U32
 276    for +anchor_end: U32
 277    for +gap_length: U32
 278    for +split: U32
 279    for +best: U32
 280    U32
 282  def best_gap_loop(fuel, reference, gap, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 283                    split, best):
 284    match fuel:
 285      case Zero{}:
 286        best
 287      case Succ{rest}:
 288        +candidate = {gap_split_matches(reference, gap, predicted, anchor_end,
 289          gap_length, split) : U32}
 290        best_gap_loop(rest, reference, gap, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length,
 291          U32.add(split, 1),
 292          choose(U32, U32.is_gt(candidate, best),
 293            \{True: candidate; False: best}))
 295  law best_gap_matches:
 296    for reference: Bases
 297    for gap: Bases
 298    for +predicted: U32
 299    for +anchor_end: U32
 300    for +gap_length: U32
 301    U32
 303  def best_gap_matches(reference, gap, predicted, anchor_end, gap_length):
 304    best_gap_loop(Succ{U32.to_nat(gap_length)}, reference, gap, predicted,
 305      anchor_end, gap_length, 0, 0)
 311  law extension_loop:
 312    for fuel: Nat
 313    for reference: Bases
 314    for query: Bases
 315    for window: Bases
 316    for +length: U32
 317    for +run: U32
 318    for +matches: U32
 319    for +mismatches: U32
 320    for +good_length: U32
 321    for +good_matches: U32
 322    for +good_mismatches: U32
 323    Extension
 325  def extension_loop(fuel, reference, query, window, length, run, matches,
 326                     mismatches, good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches):
 327    match fuel reference query:
 328      case Zero{} reference_remaining query_remaining:
 329        Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches}
 330      case Succ{rest} BasesNil{} query_remaining:
 331        Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches}
 332      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{ref_head, ref_tail} BasesNil{}:
 333        Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches}
 334      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{+ref_head, ref_tail} BasesCon{+query_head, query_tail}:
 335        +is_match = {U32.is_eq(ref_head, query_head) && U32.is_lt(ref_head, 4) : Bool}
 336        +flag = {choose(U32, is_match, \{True: 0; False: 1}) : U32}
 337        +next_window = {bases_take(15n, BasesCon{flag, window}) : Bases}
 338        +next_length = {U32.add(length, 1) : U32}
 339        +next_run = {choose(U32, is_match,
 340          \{True: U32.add(run, 1); False: 0}) : U32}
 341        +next_matches = {U32.add(matches,
 342          choose(U32, is_match, \{True: 1; False: 0})) : U32}
 343        +next_mismatches = {U32.add(mismatches, flag) : U32}
 344        choose(Extension, U32.is_gt(bases_sum(next_window, 0), 7), \{
 345          True: Extension{good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches}
 346          False: choose(Extension, U32.is_ge(next_run, 3), \{
 347            True: extension_loop(rest, ref_tail, query_tail, next_window,
 348              next_length, next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches,
 349              next_length, next_matches, next_mismatches)
 350            False: extension_loop(rest, ref_tail, query_tail, next_window,
 351              next_length, next_run, next_matches, next_mismatches,
 352              good_length, good_matches, good_mismatches)
 353          })
 354        })
 356  law approximate_extension:
 357    for reference: Bases
 358    for +query: Bases
 359    Extension
 361  def approximate_extension(reference, query):
 362    extension_loop(U32.to_nat(bases_length(query, 0)), reference, query,
 363      BasesNil{}, 0, 3, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)
 365  def longer_anchor(candidate: Anchor, best: Anchor) -> Anchor:
 366    match candidate best:
 367      case Anchor{candidate_position, +candidate_length, candidate_close} Anchor{best_position, +best_length, best_close}:
 368        choose(Anchor, U32.is_gt(candidate_length, best_length), \{
 369          True: Anchor{candidate_position, candidate_length, candidate_close}
 370          False: Anchor{best_position, best_length, best_close}
 371        })
 373  law longest_anchor_loop:
 374    for +reference: Bases
 375    for +query: Bases
 376    for +position: U32
 377    for best: Anchor
 378    Anchor
 380  def longest_anchor_loop(reference, query, position, best):
 381    match reference:
 382      case BasesNil{}:
 383        best
 384      case BasesCon{+head, +tail}:
 385        +length = {equal_prefix(BasesCon{head, tail}, query, 0) : U32}
 386        next = longer_anchor(Anchor{position, length, False{}}, best)
 387        longest_anchor_loop(tail, query, U32.add(position, 1), next)
 389  law longest_anchor:
 390    for reference: Bases
 391    for query: Bases
 392    Anchor
 394  def longest_anchor(reference, query):
 395    longest_anchor_loop(reference, query, 0, Anchor{0, 0, False{}})
 397  def better_close(candidate: Anchor, best: Anchor, +predicted: U32) -> Anchor:
 398    match candidate best:
 399      case Anchor{+candidate_position, +candidate_length, candidate_close} Anchor{+best_position, +best_length, best_close}:
 400        +candidate_distance = {distance(candidate_position, predicted) : U32}
 401        +best_distance = {distance(best_position, predicted) : U32}
 402        choose(Anchor,
 403          U32.is_gt(candidate_length, best_length)
 404            || (U32.is_eq(candidate_length, best_length)
 405              && U32.is_lt(candidate_distance, best_distance)), \{
 406            True: Anchor{candidate_position, candidate_length, True{}}
 407            False: Anchor{best_position, best_length, best_close}
 408          })
 410  law close_anchor_loop:
 411    for +reference: Bases
 412    for +query: Bases
 413    for +position: U32
 414    for +predicted: U32
 415    for +literal_run: U32
 416    for best: Anchor
 417    Anchor
 419  def close_anchor_loop(reference, query, position, predicted, literal_run, best):
 420    match reference:
 421      case BasesNil{}:
 422        best
 423      case BasesCon{+head, +tail}:
 424        +length = {equal_prefix(BasesCon{head, tail}, query, 0) : U32}
 425        +eligible = {U32.is_ge(position, subtract(predicted, literal_run))
 426          && U32.is_lt(position, U32.add(predicted, 40))
 427          && U32.is_ge(length, 7) : Bool}
 428        next = choose(Anchor, eligible, \{
 429          True: better_close(Anchor{position, length, True{}}, best, predicted)
 430          False: best
 431        })
 432        close_anchor_loop(tail, query, U32.add(position, 1), predicted,
 433          literal_run, next)
 435  law close_anchor:
 436    for reference: Bases
 437    for query: Bases
 438    for +predicted: U32
 439    for +literal_run: U32
 440    Anchor
 442  def close_anchor(reference, query, predicted, literal_run):
 443    close_anchor_loop(reference, query, 0, predicted, literal_run,
 444      Anchor{0, 0, True{}})
 449  def select_predicted(long: Anchor, close: Anchor, +reference_length: U32,
 450                       +literal_run: U32) -> Anchor:
 451    match long close:
 452      case Anchor{+long_position, +long_length, long_close}
 453           Anchor{close_position, +close_length, close_flag}:
 454        +has_long = {U32.is_ne(long_position, 0)
 455          && U32.is_ge(long_length, 11) : Bool}
 456        +has_close = {U32.is_ge(close_length, 7) : Bool}
 457        choose(Anchor, has_long, \{
 458          False: choose(Anchor, has_close, \{
 459            True: Anchor{close_position, close_length, True{}}
 460            False: Anchor{0, 0, False{}}
 461          })
 462          True: choose(Anchor, has_close, \{
 463            False: Anchor{long_position, long_length, False{}}
 464            True: choose(Anchor, Choice.anchor_preferred(long_length,
 465              close_length, reference_length, literal_run), \{
 466                True: Anchor{long_position, long_length, False{}}
 467                False: Anchor{close_position, close_length, True{}}
 468              })
 469          })
 470        })
 472  def select_unpredicted(anchor: Anchor) -> Anchor:
 473    match anchor:
 474      case Anchor{position, +length, close}:
 475        choose(Anchor, U32.is_ge(length, 11), \{
 476          True: Anchor{position, length, False{}}
 477          False: Anchor{0, 0, False{}}
 478        })
 480  law select_anchor:
 481    for +reference: Bases
 482    for +query: Bases
 483    for +has_prediction: Bool
 484    for +predicted: U32
 485    for +literal_run: U32
 486    for +reference_length: U32
 487    Anchor
 489  def select_anchor(reference, query, has_prediction, predicted, literal_run,
 490                    reference_length):
 491    match has_prediction:
 492      case False{}:
 493        select_unpredicted(longest_anchor(reference, query))
 494      case True{}:
 495        select_predicted(longest_anchor(reference, query),
 496          close_anchor(reference, query, predicted, literal_run),
 497          reference_length, literal_run)
 499  def finish_region(+region_matches: U32, +region_mismatches: U32,
 500                    counts: Counts) -> Counts:
 501    match counts:
 502      case Counts{matches, mismatches, components}:
 503        choose(Counts,
 504          U32.is_ge(U32.add(region_matches, region_mismatches), 35), \{
 505            True: Counts{U32.add(matches, region_matches),
 506              U32.add(mismatches, region_mismatches),
 507              U32.add(components, 1)}
 508            False: Counts{matches, mismatches, components}
 509          })
 511  law parse_loop:
 512    for fuel: Nat
 513    for +reference: Bases
 514    for +query: Bases
 515    for +has_prediction: Bool
 516    for +predicted: U32
 517    for +literal_run: U32
 518    for +reference_length: U32
 519    for literals: Bases
 520    for +region_matches: U32
 521    for +region_mismatches: U32
 522    for counts: Counts
 523    Counts
 525  def parse_loop(fuel, reference, query, has_prediction, predicted, literal_run,
 526                 reference_length, literals, region_matches, region_mismatches,
 527                 counts):
 528    match fuel query:
 529      case Zero{} remaining:
 530        finish_region(region_matches, region_mismatches, counts)
 531      case Succ{rest} BasesNil{}:
 532        finish_region(region_matches, region_mismatches, counts)
 533      case Succ{rest} BasesCon{+head, +tail}:
 534        apply(Anchor, Counts,
 535          \{Anchor: +position => +length => +close => (
 536            choose(Counts, U32.is_eq(length, 0), \{
 537              True: parse_loop(rest, reference, tail,
 538                has_prediction && U32.is_lt(literal_run, 40),
 539                U32.add(predicted, 1), U32.add(literal_run, 1),
 540                reference_length, BasesCon{head, literals}, region_matches,
 541                region_mismatches, counts)
 542              False: apply(Extension, Counts,
 543                \{Extension: +extension_length => +extension_matches => +extension_mismatches => (
 544                  +consumed = {U32.add(length, extension_length) : U32}
 545                  +next_position = {U32.add(U32.add(position, length),
 546                    extension_length) : U32}
 547                  choose(Counts, close, \{
 548                    True: (
 549                      +gap_matches = {best_gap_matches(reference,
 550                        bases_reverse(literals), predicted,
 551                        U32.add(position, length), literal_run) : U32}
 552                      parse_loop(rest, reference,
 553                        bases_drop(U32.to_nat(consumed), BasesCon{head, tail}),
 554                        True{}, next_position, 0, reference_length, BasesNil{},
 555                        U32.add(U32.add(U32.add(region_matches, length),
 556                          gap_matches), extension_matches),
 557                        U32.add(U32.add(region_mismatches,
 558                          U32.sub(literal_run, gap_matches)),
 559                          extension_mismatches), counts)
 560                    )
 561                    False: parse_loop(rest, reference,
 562                      bases_drop(U32.to_nat(consumed), BasesCon{head, tail}),
 563                      True{}, next_position, 0, reference_length, BasesNil{},
 564                      U32.add(length, extension_matches), extension_mismatches,
 565                      finish_region(region_matches, region_mismatches, counts))
 566                  })
 567                )},
 568                approximate_extension(
 569                  bases_drop(U32.to_nat(U32.add(position, length)), reference),
 570                  bases_drop(U32.to_nat(length), BasesCon{head, tail})))
 571            })
 572          )},
 573          select_anchor(reference, BasesCon{head, tail}, has_prediction,
 574            predicted, literal_run, reference_length))
 576  law parse:
 577    for +reference: Bases
 578    for +query: Bases
 579    Counts
 581  def parse(reference, query):
 582    +reference_length = {U32.add(U32.mul(bases_length(reference, 0), 2), 120)
 583      : U32}
 584    parse_loop(U32.to_nat(bases_length(query, 0)), reference_space(reference),
 585      query, False{}, 0, 0, reference_length, BasesNil{}, 0, 0,
 586      Counts{0, 0, 0})
 588  def ratio(+numerator: U32, +denominator: U32) -> U32:
 589    choose(U32, U32.is_eq(denominator, 0), \{
 590      True: 0
 591      False: U32.div(U32.mul(numerator, 10000), denominator)
 592    })
 594  law metrics_from_counts:
 595    for counts: Counts
 596    for +query_length: U32
 597    Metrics
 599  def metrics_from_counts(counts, query_length):
 600    match counts:
 601      case Counts{+matches, +mismatches, +components}:
 602        Metrics{matches, mismatches, components,
 603          ratio(matches, U32.add(matches, mismatches)),
 604          ratio(matches, query_length),
 605          ratio(U32.add(matches, mismatches), query_length)}
 607  law score:
 608    for +reference: Bases
 609    for +query: Bases
 610    Metrics
 612  def score(reference, query):
 613    metrics_from_counts(parse(reference, query), bases_length(query, 0))

2  import Base
   4  type Sequence is Type:
   5    Sequence{words: Array<U32>, bits: U32, length: U32}
   7  type Cursor is Type:
   8    Cursor{words: Array<U32>, bits: U32, index: U32, word: U32, available: U32}
  10  def apply(-A: Type, -B: Type, f: A -> B, x: A) -> B:
  11    f(x)
  13  def choose(-A: Type, b: Bool, f: Bool -> A) -> A:
  14    f(b)
  16  def depth(fuel: Nat, +size: U32, +capacity: U32, n: Nat) -> Nat:
  17    match fuel:
  18      case Zero{}:
  19        n
  20      case Succ{rest}:
  21        choose(Nat, U32.is_ge(capacity, size), \{
  22          True: n
  23          False: depth(rest, size, U32.mul(capacity, 2), Succ{n})
  24        })
  27  def code(c: U32) -> U32:
  28    match c:
  29      case 65: 0
  30      case 67: 1
  31      case 71: 2
  32      case 84: 3
  33      case 82: 4
  34      case 89: 5
  35      case 83: 6
  36      case 87: 7
  37      case 75: 8
  38      case 77: 9
  39      case 66: 10
  40      case 68: 11
  41      case 72: 12
  42      case 86: 13
  43      case 78: 14
  44      case _: 15
  46  def ascii(c: U32) -> U32:
  47    match c:
  48      case 0: 65
  49      case 1: 67
  50      case 2: 71
  51      case 3: 84
  52      case 4: 82
  53      case 5: 89
  54      case 6: 83
  55      case 7: 87
  56      case 8: 75
  57      case 9: 77
  58      case 10: 66
  59      case 11: 68
  60      case 12: 72
  61      case 13: 86
  62      case _: 78
  64  def bit_width(text: String) -> U32:
  65    match text:
  66      case SNil{}:
  67        2
  68      case SCon{Chr{c}, tail}:
  69        choose(U32, U32.is_gt(code(c), 3), \{
  70          True: 4
  71          False: bit_width(tail)
  72        })
  74  def fill(text: String, +bits: U32, +index: U32, +offset: U32,
  75           word: U32, words: Array<U32>) -> Array<U32>:
  76    match text:
  77      case SNil{}:
  78        choose(Array<U32>, U32.is_eq(offset, 0), \{
  79          True: words
  80          False: Array.set(U32, words, index, word)
  81        })
  82      case SCon{Chr{c}, tail}:
  83        +next = {U32.or(word, U32.shln(code(c), U32.to_nat(offset))) : U32}
  84        +position = {U32.add(offset, bits) : U32}
  85        choose(Array<U32>, U32.is_eq(position, 32), \{
  86          True: fill(tail, bits, U32.add(index, 1), 0, 0,
  87            Array.set(U32, words, index, next))
  88          False: fill(tail, bits, index, position, next, words)
  89        })
  91  def pack(+text: String, +length: U32) -> Sequence:
  92    +bits = {bit_width(text) : U32}
  93    count = U32.div(U32.add(U32.mul(length, bits), 31), 32)
  94    words = Array.new(U32, depth(31n, count, 1, 0n), 0)
  95    Sequence{fill(text, bits, 0, 0, 0, words), bits, length}
  97  def copy(sequence: Sequence) -> Sequence & Sequence:
  98    match sequence:
  99      case Sequence{words, +bits, +length}:
 100        apply(Array<U32> & Array<U32>, Sequence & Sequence,
 101          \{Tuple: a => b => (Sequence{a, bits, length}, Sequence{b, bits, length})},
 102          Array.clone(U32, words))
 104  def cursor(sequence: Sequence) -> Cursor:
 105    match sequence:
 106      case Sequence{words, bits, length}:
 107        Cursor{words, bits, 0, 0, 0}
 109  def loaded(words: Array<U32>, +bits: U32, index: U32, +word: U32,
 110             available: U32) -> Cursor & U32:
 111    choose(Cursor & U32, U32.is_eq(bits, 2), \{
 112      True: (
 113        Cursor{words, bits, index, U32.shr(U32.shr(word)), U32.sub(available, 1)},
 114        U32.and(word, 3)
 115      )
 116      False: (
 117        Cursor{words, bits, index,
 118          U32.shr(U32.shr(U32.shr(U32.shr(word)))), U32.sub(available, 1)},
 119        U32.and(word, 15)
 120      )
 121    })
 123  def next(cursor: Cursor) -> Cursor & U32:
 124    match cursor:
 125      case Cursor{words, +bits, +index, word, +available}:
 126        choose(Cursor & U32, U32.is_eq(available, 0), \{
 127          True: apply(Array<U32> & U32, Cursor & U32,
 128            \{Tuple: array => block => loaded(array, bits, U32.add(index, 1), block,
 129              U32.div(32, bits))},
 130            Array.get(U32, words, index))
 131          False: loaded(words, bits, index, word, available)
 132        })
 136  def cursor_skip(fuel: Nat, cursor: Cursor) -> Cursor:
 137    match fuel:
 138      case Zero{}:
 139        cursor
 140      case Succ{rest}:
 141        apply(Cursor & U32, Cursor,
 142          \{Tuple: following => base => cursor_skip(rest, following)},
 143          next(cursor))
 145  def cursor_at(words: Array<U32>, +bits: U32, +position: U32) -> Cursor:
 146    +per_word = {U32.div(32, bits) : U32}
 147    +word_index = {U32.div(position, per_word) : U32}
 148    +offset = {U32.mod(position, per_word) : U32}
 149    cursor_skip(U32.to_nat(offset), Cursor{words, bits, word_index, 0, 0})
 151  def cursor_words(cursor: Cursor) -> Array<U32>:
 152    match cursor:
 153      case Cursor{words, bits, index, word, available}:
 154        words
 156  def reverse(text: String, acc: String) -> String:
 157    match text:
 158      case SNil{}:
 159        acc
 160      case SCon{head, tail}:
 161        reverse(tail, SCon{head, acc})
 163  def unpack_loop(fuel: Nat, cursor: Cursor, acc: String) -> String:
 164    match fuel:
 165      case Zero{}:
 166        reverse(acc, "")
 167      case Succ{rest}:
 168        apply(Cursor & U32, String,
 169          \{Tuple: following => base =>
 170            unpack_loop(rest, following, SCon{Chr{ascii(base)}, acc})},
 171          next(cursor))
 173  def unpack(sequence: Sequence) -> String:
 174    match sequence:
 175      case Sequence{words, bits, length}:
 176        unpack_loop(U32.to_nat(length), Cursor{words, bits, 0, 0, 0}, "")
 180  def signature_loop(fuel: Nat, cursor: Cursor, +rolling: U32, +seen: U32,
 181                     counts: Array<U32>) -> Array<U32>:
 182    match fuel:
 183      case Zero{}:
 184        counts
 185      case Succ{rest}:
 186        apply(Cursor & U32, Array<U32>, \{Tuple: following => +base => (
 187          +word = {U32.mod(U32.add(U32.mul(rolling, 16), base), 4096) : U32}
 188          +bucket = {U32.and(U32.xor(word, U32.shrn(word, 8n)), 255) : U32}
 189          apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>, \{Tuple: next_counts => previous => (
 190            updated = Array.set(U32, next_counts, bucket,
 191              U32.add(previous, choose(U32, U32.is_ge(seen, 2),
 192                \{True: 1; False: 0})))
 193            signature_loop(rest, following, word, U32.add(seen, 1), updated)
 194          )}, Array.get(U32, counts, bucket))
 195        )}, next(cursor))
 197  def signature(sequence: Sequence, +length: U32) -> Array<U32>:
 198    signature_loop(U32.to_nat(length), cursor(sequence), 0, 0,
 199      Array.new(U32, 8n, 0))
 201  def hash30(+word: U32) -> U32:
 202    +a = {U32.xor(word, U32.shrn(word, 16n)) : U32}
 203    +b = {U32.mul(a, 2146121005) : U32}
 204    +c = {U32.xor(b, U32.shrn(b, 15n)) : U32}
 205    +d = {U32.mul(c, 2221713035) : U32}
 206    U32.xor(d, U32.shrn(d, 16n))
 212  def sketch_loop(fuel: Nat, cursor: Cursor, +rolling: U32, +seen: U32,
 213                  values: Array<U32>) -> Array<U32>:
 214    match fuel:
 215      case Zero{}:
 216        values
 217      case Succ{rest}:
 218        apply(Cursor & U32, Array<U32>, \{Tuple: following => +base => (
 219          +word = {U32.and(U32.or(U32.shln(rolling, 2n), U32.and(base, 3)),
 220            1073741823) : U32}
 221          choose(Array<U32>, U32.is_ge(seen, 14), \{
 222            True: (
 223              +hash = {hash30(word) : U32}
 224              +bin = {U32.and(hash, 63) : U32}
 225              +value = {U32.shrn(hash, 6n) : U32}
 226              apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
 227                \{Tuple: next_values => +previous => (
 228                  updated = Array.set(U32, next_values, bin,
 229                    choose(U32, U32.is_lt(value, previous),
 230                      \{True: value; False: previous}))
 231                  sketch_loop(rest, following, word, U32.add(seen, 1), updated)
 232                )},
 233                Array.get(U32, values, bin))
 234            )
 235            False: (
 236              sketch_loop(rest, following, word, U32.add(seen, 1), values)
 237            )
 238          })
 239        )}, next(cursor))
 241  def sketch(sequence: Sequence, +length: U32) -> Array<U32>:
 242    sketch_loop(U32.to_nat(length), cursor(sequence), 0, 0,
 243      Array.new(U32, 6n, 4294967295))
 245  def signature15_loop(fuel: Nat, cursor: Cursor, +rolling: U32, +seen: U32,
 246                       counts: Array<U32>) -> Array<U32>:
 247    match fuel:
 248      case Zero{}:
 249        counts
 250      case Succ{rest}:
 251        apply(Cursor & U32, Array<U32>, \{Tuple: following => +base => (
 252          +word = {U32.and(U32.or(U32.shln(rolling, 2n), U32.and(base, 3)),
 253            1073741823) : U32}
 254          choose(Array<U32>, U32.is_ge(seen, 14), \{
 255            True: (
 256              +bucket = {U32.and(hash30(word), 65535) : U32}
 257              apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
 258                \{Tuple: next_counts => previous => (
 259                  updated = Array.set(U32, next_counts, bucket,
 260                    U32.add(previous, 1))
 261                  signature15_loop(rest, following, word, U32.add(seen, 1), updated)
 262                )},
 263                Array.get(U32, counts, bucket))
 264            )
 265            False: (
 266              signature15_loop(rest, following, word, U32.add(seen, 1), counts)
 267            )
 268          })
 269        )}, next(cursor))
 271  def signature15(sequence: Sequence, +length: U32) -> Array<U32>:
 272    signature15_loop(U32.to_nat(length), cursor(sequence), 0, 0,
 273      Array.new(U32, 16n, 0))

9  import Base
  10  import ./common.bend as C

  14  type Values is Data:
  15    ValuesNil{}
  16    ValuesCon{head: U32, tail: Values}
  18  type FastRecord is Data:
  19    FastRecord{index: U32, width: U32, populated: U32,
  20      words: Values, sketch: Values}
  22  type Posting is Data:
  23    PostingNil{}
  24    PostingCon{record: U32, bin: U32, value: U32, tail: Posting}
  26  type Candidates is Data:
  27    CandidatesNil{}
  28    CandidatesCon{record: U32, tail: Candidates}
  30  type FastDataset is Type:
  31    FastDataset{records: Array<FastRecord>, all_long: Bool}
  33  type IndexState is Type:
  34    IndexState{records: Array<FastRecord>, buckets: Array<Posting>}
  36  type GatherState is Type:
  37    GatherState{buckets: Array<Posting>, counts: Array<U32>,
  38      candidates: Candidates}
  40  type EvaluateState is Type:
  41    EvaluateState{records: Array<FastRecord>, counts: Array<U32>,
  42      rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  44  type BuildState is Type:
  45    BuildState{records: Array<FastRecord>, buckets: Array<Posting>,
  46      counts: Array<U32>, rows: Array<C.Neighbors>}
  51  law values_from_array:
  52    for fuel: Nat
  53    for +index: U32
  54    for values: Array<U32>
  55    Values
  57  def values_from_array(fuel, index, values):
  58    match fuel:
  59      case Zero{}:
  60        ValuesNil{}
  61      case Succ{rest}:
  62        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Values,
  63          \{Tuple: next => value => (
  64            ValuesCon{value, values_from_array(rest, U32.add(index, 1), next)}
  65          )}, Array.get(U32, values, index))
  67  law values_populated:
  68    for values: Values
  69    for +total: U32
  70    U32
  72  def values_populated(values, total):
  73    match values:
  74      case ValuesNil{}:
  75        total
  76      case ValuesCon{+head, tail}:
  77        values_populated(tail, U32.add(total,
  78          C.select(U32.is_ne(head, 4294967295), 1, 0)))
  80  law fast_record:
  81    for item: C.Record
  82    FastRecord
  84  def fast_record(item):
  85    match item:
  86      case C.Record{index, label, bases, width, words, sketch}:
  87        +sketch_values = {values_from_array(64n, 0, sketch) : Values}
  88        FastRecord{index, width, values_populated(sketch_values, 0),
  89          values_from_array(256n, 0, words), sketch_values}
  91  law records_fill:
  92    for items: List<C.Record>
  93    for records: Array<FastRecord>
  94    for +all_long: Bool
  95    FastDataset
  97  law records_fill_item:
  98    for item: FastRecord
  99    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 100    for +all_long: Bool
 101    for continue: Array<FastRecord> -> Bool -> FastDataset
 102    FastDataset
 104  def records_fill_item(item, records, all_long, continue):
 105    match item:
 106      case FastRecord{+index, +width, +populated, +words, +sketch}:
 107        next = Array.set(FastRecord, records, index,
 108          FastRecord{index, width, populated, words, sketch})
 109        continue(next, all_long && U32.is_ge(width, 15))
 111  def records_fill(items, records, all_long):
 112    match items:
 113      case Nil{}:
 114        FastDataset{records, all_long}
 115      case Con{head, tail}:
 116        records_fill_item(fast_record(head), records, all_long,
 117          next_records => next_long =>
 118            records_fill(tail, next_records, next_long))
 120  law records_table:
 121    for items: List<C.Record>
 122    for +size: U32
 123    FastDataset
 125  def records_table(items, size):
 126    records_fill(items,
 127      Array.new(FastRecord, C.array_depth(31n, size, 1, 0n),
 128        FastRecord{0, 0, 0, ValuesNil{}, ValuesNil{}}), True{})
 130  law records_all_long:
 131    for items: List<C.Record>
 132    List<C.Record> & Bool
 134  def records_all_long(items):
 135    match items:
 136      case Nil{}:
 137        (Nil{}, True{})
 138      case Con{head, tail}:
 139        match head:
 140          case C.Record{index, label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
 141            C.apply(List<C.Record> & Bool, List<C.Record> & Bool,
 142              \{Tuple: rest => long => (
 143                (Con{C.Record{index, label, bases, width, words, sketch}, rest},
 144                 U32.is_ge(width, 15) && long)
 145              )}, records_all_long(tail))
 150  law posting_bucket:
 151    for +bin: U32
 152    for +value: U32
 153    U32
 155  def posting_bucket(bin, value):
 156    U32.and(P.hash30(U32.xor(value, U32.mul(bin, 2654435761))), 65535)
 158  law index_sketch:
 159    for sketch: Values
 160    for +bin: U32
 161    for +record: U32
 162    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 163    Array<Posting>
 165  def index_sketch(sketch, bin, record, buckets):
 166    match sketch:
 167      case ValuesNil{}:
 168        buckets
 169      case ValuesCon{+value, tail}:
 170        C.branch(Array<Posting>, U32.is_eq(value, 4294967295), \{
 171          True: index_sketch(tail, U32.add(bin, 1), record, buckets)
 172          False: (
 173            +bucket = {posting_bucket(bin, value) : U32}
 174            C.apply(Array<Posting> & Posting, Array<Posting>,
 175              \{Tuple: next => postings => (
 176                updated = Array.set(Posting, next, bucket,
 177                  PostingCon{record, bin, value, postings})
 178                index_sketch(tail, U32.add(bin, 1), record, updated)
 179              )}, Array.get(Posting, buckets, bucket))
 180          )
 181        })
 183  law index_records:
 184    for fuel: Nat
 185    for +index: U32
 186    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 187    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 188    IndexState
 190  law index_record:
 191    for item: FastRecord
 192    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 193    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 194    for continue: Array<FastRecord> -> Array<Posting> -> IndexState
 195    IndexState
 197  def index_record(item, records, buckets, continue):
 198    match item:
 199      case FastRecord{+record, +width, +populated, +words, +sketch}:
 200        updated = index_sketch(sketch, 0, record, buckets)
 201        continue(records, updated)
 203  def index_records(fuel, index, records, buckets):
 204    match fuel:
 205      case Zero{}:
 206        IndexState{records, buckets}
 207      case Succ{rest}:
 208        C.apply(Array<FastRecord> & FastRecord, IndexState,
 209          \{Tuple: next_records => item => (
 210            index_record(item, next_records, buckets,
 211              final_records => final_buckets =>
 212                index_records(rest, U32.add(index, 1), final_records,
 213                  final_buckets))
 214          )}, Array.get(FastRecord, records, index))
 219  law postings_gather:
 220    for postings: Posting
 221    for +query: U32
 222    for +bin: U32
 223    for +value: U32
 224    for counts: Array<U32>
 225    for candidates: Candidates
 226    Array<U32> & Candidates
 228  def postings_gather(postings, query, bin, value, counts, candidates):
 229    match postings:
 230      case PostingNil{}:
 231        (counts, candidates)
 232      case PostingCon{+record, +entry_bin, +entry_value, tail}:
 233        +same = {U32.is_gt(record, query) && U32.is_eq(entry_bin, bin)
 234          && U32.is_eq(entry_value, value) : Bool}
 235        C.branch(Array<U32> & Candidates, same, \{
 236          False: postings_gather(tail, query, bin, value, counts, candidates)
 237          True: (
 238            C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & Candidates,
 239              \{Tuple: next => +count => (
 240                updated = Array.set(U32, next, record, U32.add(count, 1))
 241                postings_gather(tail, query, bin, value, updated,
 242                  C.branch(Candidates, U32.is_eq(count, 0), \{
 243                    True: CandidatesCon{record, candidates}
 244                    False: candidates
 245                  }))
 246              )}, Array.get(U32, counts, record))
 247          )
 248        })
 250  law gather_sketch:
 251    for sketch: Values
 252    for +bin: U32
 253    for +query: U32
 254    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 255    for counts: Array<U32>
 256    for candidates: Candidates
 257    GatherState
 259  def gather_sketch(sketch, bin, query, buckets, counts, candidates):
 260    match sketch:
 261      case ValuesNil{}:
 262        GatherState{buckets, counts, candidates}
 263      case ValuesCon{+value, tail}:
 264        C.branch(GatherState, U32.is_eq(value, 4294967295), \{
 265          True: gather_sketch(tail, U32.add(bin, 1), query,
 266            buckets, counts, candidates)
 267          False: (
 268            +bucket = {posting_bucket(bin, value) : U32}
 269            C.apply(Array<Posting> & Posting, GatherState,
 270              \{Tuple: next_buckets => postings => (
 271                C.apply(Array<U32> & Candidates, GatherState,
 272                  \{Tuple: next_counts => next_candidates => (
 273                    gather_sketch(tail, U32.add(bin, 1), query,
 274                      next_buckets, next_counts, next_candidates)
 275                  )}, postings_gather(postings, query, bin, value,
 276                    counts, candidates))
 277              )}, Array.get(Posting, buckets, bucket))
 278          )
 279        })
 284  law values_common:
 285    for left: Values
 286    for right: Values
 287    for +total: U32
 288    U32
 290  def values_common(left, right, total):
 291    match left right:
 292      case ValuesNil{} remaining:
 293        total
 294      case ValuesCon{head, tail} ValuesNil{}:
 295        total
 296      case ValuesCon{+left_head, left_tail} ValuesCon{+right_head, right_tail}:
 297        values_common(left_tail, right_tail,
 298          U32.add(total, C.minimum(left_head, right_head)))
 300  law values_word_candidate:
 301    for left: Values
 302    for right: Values
 303    for +left_size: U32
 304    for +right_size: U32
 305    for +required_matches: U32
 306    Bool
 308  def values_word_candidate(left, right, left_size, right_size, required_matches):
 309    interruptions = C.subtract(U32.add(left_size, right_size),
 310      U32.mul(required_matches, 2))
 311    +required_words = {C.subtract(required_matches,
 312      U32.mul(2, U32.add(interruptions, 1))) : U32}
 313    C.branch(Bool, U32.is_eq(required_words, 0), \{
 314      True: True{}
 315      False: U32.is_ge(values_common(left, right, 0), required_words)
 316    })
 318  law values_global_filter:
 319    for left: FastRecord
 320    for right: FastRecord
 321    for +threshold: U32
 322    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 323    Bool
 325  def values_global_filter(left, right, threshold, shorter_mode):
 326    match left right:
 327      case FastRecord{li, +left_size, lp, +left_words, ls} FastRecord{ri, +right_size, rp, +right_words, rs}:
 328        +longer = {C.maximum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
 329        +shorter = {C.minimum(left_size, right_size) : U32}
 330        +normalizer = {C.branch(U32, shorter_mode, \{
 331          True: shorter
 332          False: longer
 333        }) : U32}
 334        +required = {U32.div(U32.add(U32.mul(threshold, normalizer), 9999), 10000)
 335          : U32}
 336        C.branch(Bool, U32.is_gt(required, shorter), \{
 337          True: False{}
 338          False: values_word_candidate(left_words, right_words,
 339            left_size, right_size, required)
 340        })

 346    U32
 348  def indexed_score(common, populated, threshold):
 349    F.indexed_score(common, populated, threshold)
 354  law indexed_score_agrees:
 355    for +common: U32
 356    for +populated: U32
 357    for +threshold: U32
 358    {indexed_score(common, populated, threshold)
 359      == F.score(common, populated, threshold) : U32}
 361  def indexed_score_agrees(common, populated, threshold):
 362    {==}
 366  law zero_common_is_rejected:
 367    for +populated: U32
 368    for +threshold: U32
 369    {indexed_score(0, populated, threshold) == 0 : U32}
 371  def zero_common_is_rejected(populated, threshold):
 372    {==}
 374  law candidate_evaluate:
 375    for candidates: Candidates
 376    for +query: FastRecord
 377    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 378    for counts: Array<U32>
 379    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 380    for +threshold: U32
 381    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 382    EvaluateState
 384  law candidate_compare:
 385    for query: FastRecord
 386    for other: FastRecord
 387    for +common: U32
 388    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 389    for counts: Array<U32>
 390    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 391    for +threshold: U32
 392    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 393    for continue: FastRecord -> Array<FastRecord> -> Array<U32> ->
 394      Array<C.Neighbors> -> EvaluateState
 395    EvaluateState
 397  def candidate_compare(query, other, common, records, counts, rows,
 398                        threshold, shorter_mode, continue):
 399    match query other:
 400      case FastRecord{+query_index, +query_width, +query_populated,
 401        +query_words, +query_sketch}
 402        FastRecord{+other_index, +other_width, +other_populated,
 403          +other_words, +other_sketch}:
 404        +populated = {C.minimum(query_populated, other_populated) : U32}
 405        +score = {indexed_score(common, populated, threshold) : U32}
 406        +accepted = {U32.is_ge(score, threshold)
 407          && values_global_filter(
 408            FastRecord{query_index, query_width, query_populated,
 409              query_words, query_sketch},
 410            FastRecord{other_index, other_width, other_populated,
 411              other_words, other_sketch}, threshold, shorter_mode) : Bool}
 412        updated = C.branch(Array<C.Neighbors>, accepted, \{
 413          True: C.sparse_add(rows, query_index, other_index, score)
 414          False: rows
 415        })
 416        continue(
 417          FastRecord{query_index, query_width, query_populated,
 418            query_words, query_sketch}, records, counts, updated)
 420  def candidate_evaluate(candidates, query, records, counts, rows, threshold,
 421                         shorter_mode):
 422    match candidates:
 423      case CandidatesNil{}:
 424        EvaluateState{records, counts, rows}
 425      case CandidatesCon{+target, tail}:
 426        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32,
 427          EvaluateState,
 428          \{Tuple: next_counts => +common => (
 429            reset = Array.set(U32, next_counts, target, 0)
 430            C.apply(Array<FastRecord> & FastRecord,
 431              EvaluateState,
 432              \{Tuple: next_records => +other => (
 433                candidate_compare(query, other, common, next_records,
 434                  reset, rows, threshold, shorter_mode,
 435                  next_query => final_records => final_counts => final_rows =>
 436                    candidate_evaluate(tail, next_query, final_records,
 437                      final_counts, final_rows, threshold, shorter_mode))
 438              )}, Array.get(FastRecord, records, target))
 439          )}, Array.get(U32, counts, target))
 441  law build_rows:
 442    for fuel: Nat
 443    for +index: U32
 444    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 445    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 446    for counts: Array<U32>
 447    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 448    for +threshold: U32
 449    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 450    BuildState
 452  law build_gathered:
 453    for +query: FastRecord
 454    for gathered: GatherState
 455    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 456    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 457    for +threshold: U32
 458    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 459    for continue: Array<FastRecord> -> Array<Posting> -> Array<U32> ->
 460      Array<C.Neighbors> -> BuildState
 461    BuildState
 463  law build_evaluated:
 464    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 465    for evaluated: EvaluateState
 466    for continue: Array<FastRecord> -> Array<Posting> -> Array<U32> ->
 467      Array<C.Neighbors> -> BuildState
 468    BuildState
 470  def build_evaluated(buckets, evaluated, continue):
 471    match evaluated:
 472      case EvaluateState{records, counts, rows}:
 473        continue(records, buckets, counts, rows)
 475  def build_gathered(query, gathered, records, rows, threshold, shorter_mode,
 476                     continue):
 477    match gathered:
 478      case GatherState{next_buckets, next_counts, candidates}:
 479        build_evaluated(next_buckets,
 480          candidate_evaluate(candidates, query, records, next_counts,
 481            rows, threshold, shorter_mode), continue)
 483  law build_row:
 484    for query: FastRecord
 485    for records: Array<FastRecord>
 486    for buckets: Array<Posting>
 487    for counts: Array<U32>
 488    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 489    for +threshold: U32
 490    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 491    for continue: Array<FastRecord> -> Array<Posting> -> Array<U32> ->
 492      Array<C.Neighbors> -> BuildState
 493    BuildState
 495  def build_row(query, records, buckets, counts, rows, threshold, shorter_mode,
 496                continue):
 497    match query:
 498      case FastRecord{+query_index, +query_width, +query_populated,
 499        +query_words, +query_sketch}:
 500        +saved = {FastRecord{query_index, query_width, query_populated,
 501          query_words, query_sketch} : FastRecord}
 502        build_gathered(saved,
 503          gather_sketch(query_sketch, 0, query_index, buckets, counts,
 504            CandidatesNil{}), records, rows, threshold, shorter_mode, continue)
 506  def build_rows(fuel, index, records, buckets, counts, rows, threshold,
 507                 shorter_mode):
 508    match fuel:
 509      case Zero{}:
 510        BuildState{records, buckets, counts, rows}
 511      case Succ{rest}:
 512        C.apply(Array<FastRecord> & FastRecord, BuildState,
 513          \{Tuple: next_records => +query => (
 514            build_row(query, next_records, buckets, counts, rows, threshold,
 515              shorter_mode,
 516              final_records => final_buckets => final_counts => final_rows =>
 517                build_rows(rest, U32.add(index, 1), final_records,
 518                  final_buckets, final_counts, final_rows, threshold,
 519                  shorter_mode))
 520          )}, Array.get(FastRecord, records, index))

 539  law indexed_graph_indexed:
 540    for indexed: IndexState
 541    for +size: U32
 542    for +threshold: U32
 543    for +shorter_mode: Bool
 544    C.SparseGraph
 546  def indexed_graph_indexed(indexed, size, threshold, shorter_mode):
 547    match indexed:
 548      case IndexState{next_records, buckets}:
 549        indexed_graph_built(
 550          build_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, next_records, buckets,
 551            C.array_new(size, 0),
 552            Array.new(C.Neighbors, C.array_depth(31n, size, 1, 0n),
 553              C.NeighborsNil{}), threshold, shorter_mode), size)
