 556    match dataset:
 557      case FastDataset{records, all_long}:
 558        indexed_graph_indexed(index_records(C.to_nat(size), 0, records,
 559          Array.new(Posting, 16n, PostingNil{})), size, threshold, shorter_mode)
 561  law sparse_build:
 562    for items: List<C.Record>
 563    for +size: U32
 564    for +threshold: U32
 565    for +reverse: Bool
 566    for +fast: Bool
 567    C.SparseGraph
 569  law sparse_choose:
 570    for dataset: FastDataset
 571    for fallback: List<C.Record>
 572    for +size: U32
 573    for +threshold: U32
 574    C.SparseGraph
 576  def sparse_choose(dataset, fallback, size, threshold):
 577    match dataset:
 578      case FastDataset{records, +all_long}:
 579        C.branch(C.SparseGraph, all_long, \{
 580          True: indexed_graph(FastDataset{records, all_long}, size, threshold,
 581            False{})
 582          False: C.sparse_build(fallback, size, threshold, False{}, True{})
 583        })
 585  law sparse_fast:
 586    for indexed_items: List<C.Record>
 587    for fallback: List<C.Record>
 588    for +size: U32
 589    for +threshold: U32
 590    C.SparseGraph
 592  def sparse_fast(indexed_items, fallback, size, threshold):
 593    sparse_choose(records_table(indexed_items, size), fallback, size, threshold)
 598  law sparse_build_shorter_long:
 599    for items: List<C.Record>
 600    for +size: U32
 601    for +threshold: U32
 602    C.SparseGraph
 604  def sparse_build_shorter_long(items, size, threshold):
 605    indexed_graph(records_table(items, size), size, threshold, True{})
 607  def sparse_build(items, size, threshold, reverse, fast):
 608    C.branch(C.SparseGraph, fast && Bool.not(reverse), \{
 609      False: C.sparse_build(items, size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 610      True: C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, C.SparseGraph,
 611        \{Tuple: indexed_items => fallback => (
 612          sparse_fast(indexed_items, fallback, size, threshold)
 613        )}, C.records_copy(items))
 614    })
lz_ani.bend
3  import Base
   4  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   5  import ./lib/lz_ani_indexed.bend as LZ
   7  law emit_score:
   8    for result: LZ.ScoreResult
   9    for +query_index: U32
  10    for +reference_index: U32
  11    IO(LZ.ReferenceIndex)
  13  def emit_score(result, query_index, reference_index):
  14    match result:
  15      case LZ.ScoreResult{index,
  16        LZ.Metrics{matches, mismatches, components, ani, gani, coverage}}:
  17        do IO<LZ.ReferenceIndex>:
  18          printed: Unit <- IO.print(U32.show(query_index) ++ "\t"
  19            ++ U32.show(reference_index) ++ "\t" ++ U32.show(matches) ++ "\t"
  20            ++ U32.show(mismatches) ++ "\t" ++ U32.show(components) ++ "\t"
  21            ++ U32.show(ani) ++ "\t" ++ U32.show(gani) ++ "\t"
  22            ++ U32.show(coverage))
  23          return index
  25  law score_row:
  26    for items: List<C.Record>
  27    for index: LZ.ReferenceIndex
  28    for +reference_index: U32
  29    IO(Unit)
  31  def score_row(items, index, reference_index):
  32    match items:
  33      case Nil{}:
  34        IO.pure(Unit, Unit{})
  35      case Con{head, tail}:
  36        match head:
  37          case C.Record{+query_index, label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
  38            C.branch(IO(Unit), U32.is_eq(query_index, reference_index), \{
  39              True: score_row(tail, index, reference_index)
  40              False: do IO<Unit>:
  41                next: LZ.ReferenceIndex <- emit_score(
  42                  LZ.score(index, bases, width), query_index, reference_index)
  43                score_row(tail, next, reference_index)
  44            })
  46  law run_reference:
  47    for reference: C.Record
  48    for queries: List<C.Record>
  49    IO(Unit)
  51  def run_reference(reference, queries):
  52    match reference:
  53      case C.Record{+index, label, bases, +width, words, sketch}:
  54        score_row(queries, LZ.build_index(bases, width), index)
  56  law run_rows:
  57    for rows: List<C.Record>
  58    for all: List<C.Record>
  59    IO(Unit)
  61  def run_rows(rows, all):
  62    match rows:
  63      case Nil{}:
  64        IO.pure(Unit, Unit{})
  65      case Con{head, tail}:
  66        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
  67          \{Tuple: queries => next_all => (
  68            do IO<Unit>:
  69              row: Unit <- run_reference(head, queries)
  70              run_rows(tail, next_all)
  71          )}, C.records_copy(all))
  73  law run_dataset:
  74    for dataset: C.Dataset
  75    IO(Unit)
  77  def run_dataset(dataset):
  78    match dataset:
  79      case C.Dataset{items, count}:
  80        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
  81          \{Tuple: rows => all => (
  82            do IO<Unit>:
  83              header: Unit <- IO.print(
  84                "qidx\tridx\tnt_match\tnt_mismatch\tnum_alns\tani\tgani\tqcov")
  85              run_rows(rows, all)
  86          )}, C.records_copy(items))
  88  law main:
  89    IO(Unit)
  91  def main():
  92    do IO<Unit>:
  93      dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(1)
  94      run_dataset(dataset)
set_cover.bend
4  import Base
   5  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   6  import ./lib/vclust_index.bend as V
   7  import ./lib/lz_ani_graph.bend as LZG
   9  law count_row:
  10    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  11    for labels: Array<U32>
  12    for +sentinel: U32
  13    for count: U32
  14    (C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32
  16  def count_row(neighbors, labels, sentinel, count):
  17    match neighbors:
  18      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  19        ((C.NeighborsNil{}, labels), count)
  20      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, score, tail}:
  21        +target = {raw_target : U32}
  22        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, (C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32,
  23          \{Tuple: next_labels => label => (
  24            C.apply((C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32,
  25              (C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32,
  26              \{Tuple: \{Tuple: rest => final_labels => total => (
  27                ((C.NeighborsCon{target, score, rest}, final_labels), total)
  28              )}},
  29              count_row(tail, next_labels, sentinel,
  30                U32.add(count, C.select(U32.is_eq(label, sentinel), 1, 0))))
  31          )},
  32          Array.get(U32, labels, target))
  34  law choose_center:
  35    for fuel: Nat
  36    for +index: U32
  37    for +size: U32
  38    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
  39    for labels: Array<U32>
  40    for +best: U32
  41    for +maximum: U32
  42    Pair(Pair(Array<C.Neighbors>, Array<U32>), Pair(U32, U32))
  44  def choose_center(fuel, index, size, rows, labels, best, maximum):
  45    match fuel:
  46      case Zero{}:
  47        ((rows, labels), (best, maximum))
  48      case Succ{rest}:
  49        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32,
  50          Pair(Pair(Array<C.Neighbors>, Array<U32>), Pair(U32, U32)),
  51          \{Tuple: next_labels => label => (
  52            C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors,
  53              Pair(Pair(Array<C.Neighbors>, Array<U32>), Pair(U32, U32)),
  54              \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
  55                counted = C.branch((C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32,
  56                  U32.is_eq(label, size), \{
  57                  True: (
  58                    count_row(neighbors, next_labels, size, 1)
  59                  )
  60                  False: (
  61                    ((neighbors, next_labels), 0)
  62                  )
  63                })
  64                C.apply((C.Neighbors & Array<U32>) & U32,
  65                  Pair(Pair(Array<C.Neighbors>, Array<U32>), Pair(U32, U32)),
  66                  \{Tuple: \{Tuple: rebuilt => updated_labels => raw_count => (
  67                    +count = {raw_count : U32}
  68                    updated_rows = Array.set(C.Neighbors, next_rows, index, rebuilt)
  69                    chosen = C.select(U32.is_gt(count, maximum), index, best)
  70                    choose_center(rest, U32.add(index, 1), size, updated_rows,
  71                      updated_labels, chosen, C.maximum(maximum, count))
  72                  )}},
  73                  counted)
  74              )},
  75              Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, index))
  76          )},
  77          Array.get(U32, labels, index))
  79  law assign_row:
  80    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  81    for labels: Array<U32>
  82    for +center: U32
  83    for +sentinel: U32
  84    C.Neighbors & Array<U32>
  86  def assign_row(neighbors, labels, center, sentinel):
  87    match neighbors:
  88      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  89        (C.NeighborsNil{}, labels)
  90      case C.NeighborsCon{raw_target, score, tail}:
  91        +target = {raw_target : U32}
  92        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, C.Neighbors & Array<U32>,
  93          \{Tuple: next_labels => raw_label => (
  94            +label = {raw_label : U32}
  95            updated = Array.set(U32, next_labels, target,
  96              C.select(U32.is_eq(label, sentinel), center, label))
  97            C.apply(C.Neighbors & Array<U32>, C.Neighbors & Array<U32>,
  98              \{Tuple: rest => final_labels => (
  99                (C.NeighborsCon{target, score, rest}, final_labels)
 100              )},
 101              assign_row(tail, updated, center, sentinel))
 102          )},
 103          Array.get(U32, labels, target))
 105  law assign_center:
 106    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 107    for labels: Array<U32>
 108    for +center: U32
 109    for +size: U32
 110    Array<C.Neighbors> & Array<U32>
 112  def assign_center(rows, labels, center, size):
 113    marked = Array.set(U32, labels, center, center)
 114    C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors,
 115      Array<C.Neighbors> & Array<U32>,
 116      \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
 117        C.apply(C.Neighbors & Array<U32>,
 118          Array<C.Neighbors> & Array<U32>,
 119          \{Tuple: rebuilt => updated_labels => (
 120            (Array.set(C.Neighbors, next_rows, center, rebuilt), updated_labels)
 121          )},
 122          assign_row(neighbors, marked, center, size))
 123      )},
 124      Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, center))
 126  law cover_step:
 127    for +count: U32
 128    for center: U32
 129    for +size: U32
 130    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 131    for labels: Array<U32>
 132    for continue: Array<C.Neighbors> -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32>
 133    Array<U32>
 135  def cover_step(count, center, size, rows, labels, continue):
 136    C.branch(Array<U32>, U32.is_eq(count, 0), \{
 137      True: (
 138        labels
 139      )
 140      False: (
 141        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & Array<U32>, Array<U32>,
 142          \{Tuple: next_rows => next_labels => (
 143            continue(next_rows, next_labels)
 144          )},
 145          assign_center(rows, labels, center, size))
 146      )
 147    })
 149  law cover:
 150    for fuel: Nat
 151    for +size: U32
 152    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
 153    for labels: Array<U32>
 154    Array<U32>
 156  def cover(fuel, size, rows, labels):
 157    match fuel:
 158      case Zero{}:
 159        labels
 160      case Succ{rest}:
 161        C.apply(Pair(Pair(Array<C.Neighbors>, Array<U32>), Pair(U32, U32)),
 162          Array<U32>,
 163          \{Tuple: \{Tuple: next_rows => next_labels => \{Tuple: center => count => (
 164            cover_step(count, center, size, next_rows, next_labels,
 165              r => l => cover(rest, size, r, l))
 166          )}}},
 167          choose_center(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, rows, labels, 0, 0))
 169  law cluster:
 170    for graph: C.SparseGraph
 171    for +size: U32
 172    for threshold: U32
 173    for resolution: U32
 174    for seed: U32
 175    for passes: Nat
 176    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 178  def cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes):
 179    match graph:
 180      case C.SparseGraph{raw_size, rows}:
 181        Done{cover(C.to_nat(size), size, rows, C.array_new(size, size))}
 183  law run_dataset:
 184    for dataset: C.Dataset
 185    for +threshold: U32
 186    for reverse: Bool
 187    for fast: Bool
 188    for lz: Bool
 189    for kmer_minimum: U32
 190    for kmer_identity: U32
 191    for resolution: U32
 192    for seed: U32
 193    for passes: Nat
 194    IO(Unit)
 196  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 197                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
 198    match dataset:
 199      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
 200        +size = {raw_size : U32}
 201        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
 202          \{Tuple: input => output => (
 203            graph = C.branch(C.SparseGraph, lz, \{
 204              True: LZG.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, kmer_minimum,
 205                kmer_identity)
 206              False: V.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 207            })
 208            do IO<Unit>:
 209              labels: Array<U32> <- C.result_unwrap(Array<U32>,
 210                cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes))
 211              C.output(output, labels)
 212          )},
 213          C.records_copy(items))
 215  law run_config:
 216    for config: C.Config
 217    IO(Unit)
 219  def run_config(config):
 220    match config:
 221      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 222                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
 223        do IO<Unit>:
 224          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
 225          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 226            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
 228  law main:
 229    IO(Unit)
 231  def main():
 232    do IO<Unit>:
 233      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
 234      run_config(config)
single_linkage.bend
3  import Base
   4  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   5  import ./lib/vclust_index.bend as V
   6  import ./lib/lz_ani_graph.bend as LZG
   8  law find_root:
   9    for fuel: Nat
  10    for parents: Array<U32>
  11    for +node: U32
  12    Array<U32> & U32
  14  def find_root(fuel, parents, node):
  15    match fuel:
  16      case Zero{}:
  17        (parents, node)
  18      case Succ{rest}:
  19        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32> & U32,
  20          \{Tuple: next => raw_parent => (
  21            +parent = {raw_parent : U32}
  22            C.branch(Array<U32> & U32, U32.is_eq(parent, node), \{
  23              True: (
  24                (next, node)
  25              )
  26              False: (
  27                find_root(rest, next, parent)
  28              )
  29            })
  30          )},
  31          Array.get(U32, parents, node))
  33  law union:
  34    for parents: Array<U32>
  35    for +size: U32
  36    for left: U32
  37    for right: U32
  38    Array<U32>
  40  def union(parents, size, left, right):
  41    C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
  42      \{Tuple: next => raw_left => (
  43        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
  44          \{Tuple: updated => raw_right => (
  45            +a = {raw_left : U32}
  46            +b = {raw_right : U32}
  47            Array.set(U32, updated, C.maximum(a, b), C.minimum(a, b))
  48          )},
  49          find_root(C.to_nat(size), next, right))
  50      )},
  51      find_root(C.to_nat(size), parents, left))
  53  law scan_neighbors:
  54    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  55    for +row: U32
  56    for +size: U32
  57    for parents: Array<U32>
  58    Array<U32>
  60  def scan_neighbors(neighbors, row, size, parents):
  61    match neighbors:
  62      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  63        parents
  64      case C.NeighborsCon{column, score, tail}:
  65        scan_neighbors(tail, row, size, union(parents, size, row, column))
  67  law scan_rows:
  68    for fuel: Nat
  69    for +row: U32
  70    for +size: U32
  71    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
  72    for parents: Array<U32>
  73    Array<U32>
  75  def scan_rows(fuel, row, size, rows, parents):
  76    match fuel:
  77      case Zero{}:
  78        parents
  79      case Succ{rest}:
  80        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, Array<U32>,
  81          \{Tuple: next => neighbors => (
  82            updated = scan_neighbors(neighbors, row, size, parents)
  83            scan_rows(rest, U32.add(row, 1), size, next, updated)
  84          )},
  85          Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, row))
  87  law flatten:
  88    for fuel: Nat
  89    for +index: U32
  90    for +size: U32
  91    for parents: Array<U32>
  92    for labels: Array<U32>
  93    Array<U32>
  95  def flatten(fuel, index, size, parents, labels):
  96    match fuel:
  97      case Zero{}:
  98        labels
  99      case Succ{rest}:
 100        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, Array<U32>,
 101          \{Tuple: next => root => (
 102            updated = Array.set(U32, labels, index, root)
 103            flatten(rest, U32.add(index, 1), size, next, updated)
 104          )},
 105          find_root(C.to_nat(size), parents, index))
 107  law cluster:
 108    for graph: C.SparseGraph
 109    for +size: U32
 110    for threshold: U32
 111    for resolution: U32
 112    for seed: U32
 113    for passes: Nat
 114    Result<String, Array<U32>>
 116  def cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes):
 117    match graph:
 118      case C.SparseGraph{raw_size, rows}:
 119        parents = scan_rows(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, rows, C.labels_identity(size))
 120        Done{flatten(C.to_nat(size), 0, size, parents, C.array_new(size, 0))}
 122  law run_dataset:
 123    for dataset: C.Dataset
 124    for +threshold: U32
 125    for reverse: Bool
 126    for fast: Bool
 127    for lz: Bool
 128    for kmer_minimum: U32
 129    for kmer_identity: U32
 130    for resolution: U32
 131    for seed: U32
 132    for passes: Nat
 133    IO(Unit)
 135  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 136                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
 137    match dataset:
 138      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
 139        +size = {raw_size : U32}
 140        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
 141          \{Tuple: input => output => (
 142            graph = C.branch(C.SparseGraph, lz, \{
 143              True: LZG.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, kmer_minimum,
 144                kmer_identity)
 145              False: V.sparse_build(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 146            })
 147            do IO<Unit>:
 148              labels: Array<U32> <- C.result_unwrap(Array<U32>,
 149                cluster(graph, size, threshold, resolution, seed, passes))
 150              C.output(output, labels)
 151          )},
 152          C.records_copy(items))
 154  law run_config:
 155    for config: C.Config
 156    IO(Unit)
 158  def run_config(config):
 159    match config:
 160      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 161                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
 162        do IO<Unit>:
 163          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
 164          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 165            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
 167  law main:
 168    IO(Unit)
 170  def main():
 171    do IO<Unit>:
 172      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
 173      run_config(config)
uclust.bend
3  import Base
   4  import ./lib/common.bend as C
   5  import ./lib/vclust_index.bend as V
   6  import ./lib/lz_ani_graph.bend as LZG
   8  type IndexedChoice is Type:
   9    IndexedChoice{active: Array<U32>, center: U32, score: U32}
  11  type IndexedState is Type:
  12    IndexedState{rows: Array<C.Neighbors>, active: Array<U32>,
  13      labels: Array<U32>}
  17  law indexed_find:
  18    for neighbors: C.Neighbors
  19    for active: Array<U32>
  20    for +size: U32
  21    for +best: U32
  22    for +best_score: U32
  23    IndexedChoice
  25  def indexed_find(neighbors, active, size, best, best_score):
  26    match neighbors:
  27      case C.NeighborsNil{}:
  28        IndexedChoice{active, best, best_score}
  29      case C.NeighborsCon{+column, +score, tail}:
  30        C.apply(Array<U32> & U32, IndexedChoice,
  31          \{Tuple: next_active => +is_active => (
  32            +better = {U32.is_eq(is_active, 1) &&
  33              (U32.is_gt(score, best_score) ||
  34                (U32.is_eq(score, best_score) && U32.is_lt(column, best)))
  35              : Bool}
  36            C.branch(IndexedChoice, better, \{
  37              True: indexed_find(tail, next_active, size, column, score)
  38              False: indexed_find(tail, next_active, size, best, best_score)
  39            })
  40          )}, Array.get(U32, active, column))
  42  law indexed_continue:
  43    for choice: IndexedChoice
  44    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
  45    for labels: Array<U32>
  46    for +index: U32
  47    for +size: U32
  48    for continue: Array<C.Neighbors> -> Array<U32> -> Array<U32> -> IndexedState
  49    IndexedState
  51  def indexed_continue(choice, rows, labels, index, size, continue):
  52    match choice:
  53      case IndexedChoice{active, +center, score}:
  54        C.branch(IndexedState, U32.is_eq(center, size), \{
  55          True: continue(rows, Array.set(U32, active, index, 1),
  56            Array.set(U32, labels, index, index))
  57          False: continue(rows, active, Array.set(U32, labels, index, center))
  58        })
  60  law indexed_greedy:
  61    for items: List<C.Record>
  62    for rows: Array<C.Neighbors>
  63    for active: Array<U32>
  64    for labels: Array<U32>
  65    for +size: U32
  66    IndexedState
  68  def indexed_greedy(items, rows, active, labels, size):
  69    match items:
  70      case Nil{}:
  71        IndexedState{rows, active, labels}
  72      case Con{head, tail}:
  73        +index = {C.record_index(head) : U32}
  74        C.apply(Array<C.Neighbors> & C.Neighbors, IndexedState,
  75          \{Tuple: next_rows => neighbors => (
  76            indexed_continue(indexed_find(neighbors, active, size, size, 0),
  77              next_rows, labels, index, size,
  78              final_rows => final_active => final_labels =>
  79                indexed_greedy(tail, final_rows, final_active, final_labels,
  80                  size))
  81          )}, Array.get(C.Neighbors, rows, index))
  83  law indexed_labels:
  84    for state: IndexedState
  85    Array<U32>
  87  def indexed_labels(state):
  88    match state:
  89      case IndexedState{rows, active, labels}:
  90        labels
  92  law indexed_cluster:
  93    for items: List<C.Record>
  94    for +size: U32
  95    for +threshold: U32
  96    Array<U32>
  98  law indexed_graph_cluster:
  99    for graph: C.SparseGraph
 100    for order: List<C.Record>
 101    for +size: U32
 102    Array<U32>
 104  def indexed_graph_cluster(graph, order, size):
 105    match graph:
 106      case C.SparseGraph{raw_size, rows}:
 107        indexed_labels(indexed_greedy(C.records_sort(order), rows,
 108          C.array_new(size, 0), C.array_new(size, 0), size))
 110  def indexed_cluster(items, size, threshold):
 111    C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, Array<U32>,
 112      \{Tuple: order => graph_items => (
 113        indexed_graph_cluster(
 114          V.sparse_build(graph_items, size, threshold, False{}, True{}),
 115          order, size)
 116      )}, C.records_copy(items))
 118  law lz_cluster:
 119    for items: List<C.Record>
 120    for +size: U32
 121    for +threshold: U32
 122    for +kmer_minimum: U32
 123    for +kmer_identity: U32
 124    Array<U32>
 126  def lz_cluster(items, size, threshold, kmer_minimum, kmer_identity):
 127    C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, Array<U32>,
 128      \{Tuple: order => graph_items => (
 129        indexed_graph_cluster(LZG.sparse_build(graph_items, size, threshold,
 130          kmer_minimum, kmer_identity), order, size)
 131      )}, C.records_copy(items))
 133  law score_pair:
 134    for left: C.Record
 135    for right: C.Record
 136    for reverse: Bool
 137    for threshold: U32
 138    for fast: Bool
 139    U32
 141  def score_pair(left, right, reverse, threshold, fast):
 142    C.filtered_similarity_mode(left, right, reverse, threshold, fast)
 144  law append_centroid:
 145    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 146    for item: C.Record
 147    List<C.Record>
 149  def append_centroid(centroids, item):
 150    match centroids:
 151      case Nil{}:
 152        Con{item, Nil{}}
 153      case Con{head, tail}:
 154        Con{head, append_centroid(tail, item)}
 156  law find_centroid:
 157    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 158    for query: C.Record
 159    for +threshold: U32
 160    for +reverse: Bool
 161    for +fast: Bool
 162    for +best: U32
 163    for +best_score: U32
 164    U32
 166  def find_centroid(centroids, query, threshold, reverse, fast, best, best_score):
 167    match centroids:
 168      case Nil{}:
 169        best
 170      case Con{head, tail}:
 171        C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, U32,
 172          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
 173            C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, U32,
 174              \{Tuple: q1 => q2 => (
 175                +index = {C.record_index(h1) : U32}
 176                +score = {score_pair(q1, h2, reverse, threshold, fast) : U32}
 177                C.branch(U32, U32.is_ge(score, threshold) && (U32.is_gt(score, best_score) || (U32.is_eq(score, best_score) && U32.is_lt(index, best))), \{
 178                  True: (
 179                    find_centroid(tail, q2, threshold, reverse, fast, index, score)
 180                  )
 181                  False: (
 182                    find_centroid(tail, q2, threshold, reverse, fast, best, best_score)
 183                  )
 184                })
 185              )},
 186              C.record_copy(query))
 187          )},
 188          C.record_copy(head))
 190  law greedy:
 191    for items: List<C.Record>
 192    for centroids: List<C.Record>
 193    for labels: Array<U32>
 194    for +size: U32
 195    for +threshold: U32
 196    for +reverse: Bool
 197    for +fast: Bool
 198    Array<U32>
 200  def greedy(items, centroids, labels, size, threshold, reverse, fast):
 201    match items:
 202      case Nil{}:
 203        labels
 204      case Con{head, tail}:
 205        C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, Array<U32>,
 206          \{Tuple: h1 => h2 => (
 207            C.apply(C.Record & C.Record, Array<U32>,
 208              \{Tuple: q1 => q2 => (
 209                C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, Array<U32>,
 210                  \{Tuple: c1 => c2 => (
 211                    +index = {C.record_index(q1) : U32}
 212                    +center = {find_centroid(c1, q2, threshold, reverse, fast,
 213                      size, 0) : U32}
 214                    C.branch(Array<U32>, U32.is_eq(center, size), \{
 215                      True: (
 216                        greedy(tail, append_centroid(c2, h2), Array.set(U32, labels, index, index),
 217                          size, threshold, reverse, fast)
 218                      )
 219                      False: (
 220                        greedy(tail, c2, Array.set(U32, labels, index, center), size, threshold,
 221                          reverse, fast)
 222                      )
 223                    })
 224                  )},
 225                  C.records_copy(centroids))
 226              )},
 227              C.record_copy(h1))
 228          )},
 229          C.record_copy(head))
 231  law cluster:
 232    for items: List<C.Record>
 233    for +size: U32
 234    for threshold: U32
 235    for +reverse: Bool
 236    for +fast: Bool
 237    for +lz: Bool
 238    for +kmer_minimum: U32
 239    for +kmer_identity: U32
 240    Array<U32>
 242  def cluster(items, size, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 243              kmer_identity):
 244    C.branch(Array<U32>, lz, \{
 245      True: lz_cluster(items, size, threshold, kmer_minimum, kmer_identity)
 246      False: C.branch(Array<U32>, fast && Bool.not(reverse), \{
 247        True: indexed_cluster(items, size, threshold)
 248        False: greedy(C.records_sort(items), Nil{}, C.array_new(size, 0), size,
 249          threshold, reverse, fast)
 250      })
 251    })
 253  law run_dataset:
 254    for dataset: C.Dataset
 255    for +threshold: U32
 256    for reverse: Bool
 257    for fast: Bool
 258    for lz: Bool
 259    for kmer_minimum: U32
 260    for kmer_identity: U32
 261    for resolution: U32
 262    for seed: U32
 263    for passes: Nat
 264    IO(Unit)
 266  def run_dataset(dataset, threshold, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 267                  kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes):
 268    match dataset:
 269      case C.Dataset{items, raw_size}:
 270        +size = {raw_size : U32}
 271        C.apply(List<C.Record> & List<C.Record>, IO(Unit),
 272          \{Tuple: input => output => (
 273            labels = cluster(input, size, threshold, reverse, fast, lz,
 274              kmer_minimum, kmer_identity)
 275            C.output(output, labels)
 276          )},
 277          C.records_copy(items))
 279  law run_config:
 280    for config: C.Config
 281    IO(Unit)
 283  def run_config(config):
 284    match config:
 285      case C.Config{identity, alphabet, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 286                    kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes}:
 287        do IO<Unit>:
 288          dataset: C.Dataset <- C.fasta_read(alphabet)
 289          run_dataset(dataset, identity, reverse, fast, lz, kmer_minimum,
 290            kmer_identity, resolution, seed, passes)
 292  law main:
 293    IO(Unit)
 295  def main():
 296    do IO<Unit>:
 297      config: C.Config <- C.config_read()
 298      run_config(config)
